We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [207] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST-Bench基准，用于系统评估AI智能体是否真正从保留经验中实现递归自我改进，并基于发现开发了Hermes+框架以增强改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI智能体能够在会话间保留偏好、任务历史、工具例程和技能，但"保留的经验是否真的能改善其未来行为"这一问题尚未被系统性测试过，缺乏专门的评估基准来隔离此问题。

Method: 构建PAST-Bench基准，包含26个场景和204个情节，让智能体在匹配条件下运行有序的新会话任务序列，通过开关保留经验来对比改进效果。基准涵盖记忆、程序性复用、信息收集和更新四类能力，并检验改进是否遵循"保存-检索-更新"的预期路径。基于分析结果，开发Hermes+框架，在智能体循环的各阶段加入五项针对性干预。

Result: 跨7个基础模型和4个智能体框架的测试显示：改进是真实存在的但不均匀；具有相同标题增益的智能体在预期路径证据支持上可能差异显著。Hermes+提高了平均增益并提供更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上改进最强，但效果仍依赖能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+共同为研究持久智能体如何从保留经验进步到系统性改进提供了评估与诊断基础，有助于推动递归自我改进能力的研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [208] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TurnSight框架，通过执行条件的事后视角（hindsight）在回合级别为工具集成推理（TIR）提供密集监督信号，利用跨时间跨度方向一致性选择可靠监督，并在保持优化方向的前提下自适应调制RL优势，在三个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR强化学习方法依赖轨迹级监督，缺乏长程TIR场景中的细粒度信用分配；已有的在线自蒸馏方法虽然提供更密集信号，但通常从ground-truth答案或检索技能中获取上下文，无法反映智能体实际访问的状态；此外，token级监督无法捕捉工具交互的回合级结构。

Method: TurnSight包含三个核心组件：(1) 从执行条件的事后视角直接推导回合级监督信号；(2) 构建多个不同前瞻视野的事后视角，通过跨视野方向一致性筛选可靠监督；(3) 将选定的事后信号在兄弟rollout间归一化，用于自适应调制RL优势，同时保留原始优化方向。

Result: 在三个基准测试上的大量实验验证了TurnSight框架的有效性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过回合级事后自蒸馏解决了TIR中细粒度信用分配和离线上下文不匹配的问题，为长程工具交互场景提供了更可靠的密集监督信号。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [209] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个前瞻性的基准测试：在2026年FIFA世界杯的39天赛程中，六个前沿大语言模型在每场比赛前被要求预测7个市场共104场比赛、12个小组出线者和赛前总冠军，共收集4,494个可打分的预测。由于问题提出时答案尚不存在，评估从构造上避免了数据泄漏。结果显示模型平均胜率为63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门相当；模型间高度一致但正确率不高；对平局和进球数预测不足；预测质量取决于比赛实力差距而非信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM预测基准大多是回顾性的，事件已发生、答案已在网络上，评估必须防范记忆泄露。本文设计了一种前所未有的前瞻性评测方法，在任何答案存在之前提出问题，从而从结构上杜绝泄漏。

Method: 在2026年FIFA世界杯期间，对六个具备扩展思维和原生服务端网络搜索的前沿LLM，在每场比赛开球前逐一要求填写包含7个市场的预测卡片，覆盖全部104场比赛、12个小组出线者和赛前冠军池。预测和实际结果被冻结存档，共获得4,494个可评分的预测。

Result: 模型在比赛结果上平均达到63.9%的准确率，与押注博彩公司热门持平（它们通常也确实这样做）。模型之间的相互一致性远高于正确率，多数投票没有增益。模型对平局和进球数预测不足，比分选择集中在单一原型结果上。预测准确度与比赛的实力悬殊程度相关而非信息丰富度，在最接近的比赛中表现崩溃；小组整体的预测则表现良好。排名在顶部和底部保持稳定，中间层波动，差距始终狭窄。

Conclusion: 当前一代前沿LLM在此任务上差异化不明显，表现共享了一系列系统性行为模式。作者将比赛简报、赛程和官方结果连同评分代码作为基准公开发布。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [210] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SocietyBench，一个端到端的社会事件预测基准，通过将真实新闻和社交媒体数据转化为反事实的匿名化时间线，评估大语言模型对社会事件走向的预测能力，包括概率校准和时间准确性两个维度。最强的模型仅得75/100分，说明预测社会事件动态仍然是LLM的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准大量关注任务完成能力（如修bug、操作浏览器），但缺乏对模型理解与预测真实社会事件动态这一互补社会能力的系统性衡量。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: SocietyBench包含五个流程：（1）从五个平台收集新闻和社交媒体数据；（2）蒸馏为日期索引时间线，区分事实事件与公众舆论层；（3）每个截止日期转化为经过审计的预测问题库；（4）通过三阶段流程对命名实体匿名化并对日期施以每事件常数偏移，构建反事实社会世界以消除预训练记忆影响；（5）在概率校准和时间准确性两个正交轴上评分（各100分）。

Result: 在5个异构事件和125个预测点上，六个前沿LLM中最强者仅达75/100分（基线为50）。两个评分轴可以分离：模型可能在校准上强但在时间上弱，或反之。三个基于同一基础模型的Agent框架未能超越基础模型，两个无模型启发式方法落后于所有LLM。单个轴上的事件间差距可达21.4分。

Conclusion: 当前最强LLM在预测社会事件演变方面表现有限，且Agent框架无助于提升该能力。多事件评估至关重要，因为单一事件上的表现可能具有误导性。作者已发布全部匿名化时间线、问题库、真实值和评分代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [An Empirical Analysis of the Glob Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02610)
*Phyllis Lim,Connor Adkins,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对glob模式（一种结构化字符串匹配领域特定语言）生态系统进行了实证研究，分析了1,966个开源项目、1,355个GitHub issue、444份CVE报告和361个StackOverflow帖子，跨六个生态系统系统地绘制了glob的功能支持和实际使用情况，揭示了实现对间的不一致性和安全漏洞问题，并提出了名为GlobSpec的形式化规范以解决语义歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管glob模式在现代软件开发中广泛应用（从构建脚本、配置文件到Web服务器路由），但其缺乏标准化导致生态系统碎片化，存在行为不一致、安全漏洞和可用性陷阱等问题。目前缺乏对这一基础性组件的系统性实证研究来量化这些挑战。

Method: 通过对1,966个开源项目、1,355个GitHub issues、444份CVE报告和361个StackOverflow帖子的多源数据分析，跨六个常用生态系统系统地比较了glob实现的功能支持和实际使用情况，并据此设计了形式化规范GlobSpec以解决语义歧义和表达能力差距。

Result: 研究发现各glob实现与开发者采用之间存在明显鸿沟；记录了阻碍可移植性和可靠性的不一致性；安全漏洞并非边缘案例而是主导性问题，占所有开发者讨论的近四分之一。

Conclusion: 需要通过标准化来解决glob生态系统的碎片化问题；作者提出的GlobSpec形式化规范旨在消除语义歧义，弥合当前实现与开发者需求之间的表达能力差距。

Abstract: Glob patterns, a domain-specific language for structured string matching, are a foundational yet understudied component of modern software development embedded in everything from build scripts and configuration files to web server routes. However, this widespread use rests on a fragile foundation. Despite their ubiquity across tools and programming languages, a lack of standardization has led to a fragmented ecosystem rife with inconsistent behaviors, security vulnerabilities, and usability pitfalls. This paper presents the first empirical study of the glob ecosystem to quantify these challenges and chart a path toward robust solutions.
  Through an analysis of 1,966 open source projects, 1,355 Github issues, 444 CVE reports, and 361 StackOverflow posts, we systematically map the feature support and real-world usage of globs across six common ecosystems that utilize globs. Our findings reveal a stark divide between various globbing implementations and developer adoption. In addition, we document inconsistencies that hinder portability, reliability, and create security flaws. Our analysis reveals that security vulnerabilities are not corner cases, but a dominant concern, comprising almost a quarter of all developer discussions. We propose a path forward through standardization and introduce a formal specification for globs, GlobSpec, designed to resolve semantic ambiguities and bridge the expressiveness gap between current implementations and developer requirements.

</details>


### [212] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agent Skills: Feedback Dynamics over Multiple Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02636)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Zhongwei Xie,Huihao Jing,Lingyun Xie,Qing Zong,Yauwai Yim,Zhixiong Zhang,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控评估框架，在5个基准和3个模型上系统研究了自我进化技能系统的稀疏性、反馈依赖性和测试时计算替代效果。关键发现：进化是稀疏的（388个候选中仅55个产生字节级验证最优），失败轨迹对选择进化技能至关重要，且测试时计算无法稳定替代持久性技能进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自我进化技能系统承诺通过执行反馈转化为持久性技能更新来提升智能体性能，但进化何时有效、成功与失败轨迹如何影响技能修订、以及额外的测试时计算能否获得相同收益等问题尚不明确。

Method: 构建受控评估框架，覆盖5个基准和3个模型，包含42次反馈运行和14个模型-基准设置。在同一设置中固定执行器、优化器配置、修订流程、验证规则和轮次预算，仅改变反馈类型（成功+失败/仅失败/仅成功）。此外在GPT-5.5上开展测试时扩展控制实验（Parallel Sampling和Sequential Refinement）进行对比。

Result: 388个候选中仅55个建立字节级验证最优（稀疏进化）；验证选择在14个设置中有11个选定进化技能，其中9个提升测试性能；所有11个选定均来自包含失败轨迹的反馈条件；验证与下游评测有时偏好不同反馈视角；测试时扩展中Parallel Sampling在SearchQA上接近进化技能（差距0.43分），但在SpreadsheetBench上落后30.96分，Sequential Refinement无法恢复任何收益。

Conclusion: 持久性技能自我进化应被视为稀疏的、经验证过滤的搜索过程，其收益依赖于模型和基准，而非通过额外轮次带来的持续改进。测试时计算无法一致替代持久性进化的收益。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill systems promise to improve agents by turning execution feedback into persistent skill updates without changing the underlying model. Yet it remains unclear when further evolution helps, how successful and failed trajectories shape revision, and whether extra test-time computation can recover the same gains. To address these questions, we present a controlled evaluation framework across five benchmarks and three models. Our primary study contains 42 feedback runs across 14 supported model-benchmark settings. Within each setting, we hold the executor and optimizer configuration, revision procedure, validation rule, and round budget fixed, while varying only the feedback shown to the optimizer: successes and failures (Normal), failures only, or successes only. Evolution is sparse: only 55 of 388 candidates establish byte-distinct validation bests. Validation-based selection chooses an evolved skill in 11 of 14 settings, nine of which improve released-test performance. All 11 selections come from feedback conditions that include failed trajectories, although the relative ranking of Normal and Fail-only varies across settings. Validation and downstream evaluations on test, robustness, and transfer sometimes favor different feedback views. A broader SearchQA analysis covering eight models shows similarly sparse, feedback-dependent dynamics. In the GPT-5.5 test-time-scaling controls, oracle Parallel Sampling comes within 0.43 points of the evolved SearchQA skill but remains 30.96 points behind on SpreadsheetBench; Sequential Refinement recovers neither gain. Overall, persistent skill self-evolution is better understood as sparse, validation-filtered search with model- and benchmark-dependent returns, rather than steady improvement from additional rounds. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/rethinkskill.

</details>


### [213] [When Policies Change Probabilities: Modular Decision-Making for LLM Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02677)
*Rasvik Kudum,Max Corbett,Hitansh Paliwal,Romaisa Fatima,Thomas Jiralerspong,Sneheel Sarangi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过15,792次响应、720个候选补丁的实验，发现LLM代码审查员在同时估计补丁风险并做审批决策时，将成本政策（如10:1误接受政策）混入概率估计中，导致报告的错误概率偏离真实值13.6到16.9个百分点。提出将风险估计、外部证据和决策行动分离的模块化流水线，该流水线在相等成本下降低平均损失0.073，在10:1成本下则不接受任何补丁并匹配全拒绝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代码审查系统将风险概率估计与审批决策合并到单一提示中，这违反了概率应依赖证据、成本应决定行动的分离原则。论文旨在验证这一原则是否在实际部署的审查界面中被遵守，并探索如何改进。

Method: 使用15,792次API响应，在360个仓库问题上各取一个通过和未通过测试的补丁（共720个），对四个已部署的审查界面进行匹配调用。在固定补丁和监控证据的条件下，改变成本政策（等成本vs 10:1误接受），比较报告概率和行动的差异。同时构建了一个模块化流水线：独立估计风险（不含政策信息）、整合独立监控分数、在代码层面应用成本决策。

Result: 四个审查界面在10:1高成本提示下，报告失败概率平均偏移13.6至16.9个百分点，且返回的行动比全拒绝更差。将高成本规则应用于等成本下获得的概率可减少损失，表明概率估计本身受到政策污染。模块化流水线相比校准的审查员独立评分，提高了概率准确度，在等成本下平均损失降低0.073/问题，接受58-68%补丁；在10:1成本下不接受任何补丁并匹配全拒绝。

Conclusion: 下游政策会污染其本应使用的概率估计，这论证了将风险估计、外部证据和行动决策分开评估的必要性。模块化流水线通过分离关注点实现了更好的概率准确性和决策损失控制。

Abstract: LLM code reviewers often estimate patch risk and make approval decisions in one prompt. A probability should depend on evidence; costs should determine the action taken from it. We test whether four deployed reviewer interfaces preserve this separation using 15,792 responses on 720 candidate patches, with one that passed and one that failed an archived test harness for each of 360 repository issues. In matched calls with the patch and monitor evidence fixed, replacing an equal-cost policy with a 10:1 false-accept policy changes reported failure probabilities by 13.6 to 16.9 percentage points on average. For every reviewer, the actions returned under the high-cost prompt are worse than rejecting all patches. Applying the same high-cost rule to probabilities elicited under equal costs reduces loss for all four systems, showing that probability elicitation itself contributes to the excess loss. We also evaluate a modular pipeline that elicits risk without policy information, combines an independent monitor score, and applies costs in code. Relative to calibrated reviewer-only scores, the pipeline improves average probability accuracy and, at equal costs, reduces mean loss by .073 per issue while accepting 58 to 68% of patches. At 10:1, it accepts none and matches reject-all. Downstream policy can therefore change the probability it is meant to use, motivating separate evaluation of risk, outside evidence, and action.

</details>


### [214] [Studying, Identifying, and Fixing Hidden Technical Debt in AI-Intensive Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02638)
*Beena*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文旨在表征AI网络物理系统（AI-CPS）中的技术债务（TD），提出识别和修复方法，并开发自动化工具支持代理型AI解决方案来监控、治理和偿还AI-CPS技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI组件越来越多地嵌入网络物理系统（CPS）中，形成了AI-CPS。这类系统由硬件、AI组件和常规模块组成，其技术债务比传统系统更特殊且更具挑战性，但目前缺乏系统性的表征、识别和修复方法。

Method: 研究分三个阶段：(1) 通过分析AI生态系统和AI-CPS代码仓库、访谈开发者来表征AI-CPS技术债务；(2) 基于获取的知识定义识别和缓解技术债务的方法；(3) 开发并验证一个支持代理型AI解决方案的自动化工具，用于监控、治理和偿还AI-CPS技术债务。

Result: 目前论文仍处于研究进行中状态：已完成第一阶段（表征AI-CPS技术债务）的初步工作，后续阶段的识别/缓解方法定义和自动化工具开发与验证仍在规划中。

Conclusion: 该论文系统性地研究了AI-CPS中独特的技术债务问题，通过定性和定量方法相结合进行表征，并提出面向自动化识别和治理的技术债务解决方案，最终目标是提供工具支持AI-CPS的长期维护和演进。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly pervasive in several software systems, including Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). AI-CPS are used in several domains, including autonomous vehicles, industry, home automation, robotics, and healthcare. Being composed of hardware, AI components, and conventional modules, AI-CPS can exhibit technical debt (TD) that is peculiar and potentially more challenging than that of conventional systems. This thesis aims to characterize AI-CPS TD and propose approaches for its identification and repair. In a first phase, we characterize AI-CPS TD by analyzing AI ecosystems and AI-CPS repositories, as well as interviewing developers. Based on the acquired knowledge, we define approaches to identify and mitigate such TD. Finally, we plan to develop and validate an automated tool that supports agentic AI solutions to monitor, govern, and repay AI-CPS TD.

</details>


### [215] [Instruction Stacking Collapse: A Benchmark and the Capability-Dependent Value of Prompt Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02639)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多约束指令堆叠下大语言模型指令遵循能力的退化问题，提出一个包含24条可验证指令的基准测试，并评估了一种无需训练的重写（指令编译器）方法，发现其对较弱模型的提升显著（最高+11分），对较强模型影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实生产环境中的提示词很少只有单条指令，一条系统消息可能同时要求JSON格式、字数限制、引用规范等多种约束，但现有研究对多约束累积下指令遵循退化问题的理解有限。作者希望通过系统化基准测试揭示退化规律，并探索无需训练的缓解方案。

Method: 构建了包含24条可验证（verifier-checked）指令的基准测试，每次堆叠1至20条指令进行评估，覆盖三个生产级LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash）。提出一个训练免费的"指令编译器"，单次LLM调用重写堆叠提示词并在查询间复用。使用簇稳健检验、同基线对照和家族内规模阶梯等分析手段隔离增益来源。

Result: 指令遵循率随约束数量呈非线性下降，从约96%降至最低20%，且存在结构化、可复现的成对冲突（例如"输出JSON"约束与其他九条约束联合不可满足）。指令编译器对较弱模型可恢复最高+11个百分点的遵循率，但对较强模型几乎无影响。通过对照实验确认增益来自重写本身，而非额外token、指令重排或测量空间。

Conclusion: 多约束指令堆叠会导致LLM指令遵循能力严重且非线性的退化，成对冲突是关键驱动因素。训练免费的提示词重写方法对较弱（但大规模部署的）模型有明显收益，而对强模型无显著影响，体现了修复效果的能力分级特性。研究发布了完整基准、验证器和缓存运行结果以供复现。

Abstract: Production prompts rarely carry a single instruction. One system message may require valid JSON, a word limit, three citations, and a fixed tone at the same time. We study how instruction-following degrades as such constraints accumulate. We introduce a benchmark that stacks 24 verifier-checked instructions, one to twenty at a time, and evaluate three production-tier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Instruction-following degrades non-linearly: the follow rate falls from ~96% to as low as 20%, driven by a structured and reproducible set of pairwise conflicts. A single "output JSON" constraint, for example, is jointly unsatisfiable with nine others. We then evaluate a training-free remedy: an instruction compiler that rewrites the stacked prompt in a single LLM call and is reused across queries. Its benefit is capability-graded. It recovers up to +11 points of follow rate for weaker models, which are also the models most often deployed at scale, while leaving stronger models, which already internalise the same structure, essentially unchanged. Cluster-robust tests, same-baseline controls, and a within-family scaling ladder attribute the gain to the rewrite itself rather than to additional tokens, reordering, or measurement headroom. We release the benchmark, verifiers, and cached runs for full reproduction.

</details>


### [216] [IR2Solve: Structured Intermediate Representations for Cost-Efficient Optimization Autoformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02641)
*Penglin Zhu,Linhai Zhang,Jungang Xu,Xinchi Wei,Xiuqi Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出IR2Solve，一种中间表示优先的自动公式化流水线，通过一次语义LLM调用生成模式约束的ModelIR，再经过确定性验证和IR到求解器编译两个阶段，在保持高目标正确性的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型翻译自然语言优化问题为求解器可用格式时，直接代码生成容易出错（模式、索引、语义错误），而迭代修复、搜索和多智能体工作流虽然能改善但显著增加推理成本。因此需要一种兼顾准确性和成本的方法。

Method: IR2Solve采用中间表示优先的架构：首先使用一次语义LLM调用生成模式约束的ModelIR（显式表示集合、参数、变量、目标和约束，使用受限Python风格表达式字符串）；然后经过两个确定性阶段——验证和IR到求解器编译。其中标量约束约定将有限的逐索引约束族表示为独立条目，减少自由索引和隐式量化错误。

Result: 在六个清理过的优化基准上IR2Solve实现了强劲的目标正确性，与最近的优化建模系统竞争力相当。在153个IndustryOR和ComplexLP实例的受控消融实验中显示出从结构化IR接口、标量约束指令和确定性验证带来的逐步收益。在匹配的十实例成本对比中，IR2Solve每个实例仅需一次语义调用，而Chain-of-Experts和SAC-Opt分别需要8次和39次调用，token消耗分别是IR2Solve的3.3倍和22.9倍。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示结合确定性后处理，为基于LLM的优化自动公式化提供了一种实用的准确性与成本权衡方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language optimization problems into solver-ready formulations, but direct code generation is brittle: schema, indexing, and semantic errors can cause compilation failures, infeasible models, or incorrect objectives, while iterative repair, search, and multi-agent workflows increase inference cost. We present IR2Solve, an intermediate-representation-first autoformulation pipeline that uses a single semantic LLM call to produce a schema-constrained ModelIR, followed by two deterministic stages: verification and IR-to-solver compilation. ModelIR explicitly represents sets, parameters, variables, objectives, and constraints using restricted Python-like expression strings. A concrete scalar-constraint convention represents finite per-index constraint families as individual entries, reducing free-index and implicit-quantification errors while simplifying downstream verification and compilation. Across six cleaned optimization benchmarks, IR2Solve achieves strong objective correctness and remains competitive with recent optimization-modeling systems. A controlled ablation on 153 IndustryOR and ComplexLP instances shows sequential gains from the structured IR interface, the scalar-constraint instruction, and deterministic verification. On a matched ten-instance cost panel, IR2Solve uses one semantic call per instance, whereas Chain-of-Experts and SAC-Opt use 8 and 39 calls per instance and consume 3.3 and 22.9 times the token volume of IR2Solve, respectively. These results show that structured intermediate representations, combined with deterministic post-generation processing, provide a practical accuracy-cost trade-off for LLM-based optimization autoformulation.

</details>


### [217] [CUADebug: Diagnosing and Repairing Computer-Use Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02643)
*Weijia Zhang,Kunlun Zhu,Zeyi Liu,Yinting Chen,Tianyi Ma,Jiateng Liu,Jiaxun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Xiangru Tang,Heng Ji,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CUADebug框架，用于诊断和修复计算机使用智能体（CUA）的失败问题。该系统包含CUA专属错误分类法、人工标注的OSWorld失败基准（CUAErrorBench）以及工具增强的调试器（CUADebugger）。实验表明，该方法能将故障诊断准确率从11.2%提升至19.6%，并在单次及持续重执行场景中显著提高任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体通过截图、鼠标键盘操作和界面反馈来操作真实桌面和网页界面，但其失败难以诊断和修复。与纯文本智能体不同，CUA的失败源于视觉感知、空间定位、底层交互、任务推理和环境动态的耦合，使得调试成为一个独特的多模态因果定位问题。

Method: CUADebug包含三个核心组件：1）CUA专属错误分类法，将失败归类为感知、定位/交互、任务推理与控制、外部/系统等类别；2）CUAErrorBench，包含204条人工标注的OSWorld失败轨迹基准；3）CUADebugger，通过配对的失败前后截图和动作轨迹主动检查可疑步骤，输出包含根因步骤、错误类型、证据和纠正策略的结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在204条失败轨迹中，任务推理与控制是最大失败类别（110/204），其次为感知（36）、定位/交互（25）、外部/系统（13）和其他（20）。在Claude-agent主分割上，CUADebugger将联合子类型与步骤诊断从11.2%提升至19.6%。单次重执行中，RCA方法任务完成率（28.47%-29.90%）显著优于仅历史延续（13.89%）；持续重执行中成功率从12.2%提升至25.86%。

Conclusion: CUA的根因诊断能够提供可操作的修复信号，而不仅仅是事后解释。CUADebug证明了结构化诊断工具在提升CUA任务执行成功率方面的有效性，为多模态智能体调试提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate real desktop and web interfaces through screenshots, mouse and keyboard actions, and stateful UI feedback, yet their failures remain difficult to diagnose and repair. Unlike text-only agents, CUA failures arise from coupled visual perception, spatial grounding, low-level interaction, task reasoning, and environment dynamics, making debugging a distinctive multimodal causal localization problem. We introduce CUADebug, a framework for diagnosing and repairing CUA failures. CUADebug includes a CUA-specific error taxonomy, CUAErrorBench, a human-annotated OSWorld failure benchmark, and CUADebugger, a tool-augmented debugger. Instead of prompting over the full trajectory once, CUADebugger actively inspects suspicious steps with paired before/after screenshots and action traces, then submits a structured diagnosis containing the root-cause step, error type, grounded evidence, and corrective strategy for re-execution. Human annotations over 204 failed trajectories show that task reasoning and control is the largest failure family (110/204), followed by perception (36), grounding/interaction (25), external/system (13), and an others category of 20 OSWorld infeasible-task cases. On the main Claude-agent split, CUADebugger improves joint subtype-and-step diagnosis from 11.2% to 19.6% with Gemini 2.5 Pro and improves consistently across debugger backbones. In single re-execution package evaluation, RCA-based conditions achieve higher task completion than history-only continuation (28.47% with machine RCA and 29.90% with our method, versus 13.89%); in continual re-execution, our method improves success from 12.2% to 25.86%, while human-oracle guidance reaches 29.21%. These results show that CUA root-cause diagnosis can provide actionable repair signals rather than merely post-hoc explanations.

</details>


### [218] [A Unified Feature Model for Microservice Identification and Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02644)
*Ana Almeida,António Rito Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于微服务识别工具的特征模型，基于对现有研究的大量分析，旨在统一不同方法论之间的比较与设计。评估发现该模型能捕捉现有方法的主要变化点，但现有工具各自只覆盖模型的一小部分，彼此互补却缺乏集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务自动识别方法众多，但在数据收集、分解算法、可视化等方面缺乏统一的比较框架，且不存在单一最优方法，需要整合多种方法，因此需要建立一个共同的概念框架来解决系统性实验和比较的缺失问题。

Method: 通过广泛分析现有研究提出一个面向变体丰富的微服务识别工具的特征模型；通过系统性文献映射分析代表性文献，并在现有微服务识别工具架构中实例化该模型以进行评估。

Result: 特征模型成功捕捉了现有方法论的主要变化点。虽然没有任何单一工具覆盖模型的很大比例，但分析的所有工具联合起来几乎覆盖了所有变化点，表明设计空间已被填充但分散在独立工具中。

Conclusion: 本文的核心发现是互补性：微服务识别工具的设计空间已经存在但碎片化，各工具无法相互比较或集成其他工具实现的替代方案。特征模型为分析、比较和设计此类工具提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Several approaches have been proposed for the automatic identification of microservices within monolithic systems. These methodologies differ in their data collection and analysis techniques, the decomposition algorithms applied, and the mechanisms used to visualize and refine candidate microservices. Despite this diversity, systematic experimentation and comparison across approaches remain limited by the absence of a common conceptual framework. Furthermore, current research indicates that no single optimal method exists; rather, integrating multiple approaches is necessary to fully explore the trade-offs inherent in any design solution.
  To address this gap, this paper proposes a feature model for variant-rich microservice identification tools, grounded in an extensive analysis of the state of the art. We evaluate this feature model through a systematic mapping of representative literature and by analyzing its instantiation within the architecture of an existing microservice identification tool. Our findings are two-fold. First, the proposed feature model successfully captures the primary variation points of existing methodologies, providing a unifying foundation for analyzing, comparing, and designing microservice identification tools. Second, while no individual tool covers more than a fraction of the model, the analyzed tools jointly span almost all of its variation points. This complementarity is the central result: the design space is already populated, but it is fragmented across standalone tools, none of which can compare or integrate the alternatives that the others implement.

</details>


### [219] [Verified Tool Calls Improve LLM Agent Reliability Under Non-Atomic Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02645)
*Isham Kalappurackal Mansoor,Abhishek Phadke,Pratip Rana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了面向LLM智能体的一种验证感知的工具包装器，通过后置条件验证、重试前验证和幂等键来处理现实系统中工具调用的非原子性行为（如超时、延迟可见性、部分状态更新），从而减少重复操作并维持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体框架假设工具调用是原子性的且返回二元的成功/失败信号，但现实系统存在超时、延迟可见性、部分状态更新等非原子行为，导致重复操作、任务成功误判和不必要的工具执行等问题。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的验证感知工具包装器，包含三个核心组件：后置条件验证、验证-再重试逻辑以及幂等键机制，在受控模拟环境中注入非原子故障进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著减少了重复操作，同时保持了可比较的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 强化工具交互语义是提升LLM智能体可靠性的有前景的方向，且无需修改底层语言模型。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external tools to perform multistage tasks. Existing agent frameworks typically assume that tool calls are atomic and return binary success or failure signals. However, real-world systems exhibit non-atomic behaviors such as timeouts after dispatch, delayed visibility, and partial state updates. These mismatches lead to reliability issues including duplicate actions, task success, and unnecessary tool executions. A lightweight, verification-aware tool wrapper is introduced that augments tool calls with postcondition verification, verify-before-retry logic, and idempotency keys. The approach is evaluated in a controlled simulated environment with injected non-atomic failures across multiple task templates. The results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces duplicate actions, while maintaining comparable task success rates. Overall, the findings suggest that strengthening tool interaction semantics is a promising direction for improving LLM agent reliability without requiring modifications to the underlying language model.

</details>


### [220] [Intent-Level Quantum Programming with Assertion-Guided Execution and Inspectable Intermediate Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02648)
*Ilesh Vora,Srikanth Thudumu,John Carlson,Harshil Kamdar,Rajesh Vasa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子领域特定语言（QDSL），通过意图-执行分离、一级IR自省和断言引导的模态推理三种机制，解决量子程序验证和跨后端可移植性的挑战，并在基准电路上验证了故障检测、差分测试和IR生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序难以验证，因为电路通常被表示为命令式门序列且可检查性有限，执行模态需要手动选择，输出本质上具有概率性。此外，不同后端框架的内部约定差异使得程序可移植性面临额外挑战。

Method: 论文提出三种核心机制：(i) 意图-执行分离，将算法构造（制备、叠加、纠缠、测量）表示为可检查对象，在执行前IR验证后编译为后端特定电路；(ii) 一级IR自省，在执行前暴露电路宽度、线映射、操作顺序和测量意图供开发者检查；(iii) 断言引导的模态推理，引擎根据声明的验证属性自动选择采样、态向量或双执行模态。结果以结构化形式记录以支持可复现的回归测试。

Result: 在纠缠、基于oracle、结构化变换和变分等基准电路上评估原型。故障注入实验中，总变差距离检测器在所有评估的shot预算下识别注入故障（真阳性率1.0），仅在128 shots时观察到假阳性（假阳性率2.7-3.8%）。差分测试表明在字节序规范化后，PennyLane和Qiskit编译目标在数值上一致。IR生成成本低于毫秒级。

Conclusion: QDSL通过意图-执行分离、IR自省和自动模态推理有效解决了量子程序验证与可移植性问题，实验结果证明了其在故障检测、跨后端数值一致性和IR生成效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum programs are difficult to validate: circuits are typically expressed as imperative gate sequences with limited inspectability, execution modalities must be selected manually, and outputs are inherently probabilistic. These challenges are compounded when programs must be portable across backend frameworks with differing internal conventions. We present a quantum domain-specific language (QDSL) that addresses these problems through three mechanisms: (i) intent-execution separation, where algorithmic constructs such as preparation, superposition, entanglement, and measurement are represented as inspectable objects compiled into backend-specific circuits only after pre-execution Intermediate Representation (IR) validation; (ii) first-class IR introspection, exposing circuit width, wire mapping, operation order, and measurement intent for developer inspection prior to execution; and (iii) assertion-guided modality inference, where the engine examines declared verification properties to automatically select sampling, statevector, or dual execution without user intervention. Results are logged in structured form to support reproducible regression testing. We evaluate our prototype on benchmark circuits spanning entanglement, oracle-based, structured-transform, and variational examples. In fault-injection experiments on Bell and 3-qubit GHZ circuits, a total-variation-distance detector identifies the injected faults across all evaluated shot budgets (True Positive Rate 1.0), with observed false positives (False Positive Rate 2.7-3.8%) only at 128 shots. Differential testing demonstrates numerical agreement across PennyLane and Qiskit compilation targets for the evaluated constructs after endianness canonicalization. IR generation costs are sub-millisecond.

</details>


### [221] [AI Sandbox: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02679)
*Muhammad Waseem,Md Aidul Islam,Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Mahade Hasan,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Jussi Rasku,Mika Saari,Vilma Saari,Roope Pajasmaa,Markku Oivo,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在产学研合作背景下设计和实现的一个具备治理意识的多租户AI沙箱平台。该平台将多租户用户界面与后端控制平面分离，并设有专门的执行和数据管理层，支持受控用户接入、项目中心协作、AI服务管理访问、审批流程、审计日志和可追溯实验，同时将实验配置与治理决策作为持久记录存储，以便证据复用和跨项目比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨行业与学术界的协作AI实验需要既能快速原型开发又能保持受控访问、租户隔离和透明工作流的平台。尽管对AI沙箱的兴趣日益增长，但关于如何设计和实现集实验能力与治理要求于一体的平台，仍然缺乏实用指导。

Method: 在产学研合作中基于与工业伙伴迭代细化的需求进行开发。参考架构将多租户用户界面与后端控制平面分离，并将执行和数据管理功能置于专门层级。平台提供治理化用户接入、项目中心协作、AI服务管理访问、审批工作流、审计日志和可追溯实验。实验配置、上下文信息和治理决策作为持久记录存储，支持跨项目比较和证据复用。

Result: 成功设计并实现了一个治理感知的多租户AI沙箱平台，支持结构化实验和可重用评估证据的生成，并在产学研协作环境中完成了部署与验证。

Conclusion: 开发过程为在协作研究和工业环境中部署和扩展治理感知的AI沙箱平台提供了实践经验与教训。

Abstract: Collaborative AI experimentation across industry and academia requires platforms that enable rapid prototyping while preserving controlled access, tenant separation, and transparent workflows. Despite growing interest in AI sandboxes, there is still limited practical guidance on how to design and implement platforms that integrate experimentation capabilities with governance requirements. This work presents the design and implementation of a governance-aware, multi-tenant AI sandbox for structured experimentation and the generation of reusable evaluation evidence across projects and stakeholder groups.
  The sandbox was developed within an industry-academia collaboration based on requirements that were iteratively refined with industrial partners. Its reference architecture separates the multi-tenant user interface from the backend control plane and places execution and data-management functions in dedicated layers. The platform supports governed user onboarding, project-centered collaboration, managed access to AI services, approval workflows, audit logging, and traceable experimentation. Experiment configurations, contextual information, and governance decisions are stored as persistent records, allowing evidence and outcomes to be compared and reused across projects.
  The development process provides practical lessons for deploying and extending governance-aware AI sandbox platforms in collaborative research and industrial environments.

</details>


### [222] [TraceCompiler: Skill-Guided Mining and Compilation of LLM Agent Traces into Mostly Deterministic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02680)
*Salma El Yadouni,Guanyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceCompiler，一个技能引导的系统，从嘈杂的代理轨迹中挖掘聚类并编译成可执行、基本确定性的工作流。通过精细的依赖关系分类和证据元组审计，在多个基准上实现高精确度/召回率的依赖恢复，并展示了编译工作流的实际应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在反复执行任务时会重复执行已运行过的过程，产生混合了可复用结构和重试、探索、偶然排序、重复查找等噪音的轨迹。如何从这些嘈杂轨迹中提取出可复用的确定性工作流是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: TraceCompiler采用工具间依赖规则：仅当消费方参数包含可唯一归因于早期生产方的值时，才允许工具间依赖；每条硬边携带可审计证据元组，模糊关系标记为"suspected"且不施加排序约束。绑定被分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、变换或残余LLM决策五类。

Result: 在T1基准上，依赖恢复达到0.928精确度和0.943召回率（15,775条def-use边），显著优于adjacency（0.711 F1）和frequency-thresholded直接跟随方法（0.712 F1）；编译器技能盲测达到0.992。在AppWorld上达到0.993精确度。Venmo支付意图将34次API调用减少至11次运行时调用，15/21状态测试通过。Spotify/Todoit意图因不可逆副作用不确定而被正确拒绝编译。

Conclusion: TraceCompiler展示了通过证据驱动的依赖挖掘和绑定分类，可以从噪声轨迹中有效编译出可执行的确定性工作流，并能识别何时不应编译（存在不可逆副作用时）。但作者承认未测量离线编译成本，因此不主张净效率收益。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents repeatedly rediscover procedures they have already executed, producing traces that mix reusable structure with retries, exploration, accidental ordering, and repeated lookups. We present TraceCompiler, a skill-guided system that mines clusters of noisy agent traces and compiles them into executable, mostly deterministic workflows. It admits an inter-tool dependency only when a consumer argument contains a value attributable uniquely to an earlier producer; every hard edge carries an auditable evidence tuple, and ambiguous relations are marked suspected and impose no ordering constraint. Bindings are classified as constants, user inputs, copied outputs, transforms, or residual LLM decisions. On T1, a mechanized form of the rule recovers producer-consumer dependencies at 0.928 precision and 0.943 recall over 15,775 def-use edges of its training split, against 0.711 F1 for adjacency and 0.712 for a frequency-thresholded directly-follows measure on identical data; the compiler skill run blind reaches 0.992 on 250 of those edges. On AppWorld we replay released trajectories in the deterministic simulator to recover masked return values and measure the rule against 563 token edges at 0.993 precision - a self-consistency check, since replay injects tokens by a related heuristic. We compile two recurring intents: a Venmo money-request intent reduces 34 observed API calls to 11 runtime calls and, under leave-one-out execution against the benchmark's own state tests, passes 15 of 21, the failing fold escalating rather than acting because its required branch was never observed; and a Spotify/Todoist intent the compiler correctly refuses to compile, because an irreversible side effect is under-determined. We measure call reduction but not offline compilation cost, so we claim no net efficiency result.

</details>


### [223] [Studying Developer Perceptions on the Potential of CI Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02682)
*Osamah H. Alaini,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性调查研究，旨在理解GitHub开发者如何选择CI服务以及其采用决策是出于真实项目需求还是社会影响。研究将联系约5000名活跃GitHub开发者（包括CI用户和非用户），考察CI采用驱动力、服务选择动机、CI的普适性与情境依赖性、采用障碍以及对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已识别出CI采用的障碍，但缺乏关于开发者如何选择CI服务以及采用决策究竟是由真实项目需求还是社会影响驱动的实证证据。

Method: 采用探索性调查研究方法，计划联系约5000名活跃GitHub开发者（含CI用户与非用户），通过调查问卷收集数据，围绕三个核心维度展开：CI采用与服务选择的驱动力（区分需求驱动与社会影响驱动）、CI的普适性认知与采用障碍、以及开发者对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。

Result: 该研究为探索性调查研究，目前尚未给出最终结果；其预期成果是为CI推荐系统开发者和开源项目实践者提供实证依据，帮助优化CI服务选择和采用流程。

Conclusion: 研究结论将为两类受众提供价值：一是开发CI推荐系统的研究人员，二是希望简化开源项目CI采用流程的从业者，从而弥合CI采用决策中实证证据不足的空白。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) is central to modern software development, yet developers often struggle to choose the most suitable CI service. Prior work has identified barriers to CI adoption but offers little empirical evidence on how developers select CI services or whether adoption decisions are driven by genuine project needs versus social influence. This paper presents an exploratory survey study addressing that gap. We aim to contact about 5,000 active GitHub developers, including both CI users and non-users. The study investigates: (1) what drives CI adoption and service selection, distinguishing need-driven from socially influenced motivations; (2) whether developers consider CI universally necessary or context-dependent and what barriers hinder adoption; and (3) developers' perceptions of automated CI recommendation systems. Our findings will inform researchers developing CI recommendation systems and practitioners aiming to streamline CI adoption in open-source projects.

</details>


### [224] [BulkPR-Bench: Benchmarking Queue-Level Governance of Interacting Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02685)
*Zetong Xiong,Qiao Zhao,Jun Zhang,Xueying Lyu,Zhi Li,Yixiang Tu,Xiaowen Yang,Yunjie Zhang,Yufeng Wang,Zhe Zhang,Kaize Yu,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun,Zhuoxin Liu,Zekun Lin,Jianwen Yang,Ruining Chen,Ying Zhang,Tingxuan Pan,Ke Chen,Shubin Han,Chuanhao Sun,Yehua Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BulkPR-Bench，一个可执行基准测试，用于评估智能体在需要恢复PR之间关系并返回可执行顺序的安全子集的场景下的表现。基准包含581个新撰写的候选PR，来自18个真实仓库的快照，通过状态级执行验证关系图，并使用精确oracle计算最大安全子集。主要指标为关系交付分数（RDS）和全局安全门控产出（Global-SGY）。实验表明，最强的6个模型最高RDS为66.6%（对比最强顺序基线53.1%），但仅有8/324次运行能精确完成整个队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准大多只处理单一请求或固定变更序列，忽视了现实中多个PR同时排队且相互影响的情况。当PR之间存在交互时，最大化安全交付需要联合决策合并哪些变更以及按什么顺序合并，而现有基准无法评估这种能力。

Method: 构建BulkPR-Bench基准：在18个真实仓库的冻结快照上生成581个新PR；通过注册的逐状态仓库执行（包括隐藏安全检查）验证金标准关系图；使用精确oracle计算可实现的"最大安全子集"；提出两个指标——关系交付分数（RDS）衡量安全的交付与基于关系组的正确拒绝，全局安全门控产出（Global-SGY）衡量严格的全队列交付。

Result: 在批量K=32的主协议下，6个模型中RDS最高的三个估计值分别为66.6%、62.0%和57.9%，而最强顺序基线为53.1%；仅8/324次模型运行能精确完成队列；关键关系召回率在35.2%到57.7%之间；即使提供金标准关系的诊断运行仍显示显著的剩余提升空间。

Conclusion: 当前模型在关系组层面的收益尚不能转化为可靠的全队列治理能力。模型在关系发现和子集选择上有所进展，但整体队列精确交付仍极具挑战性，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks increasingly cover long-horizon, end-to-end, and interactive development, but typically retain one requested outcome or a fixed change sequence. Sequential policies can process a pull-request (PR) queue one candidate at a time, but when queued PRs interact, maximizing safe delivery can require jointly deciding which changes to merge and in what order. We introduce BulkPR-Bench, an executable benchmark in which an agent must recover consequential PR relations and return a large safe subset in executable order under a rolling-release protocol. The suite contains 581 newly authored candidate PRs on frozen snapshots of 18 real repositories. Registered state-by-state repository execution, including hidden safety checks, validates the gold relation graph; an exact oracle then computes the largest safe subset. Our primary metric, Relational Delivery Score (RDS), scores safe delivery and correct rejection over relation groups from the realized merge trace; Global Safety-Gated Yield (Global-SGY) separately measures strict delivery of the realized whole-queue plan. Under the buffered primary protocol with batch size $K=32$, the three highest RDS estimates among the six models are 66.6%, 62.0%, and 57.9%, compared with 53.1% for the strongest sequential baseline. Only 8 of 324 model runs complete a queue exactly. Critical-relation recall ranges from 35.2% to 57.7%, and diagnostic runs supplied with the gold relations show substantial remaining headroom. Gains on relation groups therefore do not yet translate into dependable whole-queue governance.

</details>


### [225] [PRWeaver: Evaluating LLM-Based Code Auditors against Long-Horizon Malicious Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02693)
*Yuekun Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRWeaver基准，包含208个执行验证的攻击样本（来自10个真实仓库，共832个渲染变体），系统评估了基于LLM的代码审计代理在PR工作流中对抗敌方策略的鲁棒性。实验发现，跨提交分解攻击对检测影响不大，而跨PR交织和载体融合可显著降低检测率，且全窗口审查下检测率最低（16-22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码审计器正被越来越多地集成到PR工作流中，但其面对跨仓库演化分布的攻击性修改时的可靠性尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对这类对抗性策略的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建PRWeaver基准：从10个真实仓库中提取208个经执行验证的攻击，每种攻击实例化为4种匹配的评审渲染（共832个），评估3种PR审计代理和6种审计器-模型系统。设置了四种攻击策略：跨提交分解、跨PR交织（N=16）、载体融合、全窗口审查（N=24）。

Result: 跨提交分解最多使检测率变化5个百分点（影响有限）；跨PR交织使检测率下降5-13个百分点；载体融合使检测率下降10-18个百分点；全窗口审查下检测率降至16-22%（远低于逐PR审查的50-60%）。

Conclusion: 仅访问仓库历史是不够的：当良性变更与恶意变更共同占据审计者的活跃审查上下文，或当声明目的能合理解释攻击性差异时，隐匿效果最为显著。

Abstract: LLM-based code auditors are increasingly integrated into pull-request (PR) workflows, yet their reliability against adversarial changes distributed across repository evolution remains poorly understood. We introduce PRWeaver, a benchmark of 208 execution-validated attacks from ten real-world repositories, each instantiated under four matched review renderings (832 renderings in total). We evaluate three PR-auditing agents across six auditor-model systems. Across all systems, decomposing an attack changes detection by at most five percentage points, showing that commit boundaries alone do not explain evasion. In contrast, per-PR interleaving at $N=16$ and coherent carrier fusion reduce detection by 5-13 and 10-18 points, respectively. Under whole-window review at $N=24$, detection falls to 16-22%, compared with 50-60% under per-PR review. These results show that access to repository history is insufficient: concealment becomes most effective when benign and malicious changes jointly occupy the auditor's active review context or when the stated purpose plausibly accounts for the attack-bearing diff.

</details>


### [226] [Don't Regenerate, Debug: A Domain-Specific Agent for Repairing Near-Miss Hardware Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02712)
*Yansong Sun,Shenxiu Wu,Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Junhao Qiu,Junming Cao,Shudi Shao,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向硬件加速器（GPU/NPU）kernel生成的领域专用调试代理，主张从"重新生成"转向"调试"失败的候选kernel，在更小的搜索空间和更密集的反馈下实现高效修复。实验显示Debug Pass@1达66.7%，远超重新生成的25.9%，且单个成功案例的token消耗减少92.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成kernel的流水线（结合强化学习和进化搜索）会产生大量被丢弃的候选kernel，其中许多是编译运行但未通过数值验证的"近失"算子，蕴含了可复用的领域知识和昂贵的推理/编译/执行投入，但被白白浪费。作者主张范式转变：与其重新生成，不如调试这些失败案例。

Method: 提出领域专用调试代理，解决三个核心挑战：（1）通过检索模式和诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺；（2）通过反作弊检测和全覆盖评估确保完整性；（3）通过收敛保护和有界迭代控制成本。调试同时承担扩展能力边界（恢复反复重新生成无法产生的算子）和降低每个交付算子的成本的双重职责。

Result: Debug Pass@1达66.7%，优于Regenerate Avg Pass@1（25.9%）和Regenerate Pass@3（40.7%）；每个成功案例的token消耗比三次重新生成低92.8%。组件消融表明知识库是关键驱动因素，完整性门控拒绝了工作流自身接受的12.5%-33.3%的成功案例。

Conclusion: 基于调试的kernel修复范式在效率和效果上均显著优于传统重新生成策略，知识库驱动的调试能够回收被废弃的"近失"算子，同时通过完整性门控提升正确性保障。

Abstract: Kernel generation for hardware accelerators such as GPUs and NPUs has become a proving ground for large language models (LLMs), and state-of-the-art systems raise correctness through pipelines that couple LLMs with agentic reinforcement learning and evolutionary search. Such pipelines generate, compile, and execute large numbers of candidate kernels, discarding most of them and forgoing the opportunity to distill failures into reusable knowledge. Many discarded candidates are near-miss operators that compile and run but fail numerical validation; each embodies genuine domain knowledge and a nontrivial investment in LLM inference, cross-compilation, and hardware execution. We argue for a paradigm shift: rather than regenerate, debug. Debugging is far more constrained than generating from scratch: the search space is small and feedback is dense. We present a domain-specific debug agent that addresses three core challenges in autonomous repair: mitigating knowledge scarcity through retrieved patterns and diagnostic instrumentation, ensuring integrity through anti-cheat detection and full-coverage evaluation, and controlling cost via convergence guards and bounded iteration. Debugging serves two complementary roles: it extends the capability frontier by recovering operators that repeated regeneration fails to produce, and it lowers cost per deliverable operator. Debug Pass@1 achieves 66.7% versus Regenerate Avg Pass@1's 25.9% and Regenerate Pass@3's 40.7%, while consuming 92.8% fewer tokens per success than three-trial regeneration. Component ablations show that the knowledge base drives recovery, while integrity gates reject 12.5-33.3% of the successes the workflow itself accepted.

</details>


### [227] [HyperFL: Query-Adaptive Representation Learning for Software Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02967)
*Shuai Shao,Yiming Zeng,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperFL，一种基于查询自适应表示学习的软件故障定位框架，通过轻量级超网络生成查询特定的LoRA参数来动态调整查询编码器，在真实故障定位基准上相比SOTA方法SweRank实现函数级MRR@10提升13.3%、Hit@1提升16.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的故障定位方法将所有问题报告编码为固定查询表示，忽略了真实世界问题报告在长度、结构和调试信息上的多样性，导致检索性能受限。

Method: HyperFL使用轻量级超网络为查询编码器动态生成查询特定的LoRA参数，实现查询自适应编码，同时保持代码编码器固定可复用，从而高效地适应不同查询特性。

Result: 在真实世界故障定位基准上，HyperFL在多个嵌入骨干网络上一致提升检索性能，函数级MRR@10相对提升13.3%，Hit@1相对提升16.7%，优于SOTA方法SweRank。

Conclusion: 进一步分析表明HyperFL能为不同问题特性学习不同的自适应模式，验证了查询自适应表示对软件问题定位的有效性。

Abstract: Software fault localization identifies the code locations responsible for reported issues and is a fundamental step toward automated debugging and program repair. Recent retrieval-based approaches formulate fault localization as a dense retrieval task by learning a shared embedding space between issue reports and source code. However, these methods encode all issue reports using a fixed query representation, despite the substantial diversity of real-world issue reports in length, structure, and debugging information. To address this limitation, we propose HyperFL, a query-adaptive representation learning framework for software fault localization. HyperFL employs a lightweight hypernetwork to generate query-specific LoRA parameters for the query encoder, enabling dynamic query adaptation while keeping the code encoder fixed and reusable. Experiments on a real-world issue localization benchmark demonstrate that HyperFL consistently improves retrieval performance across multiple embedding backbones, achieving up to 13.3% relative improvement in function-level MRR@10 and 16.7% relative improvement in Hit@1 over the state-of-the-art method SweRank. Further analysis shows that HyperFL learns distinct adaptation patterns for different issue characteristics, highlighting the effectiveness of query-adaptive representations for software issue localization.

</details>


### [228] [ConFL: Explainable Concurrent Fault Localization via Hierarchy-Guided LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02974)
*Shuai Shao,Dingbang Wang,Yiming Zeng,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ConFL框架，通过构建并发知识库(CKB)和LLM引导的分层检索来增强大语言模型在并发缺陷定位方面的能力，在八个大型Java项目的实验中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从bug报告定位并发缺陷极具挑战性，因为存在信息不完整、误导性的程序实体提及和复杂的跨线程交互，导致现有基于LLM的方法推理不稳定且可解释性有限。

Method: ConFL框架包含两个核心组件：从源代码构建结构化并发知识库(CKB)，以及执行LLM引导的分层检索，逐步将搜索空间从组件级缩小到交互级并发上下文。同时使用交互级DSL显式编码跨线程在共享资源上的交互，避免遍历深层调用链。

Result: 在八个大型Java项目的真实并发bug上，ConFL显著优于最先进的基于IR和基于LLM的基线方法，MRR达到0.503，MAP达到0.486，且对噪声bug报告、未见bug和不同LLM骨干模型均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将结构化并发知识与LLM推理相结合可以有效提升并发缺陷定位的准确性和可解释性，为并发bug定位提供了一个新的研究范式。

Abstract: Localizing concurrent bugs from bug reports alone is challenging due to incomplete information, misleading program-entity mentions, and complex cross-thread interactions, causing existing LLM-based approaches to suffer from unstable reasoning and limited explainability. We propose ConFL, an explainable concurrent fault localization framework that augments LLM reasoning with structured concurrency knowledge. ConFL constructs a Concurrent Knowledge Base (CKB) from source code and performs LLM-guided hierarchical retrieval to progressively narrow the search space from components to interaction-level concurrency contexts. An interaction-level DSL explicitly encodes cross-thread interactions over shared resources, enabling focused reasoning without traversing deep call chains. Experiments on real-world concurrent bugs from eight large-scale Java projects show that ConFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art IR-based and LLM-based baselines, achieving an MRR of 0.503 and a MAP of 0.486, while remaining robust to noisy bug reports, unseen bugs, and different LLM backbones.

</details>


### [229] [The Ground Is Shifting: A Reflection on the Foundations of Software Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03007)
*Thomas Bock,Audris Mockus,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出软件度量研究长期依赖版本控制等工具产生的人类开发痕迹数据，但随着AI代理开始使用这些工具，数据中人类起源的假设被打破，导致许多先前研究发现可能失效。作者提出需要检测数据中基础假设的违背，并通过系统化的AI辅助复制程序重新审视关键发现，以保持软件度量研究的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件度量研究从过去依赖手动收集数据，转变为利用版本控制等工具产生的痕迹数据，但这依赖于一个隐含假设——这些工具由专业开发者用于构建真实软件。随着AI代理大量使用这些工具，该假设被频繁打破，导致基于这些数据的许多研究发现可能不再可靠，因此迫切需要应对这一变化以保持软件度量研究的相关性。

Method: 提出一个系统化的AI辅助复制程序，该程序重新审视软件度量的关键研究发现，利用现代技术和方法，检测当前数据中对基础假设（即痕迹由人类开发者产生）的违背情况，并开发在数据生成环境变化下仍然有效的新的度量方法论。

Result: 论文提出了一个行动框架而非具体实验结果：识别出AI代理生成的数据痕迹对软件度量研究根基的威胁，并倡导通过AI辅助的复制研究来验证和更新已有发现，确保研究方法在当前及未来的数据环境下依然稳健。

Conclusion: 为了使软件度量研究在未来继续发挥意义，必须立即采取行动：一方面检测当代数据中对人类起源假设的违背，另一方面开发适用于新数据环境的方法，并通过系统化的AI辅助复制程序来保证关键研究发现的可信度。

Abstract: For most of the past six decades, software measurement relied on labor-intensive manual collection of proprietary data, which hampered progress. The shift to repurposing traces from version control and related tools dramatically expanded data availability$\unicode{x2014}$especially with the rise of open-source software$\unicode{x2014}$but hinged on an often unstated assumption: that these tools are used by professional developers to build genuine software systems. However, as trace-generating tools, data types and scale, and empirical methods have all evolved, it has become clear that changes in data generation and analytical approaches affect many prior findings about software development, maintenance, and evolution. With AI agents now actively using these same tools, the resulting traces frequently violate the original assumption of human origin. To preserve the relevance of software measurement research, immediate action is needed: We must detect when foundational assumptions are violated in contemporary data and develop new methodologies that remain valid under changed circumstances. To this end, we propose a systematic AI-assisted replication program that revisits key findings using modern techniques, aiming for methods that yield consistent results on current data to keep software measurement meaningful.

</details>


### [230] [LLM Serving in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Frameworks, Methods, and System Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03036)
*Forough Majidi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件系统中LLM服务框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer）的采用情况进行大规模实证研究，发现vLLM是最流行的框架，并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法，多框架组合使用有限但能互补，框架采用因模型类型和部署场景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量LLM推理、优化和服务技术及框架的研究，但缺乏对这些框架在实践中的采用情况的了解，需要实证调查以填补这一空白。

Method: 选取五个LLM专用服务框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer），在开源软件系统中分析这些框架及其技术的单独采用和组合使用情况，比较不同LLM类别下的采用差异，并从仓库层面分析意图、焦点、用例和架构设计。

Result: vLLM在流行度和采用率上最为突出；并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别；多框架使用有限，但组合框架能连接服务栈中的互补能力；框架采用因模型系列、模态、模型大小、领域专业化和部署设置而异；LLM服务框架支持RL推理、多模态生成、微服务和云基础设施等应用和架构。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM服务框架在实践中采用的大规模实证特征描述，为研究人员、框架维护者和LLM系统从业者提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into software systems and AI services, making efficient LLM serving a concern for software engineering. Serving LLMs is challenging because inference requires computation, memory, GPU resources, and execution while maintaining latency and throughput. Although prior research has proposed LLM inference, optimization, and serving techniques and frameworks, little is known about how they are adopted in practice. In this study, we investigate the use of LLM serving frameworks and serving methods in open-source software systems. We identify and analyze five LLM-specific frameworks: vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, and FlashInfer. We examine how these frameworks and techniques are adopted individually and in combination, how adoption varies across categories of LLMs, and how repositories differ in intent, focus, use case, and architectural design. Our results show that vLLM is the most visible framework in popularity and adoption, while parallel computation, memory management, and network pruning are the most frequently used serving-method categories. Multi-framework usage is limited, suggesting that developers rely on a single serving framework; however, combined frameworks connect complementary capabilities across the serving stack. Framework adoption varies across model families, modalities, model sizes, domain specializations, and deployment settings. Repository-level analysis shows that LLM serving frameworks support applications and architectures, including Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based reasoning, multimodal generation and understanding, microservices, and cloud infrastructure. Overall, this study provides a large-scale empirical characterization of LLM serving framework adoption in practice and offers insights for researchers, framework maintainers, and practitioners working on LLM systems.

</details>


### [231] [Integration Barriers in Open-Source SSI Frameworks: An Exploratory Developer Experience Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03039)
*Breno Cerqueira Reis Nakamura,Arlindo Flavio da Conceição*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性探测研究，调查了九名开发者在使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask等开源SSI工具时的开发者体验，揭示了被动操作（如凭据接收）相对成熟，而主动构建任务（尤其是模式定制）存在显著架构摩擦的"抽象鸿沟"，并提出基于Web的沙盒、AI辅助模式生成器和可执行文档三大架构改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSI（自主主权身份）有望实现数字身份的去中心化，但其广泛采用受到集成复杂性和工具成熟度不足的阻碍，因此需要系统性地调查开源SSI工具链中的开发者体验障碍。

Method: 采用探索性探测研究方法，招募九名具备去中心化身份概念知识的开发者作为早期集成者，使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask执行核心凭据生命周期任务，通过开放性开发者报告的定性主题分析并辅以任务级难度评分来揭示重复出现的集成障碍。

Result: 研究发现存在关键抽象鸿沟：被动操作（如凭据接收）相对成熟，但主动构建任务（特别是架构定制）暴露出显著架构摩擦，这些障碍源于API抽象不足、脆弱的环境配置以及无法跟上生态系统快速演进的文档。

Conclusion: 本研究刻画了当前SSI开源生态系统中的结构性集成障碍，并提出三种架构转变建议：基于Web的沙盒环境、AI辅助模式生成器以及可执行文档策略，以弥合抽象鸿沟并提升开发者体验。

Abstract: Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) promises to decentralize digital identity, but widespread adoption remains hindered by integration complexity and tooling immaturity. This paper investigates the Developer Experience (DX) of open-source SSI tooling through an exploratory probe study. Nine developers with prior knowledge of decentralized identity concepts, representing early integrators building SSI applications, attempted core credential lifecycle tasks using Walt.id, Traction, and MetaMask. Our goal was to surface recurring integration barriers through qualitative thematic analysis of open-ended developer reports, complemented by task-level difficulty ratings. Our findings reveal a critical abstraction gap: while passive operations like credential receipt are relatively mature, active construction tasks, particularly schema customization, expose significant architectural friction. We identify that these barriers stem from inadequate API abstractions, brittle environment configurations, and documentation that fails to track the ecosystem's rapid evolution. These issues reflect structural design decisions in current frameworks. This study characterizes the structural integration barriers in the current SSI open-source ecosystem. To address the identified gaps, we propose three architectural shifts for developer tooling: web-based sandboxes, AI-assisted schema generators, and executable documentation strategies.

</details>


### [232] [Efficient Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Parser Stack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03065)
*Yongmin Li,Yihong Dong,Jia Li,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PSC，一种新的语法约束解码方法，通过将词汇表中所有token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器，使每次解码步骤只需检查一次解析器栈即可计算完整词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇表大小无关。实验表明掩码计算速度比基线快最高700倍，端到端吞吐量接近无约束解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语法约束解码（GCD）方法存在在线计算开销高的问题，延迟通常随词汇表大小线性增长，严重限制了大型词汇表LLM的吞吐量。

Method: PSC方法在预处理阶段将词汇表中所有token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器，解码时每步只需检查一次解析器栈即可获得完整词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇表大小无关。同时提供了预处理开销分析和盈亏平衡点分析，帮助用户决定是否自行预处理。

Result: 实验结果显示PSC在复杂编程语言语法上掩码计算速度比基线快高达700倍，在符合模式的JSON上快达30倍；端到端LLM吞吐量接近无约束解码水平。

Conclusion: PSC显著降低了语法约束解码的计算开销，使受约束解码的吞吐量接近无约束解码，为大规模词汇表LLM的语法受限生成提供了高效解决方案，并通过盈亏平衡分析指导用户的预处理决策。

Abstract: LLMs are widely used to generate structured output like source code or JSON. Grammar-constrained decoding (GCD) can guarantee the syntactic validity of the generated output, by masking out tokens that violate rules specified by a context-free grammar. However, the online computational overhead of existing GCD methods, with latency typically scaling linearly with vocabulary size, limits the throughput of LLMs, especially for models with large vocabularies. To address this issue, we propose PSC, a novel grammar-constrained decoding method. By combining acceptance conditions of all vocabulary tokens into a single classifier of the parser stack during preprocessing, PSC can compute the complete vocabulary mask by checking the parser stack exactly once per decoding step, with time complexity independent of the vocabulary size. Experiments show that PSC computes masks up to 700$\times$ faster than baselines on complex programming language grammars, and up to 30$\times$ faster for schema-conformant JSON; end-to-end LLM throughput with PSC approaches that of unconstrained decoding. We analyze the preprocessing overhead for preprocessing providers and decoding users, and provide a break-even point analysis to help users decide whether to do preprocessing by themselves.

</details>


### [233] [Cross-Ecosystem Bug Classification in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03173)
*Mir Mohammad Yousuf,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对量子软件工程中的缺陷进行了跨生态系统的比较研究，分析了来自Qiskit（12,910个issue）和其他11个仓库（4,613个issue，包括Cirq和PyQuil）的缺陷。使用基于规则的分类框架，发现经典缺陷占67%，量子特定缺陷占27-30%。该框架在缺陷类型层面具有泛化能力，并在细粒度层面优于四个监督机器学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工程中经典组件与量子组件的交互产生了复杂且难以理解的缺陷模式，但对这些缺陷的表征研究不足，缺乏跨生态系统的系统性比较。

Method: 收集12,910个Qiskit issue和4,613个来自11个其他仓库的issue，使用基于规则的分类框架按缺陷类型、类别、严重程度、质量属性和量子特定子类型进行分析，并与四种监督机器学习基线进行对比，同时进行2017-2025年的纵向分析。

Result: 经典缺陷占67%，量子特定缺陷占27-30%。Qiskit仓库呈现更多兼容性相关缺陷，其他生态系统呈现更高的语法和量子特定缺陷率。量子特定缺陷以门和电路问题为主，但非Qiskit项目展现更广泛的多样性。基于规则的框架在缺陷类型层面具有良好的泛化能力，且一致优于数据驱动模型。纵向分析表明量子特定缺陷随时间保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 这是首个跨生态系统的量子软件缺陷分布比较研究，证明了可解释、可自动化的基于规则分类框架在指导量子软件测试、调试和质量保证方面的实用性。

Abstract: Quantum software engineering faces unique challenges due to the interaction of classical and quantum components, which produce complex and often poorly understood bug patterns. Characterizing these bugs is essential for advancing testing, debugging, and quality assurance in quantum ecosystems. This paper presents a comparative study of 12,910 issues from Qiskit and 4,613 issues from 11 additional repositories, including Cirq and PyQuil. Using a rule-based classification framework, we analyze bugs by type, category, severity, quality attributes, and quantum-specific subtypes. Results show that classical bugs consistently dominate (67%) across ecosystems, while quantum-specific bugs account for 27-30%. Ecosystem-specific trends emerge: Qiskit repositories exhibit more compatibility related bugs, whereas other ecosystems show higher syntax and quantum-specific bug rates. Across both ecosystems, gate and circuit issues dominate quantum-specific bugs, though non-Qiskit projects reveal broader diversity, including algorithmic, resource, and hybrid-interface issues. Statistical validation confirms that the framework generalizes at the bug-type level while detecting significant variations at finer levels. Benchmarking against four supervised machine-learning baselines further shows that the rule-based framework consistently outperforms data-driven models, particularly for fine-grained quantum-specific subtypes, while longitudinal analysis (2017-2025) indicates that quantum- specific bugs remain relatively stable over time rather than exhibiting a steady increase. This study provides the first cross- ecosystem comparison of bug distributions in quantum software, demonstrating the utility of an interpretable, automation-ready, rule-based framework for guiding testing, debugging, and quality assurance.

</details>


### [234] [Fail-Fast, Restart-Smart: Early Failure Prediction and Restart for SWE Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03222)
*Chenyu Wang,Yunbo Lyu,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yaniv Harel,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FailFast-RestartSmart两阶段控制器，通过轻量级0.6B监控模型从可观察前缀预测失败并及时终止，然后以相同策略重新启动轨迹，同时将中断的代码差异作为可选项供智能体参考。在SWE-bench Verified上可节省14.6%-20.4%执行token，并将分辨率从66.6%提升至71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体解决仓库级问题时，长时间轨迹因上下文累积而成本高昂，且失败运行往往包含冗余探索或循环，可能可提前检测。但提前终止可能打断本可成功的轨迹，而失败的轨迹中也可能包含有用的代码编辑，需要在节省成本和保留有效信息之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出两阶段控制器：1) FailFast，一个0.6B轻量级监控模型，使用终止和密集失败转通过的监督信号训练，仅从可观察前缀预测失败，不使用策略的logits或隐藏状态；2) RestartSmart，当FailFast发出警报时，启动一次全新的同策略滚动而不带先前提示历史，并将中断的仓库差异作为可选覆盖层供智能体检查、应用或丢弃。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，仅用Qwen3.6-27B轨迹训练的监控模型可迁移到三个其他策略（包括闭源API模型），在5%假阳性率下节省14.6%-20.4%执行token；Qwen3.6-27B上20.4%的节省超过per-step AgentStop的12.5%。在25%假阳性率下，RestartSmart将Qwen3.6-27B分辨率从66.6%提升到71.8%，而冷重启仅达66.8%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持提前终止配合顺序同策略恢复的有效性，表明轻量级监控模型能够跨策略迁移预测失败，且重启时保留中断差异可显著提升任务解决率。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents resolve repository-level issues through long trajectories that grow increasingly expensive as context accumulates. Failed runs tend to be longer and exhibit redundant exploration or looping, suggesting that some failures may be detectable before completion. Early termination, however, risks interrupting trajectories that would otherwise succeed; conversely, an unsuccessful trajectory may still contain useful repository edits. We present FailFast-RestartSmart, a two-stage controller for a single active trajectory. FailFast is a lightweight 0.6B monitor trained with terminal and dense fail-to-pass supervision to predict failure from observable prefixes without policy logits or hidden states. Upon an alarm, RestartSmart launches a fresh same-policy rollout without prior prompt history and offers the interrupted repository diff as an optional overlay that the agent may inspect, apply, or discard. On SWE-bench Verified, a monitor trained solely on Qwen3.6-27B trajectories transfers to three other policies, including a closed-API model, and saves 14.6%-20.4% of execution tokens at a target 5% false-positive rate; on Qwen3.6-27B, its 20.4% saving exceeds the 12.5% achieved by our per-step AgentStop adaptation. At a target 25% false-positive rate, RestartSmart raises Qwen3.6-27B resolution from 66.6% to 71.8%, whereas cold restart reaches only 66.8%. Together, these results support early termination with sequential same-policy recovery.

</details>


### [235] [NotDec: WebAssembly Decompilation With Inter-Procedural Type Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03286)
*Jikai Wang,Ningyu He,Tianming Liu,Junhai Wang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NotDec是一个先进的WebAssembly反编译框架，通过扩展类型检查算法、跨过程类型恢复和Memory SSA等技术，实现了高可读性、语义一致的C代码反编译，在重编译成功率和类型恢复精度上显著优于Ghidra等基线工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly被广泛应用于浏览器、容器、物联网和服务端无服务器平台，成为通用的底层字节码标准，但其隐藏漏洞和恶意意图的审计至关重要。现有反编译工具存在输出冗长、可读性差、类型恢复能力有限等问题。

Method: NotDec框架包含三个核心组件：1) 扩展WebAssembly类型检查算法将字节码提升为基于SSA的中间表示(IR)；2) 应用跨过程类型恢复算法Retypd，结合指针和数值值区分方法来恢复复杂数据结构；3) 利用Memory SSA和保持语义的结构化控制流分析，生成可读且语义一致的C代码。

Result: NotDec在全部5,241个Juliet样本和所有Howard数据集程序上实现100%的重编译成功率，远超Ghidra的45.95%。在类型恢复精度上，NotDec在真实世界程序中恢复85.33%的结构体成员访问，远超Ghidra的9.24%。跨过程版本在大二进制文件上存在可扩展性挑战，但过程内变体NotDec_F在效率上表现优异，内存占用不到Ghidra的一半，在未优化二进制上执行时间减少高达97%。

Conclusion: NotDec显著提升了WebAssembly反编译的可读性、语义一致性和类型恢复能力，在重编译成功率和类型恢复精度上大幅超越现有工具，为WebAssembly安全审计提供了高效可靠的反编译解决方案。

Abstract: With WebAssembly widely supported in browsers, containers, IoT devices, and serverless platforms and increasingly adopted as a universal low-level bytecode standard, auditing its hidden vulnerabilities and malicious intentions has become critical. Decompiling existing WebAssembly modules can help security researchers and end users understand binary behavior, but current tools suffer from verbose result, poor readability, and limited type recovery.
  We present NotDec, an advanced WebAssembly decompilation framework. NotDec extends the WebAssembly type checking algorithm to lift bytecode into an SSA-based IR, applies the inter-procedural type recovery algorithm Retypd with pointer and numeric value differentiation methods to recover complex data structures, and leverages Memory SSA alongside semantics-preserving structured control-flow analysis to emit readable, semantically consistent C code.
  NotDec achieves 100% recompilation success rate on all 5,241 Juliet samples and all Howard dataset programs, significantly outperforming baselines including Ghidra (45.95% success rate). On type recovery accuracy, NotDec recovers 85.33% of struct member accesses in real-world programs, vastly exceeding Ghidra's 9.24%. While the full inter-procedural version faces scalability challenges on large binaries, the intra-procedural variant NotDec_F demonstrates superior efficiency, consuming less than half of Ghidra's memory and up to 97% less execution time on unoptimized binaries.

</details>


### [236] [Evaluating LLM Trade-offs for Enterprise Automation: Lessons from Workflow Generation in a Production Enterprise Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03311)
*Xavier Wrenn,Radoslav Raykov,Aleksandar Angelov,Hirokuni Kitahara,Yuji Watanabe,Anca Sailer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种大语言模型在企业平台中生成AI驱动工作流的表现，通过29个真实IT自动化场景进行基准测试，提出了一种分段式（piecewise）流水线架构，将工作流构建分解为变量脚手架、基础块组装和嵌套块生成，显著提升结构成功率（从31.5-82.8%提升至74.1-97.8%），并证明了较小模型可通过分段分解达到生产可行水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业合规管理需要快速适应不断演进的监管框架和严格的修复SLA，传统静态编排器在混合云环境中难以应对事件驱动评估对自动化代码的实时自适应需求，需要探索AI驱动工作流生成的新方法。

Method: 对六种大语言模型进行系统评估，涵盖两种生成流水线架构（单体式vs分段式），在29个真实IT自动化场景中进行8次独立运行，共2,784次测试，并分析了成本、延迟、模型选择等生产权衡因素。

Result: 单体流水线结构成功率为31.5-82.8%，分段式流水线提升至74.1-97.8%；mistral-medium-2505和gpt-oss-120b取得最高结构成功率（96.1%和97.8%），但mistral-medium-2505比mistral-small贵19倍；mistral-small在分段分解下可达95.7%结构成功率，每工作流仅0.01美元。

Conclusion: 分段式分解使较小模型达到生产可行性，消除了对昂贵前沿模型的依赖；实际部署中需区分结构有效性与语义正确性，并提供模型无关、可扩展的云工程自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise compliance management requires rapid adaptation to evolving regulatory frameworks (e.g., DORA, AI RMF, FedRAMP) and tight remediation SLAs. Traditional static orchestrators often fail in hybrid cloud environments where event-driven assessments demand that automation code adapt to runtime context in seconds. This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [237] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究OSS中AI治理政策，分析了29,624个GitHub仓库，识别出385个采用AI政策的项目，提出TRACE框架（涵盖透明度、责任、归属、约束、执行五个维度），并利用倾向性得分匹配和双重差分法评估政策效果。结果显示AI治理主要起规范而非禁止作用，政策采纳带来维护者参与度提升、AI披露增加、评审互动更丰富和代码质量改善，且强调透明与责任的政策效果优于单纯限制性政策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在快速改变开源软件开发，各项目开始引入AI辅助贡献的治理政策，但学界对这些政策的差异及对开发者体验的影响知之甚少。

Method: 对29,624个GitHub仓库进行大规模实证分析，识别385个采纳AI政策的项目；提出TRACE框架分类五个治理维度；采用倾向性得分匹配和纵向双重差分法（difference-in-differences）估计政策效果。

Result: AI治理主要起到规范而非禁止AI辅助开发的作用；政策采纳显著提升维护者参与度、AI披露程度、评审互动质量和代码质量，同时AI辅助贡献持续增长；强调透明与责任的政策比纯粹限制性政策带来更好的社区和质量结果。

Conclusion: 不同AI治理策略对开发者体验有显著不同的影响，研究为OSS社区设计有效的AI政策提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [238] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变质关系（MR）的框架，用于评估UI组件测试套件的行为验证质量。该框架从组件源码、文档和测试中推断组件特定的MR，并通过混合确定性与语义分析将测试与MR对齐，计算关系级MR覆盖率。实验表明，现有测试套件触及的行为关系远多于明确验证的关系（MR Cover仅42.5%-47.6%），MR覆盖率能补充基于执行覆盖率指标的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UI组件库评估依赖语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率等执行指标，但这些指标无法有效反映测试是否验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为关系，缺乏对行为验证充分性的洞察。

Method: 构建基于MR的评估框架：利用UI特定分类体系从组件源码、文档和测试中推断组件特定的变质关系；通过混合确定性与语义分析将测试与推断的MR对齐；计算关系级MR覆盖率指标（如MR Cover、MR Touch）；人工验证推断MR空间和测试-MR对齐的准确性。

Result: 在三种LLM配置下，MR Cover保持在42.5%-47.6%之间且始终低于MR Touch；大多数未覆盖的关系属于弱oracle案例（行为被触及但缺乏显式行为验证）；MR覆盖率能揭示语句/分支覆盖率无法反映的行为缺口；多数问题描述可映射到推断的MR关系类型，MR标签在MR相关故障检测中呈现趋势性关联。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率提供了一种互补的关系级视角，用于评估现代UI组件测试中的行为验证充分性，弥补了传统执行覆盖率指标的不足。

Abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [239] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAV框架，通过路由（Route）、对齐（Align）、验证（Verify）三个阶段协同优化，在不修改骨干模型架构的前提下，显著提升代码生成的功能正确性，在MBPP基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成上虽有显著进步，但在异构编程任务中仍难以实现强功能正确性，单一提示策略和单一直接生成输出往往不足以应对多样化的任务需求。

Method: RAV包含三个协调阶段：1）Route阶段在生成前进行任务感知的提示路由；2）Align阶段通过对齐的LoRA适配来减少微调提示与推理提示之间的不匹配；3）Verify阶段通过针对可见公共测试执行多个候选输出来选择最终结果。

Result: 在MBPP基准的Sanitized和Full两种设置下，完整RAV管线分别达到0.8911和0.8520，相比基础模型提升了6.35和9.92个百分点。消融实验表明，任务感知路由和对齐适配在与基于执行的验证结合时效果显著增强。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化任务提示方式、模型适配方式和最终输出选择方式，可以在不修改骨干架构的情况下有意义地提升代码生成的功能正确性，且鲁棒性和污染分析支持了观察到的改进的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
