  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [240] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了自我进化的编码代理（self-evolving coding agents）这一新兴领域，定义了其与常规编码代理及通用自我进化代理的区别，提出了以对象为中心的分类体系，并从进化时机和软件特定证据两个正交视角进行补充分析，最后总结了该领域面临的挑战及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的编码代理在部署后大多保持静态，而软件开发本身是动态、反馈密集的过程——代码库会演化、依赖会变化、测试会失败、修复尝试会留下可复用的经验。这种矛盾促使了自我进化编码代理研究的发展，但该领域缺乏系统性的整理与综述。

Method: 本文采用系统的文献综述方法：首先明确定义自我进化编码代理并将其与常规编码代理和通用自我进化代理区分；然后提出以对象为中心的（object-centered）分类体系来描述系统中进化的内容；再以两个正交视角补充：进化发生的时机（when）以及驱动进化的软件特定证据（what software-specific evidence drives it）。

Result: 研究发现可执行反馈、仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为代理自我进化的天然领域，但也带来了反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化性等新挑战。文章收集的相关论文已整理在GitHub仓库中。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在厘清自我进化编码代理的概念边界，为设计更自适应、可靠且具备软件意识的代理系统提供基础，并指出了未来研究需要重点解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [241] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于Transactive Memory Systems理论，通过对152名软件专业人员的调查数据构建五个PLS-SEM模型，发现AI工具采用与社区坏味道（community smells）的关系取决于协作类型：在专业化工作中，AI通过提升知识共享同伴互动来减少坏味道；在协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，补充而非替代人际互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具的日益普及正在改变软件团队的协作、知识共享和协调方式，但其对团队社会动态（尤其是不良协作模式）的影响尚不明确，存在研究空白。

Method: 以Transactive Memory Systems（TMS）理论为基础，验证人-AI与人-人交互在专业化和协调两个维度上的测量工具，基于152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员调查数据，测试五个PLS-SEM模型；社区坏味道构念源自文献综述，并经专家调查和因子分析验证。

Result: AI采用与社区坏味道之间并非单一关系：在专业化工作中，AI与更高的知识共享同伴互动相关，进而与更少的坏味道相关；在协调工作中，AI直接与更高的沟通质量相关，对人际互动起补充而非替代作用。

Conclusion: 提供了一个经实证验证、基于TMS理论的模型，表明AI与社区坏味道之间的关系取决于协作类型，并提供可复用的测量工具及对研究和实践的循证启示。

Abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [242] [Leveraging System-Level Observations to Inform Bayesian Learning of Model Parameters for Quantitative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03489)
*Simos Gerasimou,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EPIK是一种结合贝叶斯学习与定量验证的方法，通过系统级可观测属性来引导先验知识的嵌入，以提升量化验证的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断中的先验知识（PK）对定量验证结果的准确性和鲁棒性有重大影响，而传统方法要求PK直接作用于形式模型的转换参数，不准确或无信息的PK会导致错误的验证结果。

Method: EPIK利用系统级可直接观测且具有真实世界语义的属性，通过构建一个双重优化问题来推导未知转换参数的分布，然后将这些分布嵌入到定量验证中，用于验证新的或难以测量的属性。

Result: 基于多个真实世界案例的变体以及多种EPIK实例化的详细实验评估表明，该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: EPIK为贝叶斯定量验证中的先验知识获取提供了一种更实用、更灵活的新途径，能够提升验证结果的准确性，并适用于多种实际场景。

Abstract: Combining Bayesian learning and quantitative verification is a powerful toolset for analysing key quantitative properties of software systems, like reliability and response time. However, the accuracy and robustness of verification results strongly depend on the prior knowledge (PK) underlying Bayesian inference. This knowledge reflects original beliefs about the probability of events and typically depends on domain expertise. Using inaccurate or uninformative PK can negatively affect quantitative analysis, yielding incorrect verification results. Our EPIK approach tackles this important challenge by eliciting and embedding PK in quantitative verification equipped with Bayesian estimators. Unlike existing approaches that require PK on formal model transition parameters, EPIK leverages system-level properties that are directly observable and are linked to real-world semantics. EPIK formulates a twofold optimisation problem to derive the distributions of unknown transition parameters and then embeds these distributions to verify new or difficult-to-measure (elusive) properties. The detailed experimental evaluation using multiple variants of real-world case studies and diverse EPIK instantiations shows its effectiveness, flexibility and generality.

</details>


### [243] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeAssay，一个包含185个Python任务的分类优先基准测试，通过审计地面真值、公开/隐藏测试、变异测试套件验证和代码属性度量来评估LLM代码生成。研究发现审计后修正了9%的正确性标签，并将模型间最佳到最差的差距从11.9提升到23.7个百分点，且安全提示在提升代码质量方面无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于测试的LLM代码生成评估依赖测试基准的可靠性，但测试基准确性往往未经验证，且基于测试的正确性只能捕捉生成代码的部分可观察属性，无法全面评估代码质量。

Method: 构建CodeAssay基准，包含185个Python任务、10个软件工程类别。采用审计过的地面真值、公开测试（用于生成与修复）、隐藏测试（用于评分）、基于变异的测试套件验证，以及选定的代码属性度量。对7个专有LLM和14个模型-提示配置进行系统评估。

Result: 审计后重新评分改变了170/1890个正确性标签（9.0%），最佳到最差模型差距从11.9个百分点扩大到23.7个百分点。完整和隐藏测试套件的变异分数分别为82.6%和74.8%。7个专有LLM的标准提示正确性范围为77.3%-98.9%，21个模型对中有12对存在显著差异。安全提示未产生正确性显著变化，也未一致减少静态分析发现，反而增加了程序长度和圈复杂度。

Conclusion: 可靠评估LLM生成代码需要验证过的地面真值、受保护的测试以及多种明确解释的度量指标。CodeAssay为AI辅助软件开发的循证模型评估提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [244] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiHolmes，一个基于LLM的仓库级时间低效修复定位框架，通过差异化剖析识别热点、提取紧凑执行路径、结合领域引导的LLM推理来定位低效逻辑，并构建了首个仓库级低效定位基准RepoEffi-Bench（含140个Python仓库问题），实验表明其在文件级和函数级定位上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件系统常存在时间低效问题导致执行时间过长，但不同于功能缺陷，这类问题不会产生测试失败或堆栈线索，传统方法和基于LLM的缺陷定位方法均不适用。运行剖析虽能提供替代证据，但面临三个挑战：单次剖析无法区分热点与执行噪声、现有剖析器难以从繁杂后台执行中提取相关路径、观测热点与真实修复位置之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: EffiHolmes采用三个核心步骤：(1)在默认和缩放工作负载下进行差异化剖析，识别低效热点；(2)提取连接热点到所报告低效函数的紧凑执行路径；(3)使用领域引导的LLM推理定位底层低效逻辑。同时构建RepoEffi-Bench基准，包含从流行Python仓库收集的140个高质量问题。

Result: EffiHolmes在多个基线（检索式、智能体式、剖析式）上持续取得更优表现：使用GPT-5.1时文件级Acc@3提升4.29个百分点，使用qwen3-4b时函数级Acc@5提升15.00个百分点，且在不同模型容量下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EffiHolmes证明了差异化剖析与LLM推理相结合能有效解决仓库级时间低效修复定位问题，突破了传统缺陷定位方法在该场景下的局限，并提供了首个公开基准以推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable. Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [245] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReBug是一个基于上下文的智能体系统，能够从自然语言缺陷报告中重建、执行并验证Web GUI缺陷的浏览器级复现流程。在667个真实缺陷报告上，平均RSR达49.96%，任务完成率74.96%，动作执行成功率86.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言缺陷报告复现Web GUI缺陷对软件维护至关重要，但现有技术主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏对Web GUI的端到端视觉执行和验证能力，且报告常缺少依赖和输入文件等前置条件。

Method: ReBug将复现分为两个阶段：准备阶段从报告和可用工件中重建缺失的前置条件并生成高层复现计划；执行阶段在真实浏览器中进行工具中介的交互，维护页面状态和动作历史的结构化摘要，并根据报告中的预期验证最终状态。

Result: 在来自四个开源Web应用的667个真实缺陷报告上，ReBug优于两个基线方法，平均RSR为49.96%，平均任务完成率为74.96%，平均动作执行成功率为86.54%。历史回放显示成功流程能在恢复的缺陷版本上暴露原始缺陷行为。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文重建和状态感知的浏览器执行能有效支持基于缺陷报告的浏览器复现，该方法的实用性通过对真实Web应用的广泛评估得到验证。

Abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [246] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型在截图生成代码任务中，因重复UI模式而产生视觉模式完成偏置的问题。作者构建了首个针对该偏置的基准测试，包含从Design2Code数据集精选的30个网页、1,440张评估截图，覆盖卡片结构和文本样式两种模式，并测试了五个前沿MLLM。结果显示所有模型都强烈偏向重复基线，卡片宽度扰动平均偏置率达69.78%，文本字体大小扰动达80.22%，而准确率分别仅为21.17%和7.89%。推理分析表明更多推理努力与更低偏置相关，但模型即使识别出异常元素仍会覆盖为模式一致答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在将网页截图转换为前端代码时，重复出现的UI模式可能导致模型输出视觉上不正确但与模式一致的结果。这种模式完成偏置尚未被系统性研究，作者希望首次量化这一失败模式并评估其严重程度。

Method: 作者设计了截图到代码的填空任务基准：在重复UI模式中扰动一个局部元素，让模型从截图和HTML上下文中恢复被遮罩的宽度或字体大小值。从Design2Code数据集中精选30个网页，构建1,440张评估截图，涵盖结构性卡片和文本样式模式，并包含标准与噪声叠加条件。评估五个前沿MLLM，并进行推理过程分析。

Result: 所有五个被测MLLM均表现出强烈的模式完成偏置：卡片宽度扰动平均偏置率69.78%，文本字体大小扰动80.22%；平均准确率仅21.17%和7.89%。Codex-5.3表现最佳但在卡片（68.61%准确率）到文本（13.89%）任务上有大幅下降；Flash-3.0在文本任务上偏置率达96.11%。噪声、更细微的扰动和边界位置进一步加剧偏置。

Conclusion: 多模态代码生成存在一个具体的失败模式——模式完成偏置，其严重程度与视觉显著性密切相关。即使模型能够识别出异常元素，仍可能覆盖该判断而输出模式一致的答案，表明该偏置具有深层认知层面的顽固性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [247] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCite，一个三阶段方法，用于检测和修复Linux内核代码注释中因函数重构或删除而过时的函数引用。在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上检测到869个过时引用，89.0%的修复建议有用，42.5%可直接应用，提交的75个补丁中有50个被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核演化过程中，代码注释中的函数引用可能因函数被重构或删除而变得过时，这种外部导致的过期引用会误导维护者并阻碍代码理解。已有的代码-注释不一致检测工作主要针对Javadoc注释与其直接注释函数之间的语义错位，不适用于Linux内核中这种外部诱导的过期引用类型。

Method: ReCite采用三阶段方法：(1)检测未解析的函数形式符号——注释中看似引用函数但当前代码库中找不到匹配函数的符号；(2)通过Git历史追踪每个未解析符号的演化历程；(3)基于演化历史和当前代码上下文，生成基于LLM的修复建议。

Result: 在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上，ReCite检测到869个过时引用并生成修复建议。对200个抽样修复的人工评估显示，178个(89.0%)提供有用修复指导，85个(42.5%)可直接应用。提交的75个补丁中有50个已被接受。此外还对所有未解析的函数形式符号进行了实证研究。

Conclusion: ReCite有效解决了Linux内核中因外部代码演化导致的注释函数引用过期问题，通过结合符号检测、Git历史追踪和LLM修复建议生成，能够高精度地识别和修复此类过时引用，并且修复建议被广泛接受（50/75已接收），证明了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [248] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调查了197个真实TUI应用，发现仅12%的测试代码覆盖界面，45%的测试从未发送输入。作者构建了一个基于4种TUI框架的headless基准测试，比较了4个前沿LLM与随机探索在相同预算下的表现，发现没有模型全面占优，随机基线因吞吐量更高而在崩溃发现上有优势，但LLM在单次交互效率上更优且能触达输入门控故障。自动推导启动输入是最大的实际改进点，行覆盖率对崩溃发现预测能力弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端用户界面(TUI)在开发者工具中已广泛使用，结合了GUI的有状态屏幕导向行为与终端部署方式，但缺乏专门的测试方法论，现有测试实践严重不足。

Method: 调查197个真实TUI应用，将应用转化为覆盖ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python和ink/TypeScript四种框架的headless基准测试，打包为带插桩的Docker镜像，记录行/控件覆盖率、渲染终端状态和崩溃，在相等时间预算下比较四个前沿LLM与随机探索。

Result: 没有模型全面占优。随机是强的时间预算基线，但其崩溃优势来自更高吞吐量；按交互次数衡量，LLM引导更高效且能发现输入门控故障。自动推导启动输入带来最大实际收益，行覆盖率对崩溃发现的预测能力弱。

Conclusion: 自动化TUI测试是可行的但远未解决，诚实的基线与基准比模型选择更重要。作者发布了工具tuicov和测试框架tuibot。

Abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates. Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Everyone Conforms, No One Believes: Pluralistic Ignorance in LLM Agent Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02758)
*Yashwanth YS*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM多智能体系统能否模拟"多元无知"（pluralistic ignorance）这一社会现象，发现该现象在LLM智能体群体中稳健地涌现。基于100个场景和8个模型的基准测试，智能体公开遵从率达到64-94%，但"规范企业家"打破虚假共识的级联成功率极低（多数模型低于26%），表明LLM模拟可能系统性高估社会规范的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统被广泛用于模拟社会动态，但尚不清楚它们能否捕捉"多元无知"现象——即多数人私下反对某规范却公开遵从，且各自认为自己是唯一持异议者。这一现象驱动规范持续、社会运动和政治革命，因此验证LLM模拟能否复现它对于评估模拟的真实性至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了涵盖10个领域、5个权力层级、共100个场景的基准测试集，基于人类多元无知文献设计提示词；评估了来自6个组织的8个模型，测量智能体的公开遵从率与私下反对率；测试单一"规范企业家"公开异议能否触发级联打破虚假共识；并进行提示词组件消融实验，以区分遵从行为是涌现的还是由提示词驱动的。

Result: 智能体公开遵从率高达64-94%；遵从行为具有领域敏感性（职场和社会关系场景几乎普遍遵从）且高度依赖模型，但与模型能力不相关；7/8个模型的级联成功率低于26%，一个模型在所有场景中零级联，GPT-4o例外为48%；消融实验显示即使去掉虚假共识框架和融入目标，遵从率仍达52-92%，证明遵从是涌现行为而非指令驱动。

Conclusion: 模型选择是未获重视的自由度，从根本上影响模拟结果；LLM模拟可能系统性高估社会规范的稳定性，遗漏现实中驱动规范变迁的脆弱临界点动态，这对基于LLM代理进行社会模拟的研究构成重要警示。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly used to simulate social dynamics, from opinion formation to collective decision-making. These simulations can reproduce certain social phenomena, but it is unknown whether they capture pluralistic ignorance, a state where a majority privately rejects a norm yet publicly conforms, each believing they are alone in dissenting. This phenomenon drives norm persistence, social movements, and political revolutions. We show that pluralistic ignorance emerges robustly in LLM agent populations. We construct a benchmark of 100 scenarios across 10 domains and 5 authority levels, grounded in the human pluralistic ignorance literature, and evaluate 8 models from 6 organizations. Agents publicly conform at rates of 64 to 94% despite privately opposing the norm. Conformity is domain-sensitive (workplace and social relationship scenarios produce near-universal compliance) and highly model-dependent, though uncorrelated with capability. We test whether a single "norm entrepreneur" can break the false consensus by publicly dissenting. For 7 of 8 models, cascades succeed less than 26% of the time, with one model showing zero cascades across all scenarios. GPT-4o is a notable outlier at 48%, revealing qualitatively distinct dynamics across model families. A prompt component ablation across all 8 models establishes that conformity is emergent rather than instruction-driven: removing both the false-consensus framing and fit-in goal reduces conformity but does not eliminate it (52 to 92% in the minimal condition). Our findings identify model selection as an unacknowledged degree of freedom that fundamentally shapes simulation outcomes. More broadly, the near-absence of cascades suggests LLM simulations may systematically overestimate the stability of social norms, missing the fragile tipping-point dynamics that drive real-world norm change in human societies.

</details>


### [250] [Stateful Governance for Concurrent Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02764)
*Yuxiang Peng,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究并发智能体系统中的有状态治理问题，提出了策略状态可串行化（policy-state serializability）这一正确性条件，并设计了Provenact运行时架构——将策略保持为可审查的程序，同时协调状态与效应来维护策略决策的时效性。实验表明Provenact能有效防止基线方法遗漏的过期授权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正从建议性界面转向执行退款、库存预留、云资源调配和金融转账等关键操作。现有安全机制仅在请求时基于现有信息决定操作是否被允许，但对于有状态策略，请求时的视图可能不完整——预算、库存、审批状态和风险信号在效果真正发生前可能已变化，导致早先的授权或审批失效，即"过期授权"（stale authorization）问题。

Method: 作者首先定义了策略状态可串行化作为正确性条件，要求已提交的效果必须针对其发生前的策略状态可解释为已授权。随后提出Provenact运行时架构，该架构将策略作为可审查的程序运行，同时协调维护策略决策所需的状态和效应。系统基于PostgreSQL构建原型进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明Provenact能够防止基线方法（将策略状态作为普通请求上下文传递）遗漏的过期授权；在无关工作并行进行时能保留延迟审批；策略演进化主要体现于策略文本而非可信提供者代码；在脚本化、无LLM的采购工作流中避免了基线产生的共享预算和库存上的授权违规。

Conclusion: Provenact为将状态治理边界整合到智能体框架和提供者支持的共享资源领域提供了可行路径，是实现并发智能体系统安全治理的重要一步。

Abstract: AI agents are moving from advisory interfaces into systems that execute consequential operations: issuing refunds, reserving scarce inventory, provisioning cloud resources, and initiating financial transfers. These workflows require governance over effects, not only over model outputs. Existing safeguards often decide whether an action is allowed from the information available when the action is requested. For stateful policies, that request-time view may be incomplete: budgets, inventory, approval status, and risk signals can change before the effect occurs, making an earlier authorization or approval stale.
  This paper studies stateful governance for concurrent agentic systems. We identify stale authorization as the core failure mode and define policy-state serializability, a correctness condition requiring committed effects to be explainable as authorized against the policy state immediately before they occur. We present Provenact, a runtime architecture that keeps policies as reviewable programs while coordinating the state and effects needed to preserve their decisions. In experiments with a PostgreSQL-backed prototype of Provenact, the system prevents stale authorizations missed by baselines that pass policy state as ordinary request context, preserves delayed approvals while unrelated work proceeds, keeps policy evolution mostly in policy text rather than trusted provider code, and avoids policy violations in a scripted, LLM-free procurement workflow where agent-governance baselines produce stale authorizations over shared budgets and inventory. More broadly, Provenact suggests a path for integrating stateful governance boundaries into agent frameworks and provider-backed domains where agents act on shared resources.

</details>


### [251] [Emergence of Biased Consensus in Multi-Agent LLM Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02827)
*Maya Okawa*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体LLM辩论中集体偏见（集体规范）的涌现机制，提出基于物理社会动力学模型的分析框架，预测并实验验证了当从众性超过临界阈值时会发生向集体偏见的相变，并发现噪声是关键驱动因素，而智能体异质性可抑制该涌现现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM辩论在决策和问题解决任务上表现优异，但其安全性和公平性风险尚未被充分理解。交互可能放大单个LLM的偏见，这对实际部署构成担忧，因此需要深入理解集体偏见如何在多智能体辩论中涌现。

Method: 作者借鉴物理学中社会动力学模型，提出一个分析框架来解释多智能体LLM辩论中集体偏见的涌现。该框架预测：当从众性超过某个临界阈值（给定LLM初始偏见和辩论噪声条件）时，系统会发生向集体偏见的相变。作者通过控制实验验证理论预测，观察到与底层相变一致的有限尺寸交叉现象，并在投资决策和LLM-as-judge评估等实际任务中验证了结论的泛化性。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）噪声（如LLM采样温度）是驱动集体偏见涌现的关键因素；2）观察到与相变理论预测一致的有限尺寸交叉；3）智能体异质性通过平滑化相变来抑制集体偏见的涌现；4）研究发现可推广至现实的决策任务，包括投资决策和LLM-as-judge评估。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM辩论中的集体偏见并非偶然现象，而是可以通过物理相变理论进行预测和解释的系统性行为。理解这一机制有助于设计更安全、更公平的多智能体LLM系统。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM debates achieve strong performance on decision-making tasks as well as problem-solving benchmarks, yet their safety and fairness risks remain poorly understood. Notably, interaction can amplify the biases of single LLMs, raising concerns for real-world deployment. We identify the emergence of collective (often biased) norms in multi-agent LLM debates and show that noise (e.g., LLM sampling temperature) is a key driver. To explain this, we propose an analytical framework drawing on physics-inspired theoretical models of social dynamics. We predict a phase transition to collective bias when conformity surpasses a critical threshold given the LLMs' initial bias and debate noise. We test the theoretical predictions through controlled experiments and observe a finite-size crossover consistent with an underlying phase transition. We further find that agent heterogeneity suppresses emergence by smoothing (rounding) this transition. Finally, we show that these insights generalize to realistic decision-making tasks, including investment decisions and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation.

</details>


### [252] [SABRE: A Multi-Agent Approach for Selecting Out-of-Distribution Detectors Under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02959)
*Mary Wisell,Salimeh Sekeh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出SABRE框架，用三个语言模型智能体在推理时按域动态选择最佳的post-hoc OOD检测器，解决了单一检测器在不同域间不可靠（甚至反转）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有post-hoc OOD检测假设在benchmark上选定的检测器在部署后仍然可靠，但实验表明在单个冻结编码器上，一个检测器在不同域间表现会反转（将分布内输入误判为异常），且最佳检测器随域而变，不存在固定可靠的选择。

Method: SABRE框架包含三个语言模型智能体在有限查询预算下协作：Selector（选择下一个要咨询的检测器）、Reporter（整合每个输入的证据）、Analyst（在部署域中留出的小标注样本上校准检测器可靠性，且不接触测试输入的标签）。此外提出了四个多模态密度检测器加入检测器库。

Result: SABRE能自动追踪每个域中最强的检测器而无需先验知识，在传统检测器反转的情况下恢复可靠检测，在检测器有效时收敛到该检测器。组件分析显示智能体互补：Reporter的反馈带来持续增益，Analyst的校准对防止反转至关重要。

Conclusion: 由于没有固定规则可以跨域可靠，可靠性必须在部署时建立而非从benchmark假设。SABRE证明了这一过程可以自动化实现。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for vision-language models assumes that a detector chosen on a benchmark stays reliable once deployed. We show this fails across domains: on a single frozen encoder, a detector that leads in one domain can invert in another, scoring in-distribution inputs as more anomalous than genuine outliers, and the best detector changes from domain to domain, so no fixed choice is reliable throughout. We introduce SABRE (Selective Agentic Budgeted Reliability Ensemble,) which replaces this fixed choice with per-regime selection at inference. Three language-model agents reason over a library of post-hoc detectors under a bounded query budget: a Selector chooses which detector to consult next, a Reporter consolidates the evidence for each input, and an Analyst calibrates detector reliability on a small labeled sample held out from the deployment domain and disjoint from the test data, weighting selection and aggregation without ever observing a scored input's label. The library includes four multimodal density detectors we propose. Inferring the operating regime from data, SABRE tracks the strongest detector in each domain without prior knowledge of it, recovering reliable detection where a conventional detector inverts and converging to that detector where it is sound. A component analysis shows the agents are complementary: the Reporter's feedback yields consistent gains, and the Analyst's calibration is decisive against inversion, ruling out unreliable detectors so that aggregation no longer cancels the sound ones. Since no fixed rule can be trusted across domains, reliability must be established at deployment rather than assumed from a benchmark, and SABRE shows this can be done automatically.

</details>


### [253] [When Truth Is Distributed: Misinformation Derails Collective Fact Recovery in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03421)
*Chenfei Yan,Zeyang Yue,Feifei Zhao,Erliang Lin,Lu Jia,Haibo Tong,Mingyang Lyu,Chengyi Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-Agreement框架，通过受控实验揭示基于LLM的多智能体系统中虚假证据如何通过通信被采纳并持续传播，导致集体事实恢复能力大幅下降（从72.50%降至14.17%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终结果，忽视了LLM多智能体系统在分布式信息聚合过程中的可靠性和传播动态，尤其是在通信中局部错误如何被放大为集体风险这一关键问题。

Method: 引入Hi-Agreement受控评估框架：将全体诚实协作与关键证据持有者受控欺骗进行严格配对，通过多阶段投票、证词采纳和证据根源谱系传播分析聚合过程；使用120个五智能体物体移动环境（部分观测共同决定唯一终点），评估3个同质LLM多智能体系统。

Result: 在配对条件下，集体事实恢复率从72.50%骤降至14.17%，每个系统均有显著下降。过程追踪和退出消融实验表明：单一虚假证词比真实证词更易被采纳，传播到更高阶，并在欺骗者退出后仍通过诚实智能体持续存在。无第一手证据的观察者能抑制错误共识但无法提升事实恢复。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了分布式事实恢复的脆弱性及其底层机制：虚假证据在进入通信后通过其他智能体的采纳和持续传播获得集体影响力，这对LLM多智能体系统的可靠性设计提出重要警示。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems promise effective collaborative reasoning, but communication may amplify local errors into collective risks. Existing evaluations emphasize final outcomes, leaving the reliability and propagation dynamics of distributed information aggregation unclear. We introduce Hi-Agreement, a controlled evaluation framework that strictly pairs all-honest collaboration with controlled deception by a key evidence holder and analyzes the aggregation process through multi-stage voting, testimony adoption, and evidence-root lineage propagation. Using 120 five-agent object-movement environments where partial observations jointly determine a unique endpoint, we evaluate 3 homogeneous LLM-based multi-agent systems. Across these paired conditions, aggregate truth recovery falls from 72.50% to 14.17%, with significant declines for every system. Process tracing and exit ablations show that a single false testimony is adopted more readily than truthful testimony, propagates to higher orders, and persists through honest agents after the deceiver exits. Observers without first-hand evidence suppress incorrect consensus but do not improve truth recovery. Together, these findings reveal both the fragility of distributed fact recovery and its underlying mechanism: false evidence gains collective influence through its adoption and continued propagation by other agents after entering communication.

</details>


### [254] [Group Perspective Matters: Regulating Debate Relationships Can Mitigate Blind Conformity in Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03648)
*Hao Wu,Shoucheng Song,Chang Yao,Haoyu Wang,Huaiyu Wan,Youfang Lin,Kai Lv*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出DEAR框架，从群体交互视角动态调控大语言模型多智能体辩论中的"辩论关系"，以缓解盲目趋同问题。DEAR通过量化解与分歧作为群体证据，并利用两个RL-Agent（选择RL-Agent和行为RL-Agent）通过多智能体强化学习联合优化，实现动态选择参考同伴和自适应调整生成行为，在提升推理性能的同时显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（MAD）能提升LLM推理性能，但LLM容易产生盲目趋同。现有的个体评估方法（如基于置信度或困惑度）无法反映推理的正确性，甚至可能加剧盲目趋同问题。因此需要从群体交互视角对辩论关系进行调控。

Method: 提出DEAR框架：1) 量化解（consensus）与分歧（divergence）作为群体证据以捕捉辩论状态；2) 通过三个阶段运作——"是什么"（感知群体咨询趋势和不确定性）、"谁"（选择RL-Agent动态选择参考同伴）、"如何"（行为RL-Agent自适应调整生成行为）；3) 将两个RL-Agent的执行建模为序贯决策过程，通过多智能体强化学习联合优化。

Result: 大量实验表明DEAR取得了优越的性能，同时显著减少了token消耗。

Conclusion: 从个体评估转向群体交互调控是缓解多智能体辩论中盲目趋同问题的有效途径，DEAR通过动态调控辩论关系在提升推理性能的同时提高了资源效率。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) improves the reasoning performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) through multi-round interaction. However, LLMs in MAD are highly susceptible to blind conformity. Existing individual evaluation methods, typically based on confidence or perplexity, fail to reflect the correctness of reasoning and may even exacerbate blind conformity. To address this, we shift the perspective from individual evaluation to group interaction. We define mutual referencing among LLMs as \textbf{Debate Relationships} and recognize that regulating these relationships is the key to mitigating blind conformity. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for \textbf{D}ynamically r\textbf{E}gulating deb\textbf{A}te \textbf{R}elationships (DEAR) from the group perspective. At first, DEAR quantifies consensus and divergence as \textit{group evidence} to capture the debate state. Then, DEAR operates through three stages: 1) What: perceiving group consultation tendency and uncertainty; 2) Who: introducing a Selection RL-Agent to dynamically select reference peers; and 3) How: adopting a Behavior RL-Agent to adaptively adjust generation behaviors. Notably, we formulate the execution of the two RL-Agents as a sequential decision-making process, jointly optimizing via multi-agent reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DEAR achieves superior performance while significantly reducing token consumption.

</details>


### [255] [An Actionable Diagnosis of Multilingual, Multi-Agent Planning Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03735)
*Vikas Pahuja,Jonathan Brokman,Omer Hofman,Tamir Nizri,Daniel Vishna,Seraphina Goldfarb-Tarrant,Kelly Marchisio,Hisashi Kojima,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言多智能体系统中规划器（planner）作为请求到行动接口的性能退化问题，提出了规划-接地失败（planning-grounding failures）的可操作分类体系，并基于此设计了TART方法，在多个语言、多个LLM骨干、两个数据集和两种智能体配置下一致提升性能，在GAIA基准上平均提升5.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言多智能体系统在非英语场景下性能显著退化，但以往研究很少追踪用户请求转化为可执行计划时关键信息是如何丢失的。作者希望从规划器这一"请求到行动"的接口入手，揭示失败的根本原因并提出可操作的缓解方案。

Method: 首先从真实失败的任务执行中提炼出规划-接地失败的分类体系（taxonomy），并通过LLM分析验证这些失败在低资源语言中占比更高。随后提出TART（Taxonomy-Guided Actionable Representation），将分类体系的关键方面显式地传递给规划器和下游子智能体，帮助其避免或纠正这些失败模式。

Result: 在多个语言、三个LLM骨干、两个数据集和两种智能体配置下，TART一致地提升了性能。在多语言GAIA基准上，该方法使最先进系统在涵盖低资源到高资源的11种语言上的准确率平均提升了5.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 规划-接地失败是跨语言多智能体系统退化的关键因素，尤其在低资源语言中表现最为突出。引入分类体系引导的表示可以使规划器和子智能体显式感知这些失败模式，从而有效缓解跨语言性能退化问题。

Abstract: Multilingual multi-agent systems exhibit substantial degradation beyond English, yet prior work rarely identifies how task-critical information is lost when user requests are converted into executable plans. We study the planner in a multi-agent system as the request-to-action interface and derive an actionable taxonomy of planning-grounding failures from failed real-world task executions. LLM-based analysis shows that these failures constitute an increasing share of unsuccessful executions as language-resource availability declines, with the strongest effects in low-resource languages. To test whether the taxonomy supports mitigation, we introduce TART, Taxonomy-Guided Actionable Representation, that makes the taxonomy's key aspects explicit to the planner and downstream sub-agents. Across multiple languages, three LLM backbones, two datasets, and two agentic configurations, TART consistently improves performance. On multilingual GAIA, it raises a state-of-the-art system's accuracy by 5.6 percentage points averaged across eleven languages spanning low- to high-resource settings.

</details>


### [256] [History Matters: Meta-policy Delegation with Heterogeneous Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03833)
*Ziqing Lu,Avinash Reddy Mudireddy,Sarra Alqahtani,Weiyu Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究异构多智能体系统（MAS）中的任务委派问题，提出基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的委派训练方法，并引入两种新框架：一是策略依赖交互历史的MARL框架以改善协调性，二是多维度货币机制以促进MAS中的协作与委派。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在未来决策系统中愈发重要，异构MAS成员具有不同能力和运营成本，在资源受限场景下需要有效的任务委派机制，以最小化总执行成本并实现高效协作。

Method: 1) 提出基于MARL的委派训练方法，使智能体在最小化总执行成本的同时做出序贯委派决策；2) 将该方法扩展至具有预设委派拓扑结构的MARL；3) 提出第一个框架：智能体策略同时依赖当前MDP状态和交互历史（包括先前联合动作），即使在全可观测环境中也能改善协调；4) 提出第二个框架：新颖的多维度货币机制促进MAS协作与委派。

Result: 通过历史依赖策略框架克服了传统MARL方法仅依赖当前状态的局限，提升了协调能力；多维度货币机制为MAS协作提供了新的激励手段。

Conclusion: 本文为异构多智能体系统在资源受限场景下的任务委派提供了系统的MARL解决方案，并通过两种创新的理论框架（历史依赖策略与多维货币机制）进一步拓展了协作与委派的可行性和效率。

Abstract: AI agents are expected to play an increasingly important role in future decision-making systems. In this paper, we consider collaborative systems composed of heterogeneous multi-agent systems (MAS), where their members have different capabilities and operating costs. We study how agents can delegate tasks to one another so that certain research tasks can be completed effectively under resource-constrained scenarios. We first develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning-based (MARL) delegation training that enables agents to make sequential delegation decisions while minimizing the total execution cost. We then extend this approach to MARL with prescribed delegation topologies. Furthermore, we introduce two new frameworks for collaboration and delegation in multi-agent systems. The first framework proposes that an agent's policy depends not only on the current state of the underlying Markov decision process but also on the interaction history, including previous joint actions. This history-dependent formulation can improve coordination even in fully observable environments, where conventional MARL methods typically restrict policies to depend only on the current state. The second framework proposes a novel, potentially multi-dimensional monetary mechanism to facilitate the collaboration and delegation for MAS.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Hybrid LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agents for Complex Sequential Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03502)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé Ulrich Gaba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LLM规划能力与RL动作优化相结合的混合智能体架构，用于解决长时程顺序决策任务，实验表明该方法在样本效率、成功率和动作连贯性上均优于单独的RL或LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程顺序决策任务上表现不佳，缺乏精确的动作优化能力；而RL方法虽然擅长顺序控制，但缺乏高层抽象和任务分解能力。两者互补，需要一种整合方案。

Method: 构建LLM增强的强化学习智能体架构：LLM负责生成子目标、结构化计划和上下文指导，RL智能体通过与环境的交互来优化底层动作执行。

Result: 在顺序决策任务上，相比纯RL和纯LLM基线，该方法实现了更高的样本效率、更高的成功率以及更连贯的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: LLM与RL的混合范式是构建更强大自主系统的有前景方向，能够兼顾高层规划与底层优化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool-use, enabling new forms of autonomous agents. However, LLM-based agents struggle with long-horizon sequential decision tasks that require precise action optimization and environment interaction. Reinforcement Learning (RL), while effective for sequential control, often lacks the high-level abstraction and task decomposition abilities needed for complex scenarios. This paper introduces an LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent that integrates LLM-driven planning with RL-based action optimization. The proposed architecture leverages the LLM to generate subgoals, structured plans, and contextual guidance, while the RL agent refines low-level actions through interaction with the environment. Experiments on sequential decision tasks demonstrate improved sample efficiency, higher success rates, and more coherent action trajectories compared to RL-only and LLM-only baselines. This hybrid paradigm highlights a promising direction for building more capable autonomous systems.

</details>


### [258] [Adversarial Fast-Moving Real-World Domains as Test Beds for Benchmarking AI Scientist Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03569)
*William Bolton,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在真实世界对抗性快速变化的领域（F1赛车和万智牌）中评估AI科学家产生新颖想法能力的基准框架，研究发现模型能产生看似合理的想法，但只有少数与真实专家方案对齐，表明AI科学家的关键能力缺口不在于想法生成，而在于筛选、优先排序和连贯新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家基准多依赖合成任务或回顾性目标，可能受到先前知识暴露的干扰，难以真正评估模型的创新想法生成能力，需要更真实、更复杂的评估框架。

Method: 在F1领域，让模型针对2026赛季汽车设计概念进行构思，以真实季前创新作为基准；在万智牌领域，让模型从近期更新的卡池中提出卡组，与19个职业巡回赛卡组进行对比评估。

Result: F1中最佳模型GPT-5.2在166个想法中匹配了40个真实创新中的10个；MTG中Gemini 3 Flash的最佳卡组恢复了第三名PT卡组中7张新卡中的5张，且模型最常选择的卡与PT卡组广泛采用的卡显著相关（Spearman ρ=0.74, p=0.0003）。

Conclusion: AI科学家在生成想法方面表现尚可，但在筛选、优先排序和形成连贯新颖方案方面存在显著不足，这一能力差距是未来AI科学家发展的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Benchmarking the ability of AI scientists to generate novel ideas is notoriously difficult. Existing benchmarks in this field have made progress in evaluating scientific reasoning and research replication, but often rely on synthetic tasks or retrospective targets, which may be confounded by prior exposure. We hypothesize that complex, adversarial, fast-moving real-world domains where expert practitioners independently generate observable outputs can provide a practical solution to fill this gap and evaluate the capabilities needed for AI scientists, including reasoning, novelty, and hypothesis formulation. We instantiate this framework in two structurally different domains, Formula 1 (F1), where models ideate around car design concepts for the 2026 season, and real pre-season innovations provide a ground truth, and Magic: The Gathering (MTG), where models propose decks from a recently updated card pool and are evaluated against 19 Pro Tour (PT) decklists. Across both domains, models produce plausible outputs, but few align with real-world expert solutions. In F1, the best model, GPT-5.2 matched 10 of 40 real innovations with 166 ideas proposed across runs. In MTG, the best deck from Gemini 3 Flash recovered 5 of 7 new-set cards from the third-place PT deck, and across all 108 decks, the cards models selected most often were also the cards most widely adopted by PT decks (Spearman $ρ= 0.74$, $p = 0.0003$). These results suggest that a key capability gap for AI scientists is not idea generation, but filtering, prioritization, and coherent novelty.

</details>


### [259] [Is Inter-Seed Cross-Play Enough? Evaluating the Robustness of Zero-Shot Coordination Algorithms to Implementation Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03644)
*Maksymilian Wolski,Nicholas Hoernle,Johannes Forkel,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估零样本协调（ZSC）算法对实现细节差异的鲁棒性，提出了"跨实现交叉博弈"（cross-implementation cross-play）评估方案，并对流行的Other-Play算法进行了评估。结果表明，标准ZSC评估方案在实践中可以作为更彻底的跨实现评估的合理代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本协调算法在实际应用中的评估存在局限：现有评估几乎只用单一实现配合不同随机种子进行测试，忽略了独立团队在解释和实现同一算法规格时可能产生的差异，这引发了对算法对规格歧义和实现细节鲁棒性的疑问。

Method: 提出了一种新的评估方案——跨实现交叉博弈（cross-implementation cross-play），通过变化此前研究中已被证明会影响多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法性能的实现细节来评估算法，并采用该方案对流行的ZSC算法Other-Play进行了系统评估。

Result: 评估结果令人鼓舞：对于Other-Play算法，标准的ZSC评估在事实上可以作为更彻底的跨实现评估的合理代理，表明该算法对实现差异具有较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了ZSC算法鲁棒性评估领域的空白，为后续ZSC算法的评估方法提供了新的参考框架，也为算法设计者在实现和部署ZSC算法时提供了关于评估可靠性的重要见解。

Abstract: AI agents deployed in real-world settings must be capable of coordinating with humans and other AI agents they have not encountered before. Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) algorithms aim to achieve this by specifying high-level learning rules such that independently engineered agents can coordinate with each other at test time. Rigorous evaluation of ZSC algorithms remains difficult: ideally, multiple independent implementations of each proposed algorithm must be used, reflecting the variation that arises when independent parties interpret and implement the same specification. In practice, however, ZSC algorithms have almost exclusively been evaluated using a single implementation trained across different random seeds, with only a handful of works additionally varying the neural network architecture. This leaves open questions about robustness to specification ambiguities and implementation details. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of this robustness. We introduce a new evaluation scheme, cross-implementation cross-play, varying implementation details that prior work has shown to affect the performance of multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms, and we evaluate Other-Play, a popular ZSC algorithm, with this scheme. Our findings are encouraging and suggest that, for Other-Play, the standard ZSC evaluation is, in fact, a reasonable proxy for this more thorough cross-implementation evaluation.

</details>


### [260] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）重新定位为社会性协调问题，而非单纯输出多样化问题。作者借助社会学理论，将视角理解为由角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并在权力和专业领域中分布，进而将其转化为具体AI系统设计启示，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调以及情境敏感的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署到日益多样化的社会情境中，对齐不能再被视为对单一统一价值体系的优化。然而当前多元对齐方法缺乏对价值观如何在社会中被组织、争论和协调的清晰说明，需要社会学理论提供概念和设计资源来填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用理论论证方法：借鉴社会学传统，从三个维度分析视角的社会性结构——角色决定的视角结构化、互动塑造的视角形成、权力与专业领域中的视角分布；并将这些洞见转化为AI系统设计的实际启示，包括基于角色的表征、结构化协调机制和情境敏感评估框架。

Result: 论文提出了一套多元对齐的设计空间，涵盖角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环等关键环节；将多元对齐从输出多样化转向社会性协调，并为未来在真实世界中实施和实证评估这些方法指明了方向。

Conclusion: 多元对齐本质上是一个需要社会理论支撑的系统设计问题，核心挑战在于如何在结构化且可问责的方式下协调多种合法视角。未来需要在现实环境中验证这些设计方案的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [261] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent，一种基于工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）的框架，用于LLM智能体的动态工具规划与执行，通过模式级别的工具关系建模和欠账导向的扩展策略，在AppWorld基准上提升了任务完成性能并减少了冗余API调用和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体依赖LLM从文本描述推断工具组合，存在隐式推理局限性和对动态环境的适应性不足，导致在复杂任务中探索效率低下、执行不可靠。

Method: 1) 在模式级别建模工具关系，构建有向工具-模式超图（工具作为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边）；2) 提取任务相关的工具上下文图，构建模式感知的任务DAG；3) 执行时通过欠账导向扩展构建状态条件化的工具支持图，识别未解决需求并检索支持的生产者工具。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent相比现有智能体基线提高了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: 基于模式级超图结构引导的动态规划与执行策略能够有效提升LLM智能体在复杂工具使用任务中的可靠性和效率，为工具规划提供了一种结构化的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [262] [TraceCAD: Trace-Guided Repair for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03062)
*Fengxiao Fan,Jingzhe Ni,Fan Sang,Xiaolong Yin,Yu Liu,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceCAD是一个面向LLM-based CAD代理的恢复层，通过持久化状态关联需求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果，实现局部化搜索与可复用技能记忆，从而提升CAD程序修复的几何质量和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based CAD代理在修正循环过程中会丢失关于已满足需求、故障操作和先前修复的证据，导致修复效率和质量下降。

Method: 提出TraceCAD恢复层，包含四个关键组件：1) 将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果关联为持久状态；2) 诊断可能出错的建模操作；3) 在依赖区域内进行有界编辑搜索，并通过执行和保留检查验证候选方案；4) 将成功和失败的修复结果存入可重用的技能记忆库。

Result: 在DeepCAD基准上，通过200模型消融实验和1000模型对比实验，TraceCAD在IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离上取得有竞争力的几何质量。移除持久状态导致恢复分数几乎减半；移除局部化搜索导致几何回归翻倍且代码代理调用翻倍；使用不相交训练模型初始化技能库进一步减少了重试次数、token成本和延迟。

Conclusion: 持久化、局部化和可复用的恢复机制能够显著提升最终CAD质量和修复可靠性，验证了TraceCAD作为恢复层的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based CAD agents produce executable parametric programs, but their correction loops may lose evidence about satisfied requirements, faulty operations, and prior repairs. We introduce TraceCAD, a recovery layer that links requested features, modeling steps, failure evidence, and candidate outcomes as persistent state. TraceCAD diagnoses likely faulty operations, searches bounded edits in their dependency regions, validates candidates through execution and preservation checks, and retains successful and failed repair outcomes in reusable skill memory. On DeepCAD-derived benchmarks with 200-model ablations and a 1K-model comparison, TraceCAD achieves competitive geometric quality in terms of IoU, Chamfer distance, and Hausdorff distance. Removing persistent state nearly halves recovery score; removing localized search more than doubles geometric regression and doubles code-agent invocations. Initializing the skill store on disjoint training models further reduces retries, token cost, and latency. These results demonstrate that persistent, localized, and reusable recovery improves final CAD quality and repair reliability.

</details>


### [263] [LiveEvalBench: Toward Open-World Evaluation for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03689)
*Yiyao Wang,Zhen Wen,Yinghao Tang,Yixiao Fu,Lin Yuan,Xiaolau Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveEvalBench是一个将前端项目生成评估从静态问题重构为智能体化、自适应、可扩展流程的自动化框架。它通过Build Engineer、Code Engineer和UI Tester三个角色的协作审查工作流，结合共享评分标准与实现特化标准的自适应协议，对网页生成进行全面评估，并与人类专家判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将网页生成视为静态评估问题，但前端工件本质上是交互式的、允许多样化有效实现，且迭代速度快于刚性评估管线。现有方法无法适应这些特性，需要新的评估范式。

Method: LiveEvalBench将评估实例化为协作审查工作流，由Build Engineer、Code Engineer和UI Tester三个角色在全生命周期（部署、代码检查、浏览器交互）中收集证据。采用自适应协议，将共享评分标准（用于跨模型可比性）与实现特化标准（针对每个工件定制）相结合，并支持增量集成新的评估角色和评估维度。

Result: 在多样的真实网页生成场景中，LiveEvalBench与人类专家判断高度一致，并为前沿模型的网页生成能力提供了细粒度的洞察。

Conclusion: LiveEvalBench通过将网页生成评估重塑为智能体化、自适应和可扩展的过程，为前端项目评估提供了一个更贴合实际需求的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable of synthesizing executable frontend projects, yet existing benchmarks still treat web generation as a static evaluation problem. We argue that frontend artifacts demand a different paradigm: they are interactive rather than static, admit diverse yet equally valid implementations, and evolve faster than rigid pipelines can accommodate. To address these gaps, we present LiveEvalBench, an automated framework that reformulates web-generation evaluation as an agentic, adaptive, and extensible process. LiveEvalBench instantiates evaluation as a collaborative review workflow, in which a Build Engineer, a Code Engineer, and a UI Tester collectively gather evidence across the full lifecycle of a frontend project, from deployment and code inspection to browser-based interaction. To handle implementation diversity, an adaptive protocol couples shared rubrics for cross-model comparability with implementation-grounded criteria tailored to each artifact. The framework further supports incremental integration of new evaluator roles and assessment dimensions without pipeline redesign. Experiments across diverse real-world web-generation scenarios show that LiveEvalBench aligns closely with human expert judgment and provides fine-grained insights into frontier models' web generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wyysteelhead/LiveEvalBench

</details>


### [264] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型中存在的"人工蜂群思维"效应（即模型在开放问题上趋于同质化共识，响应间相似度高达0.80-0.90）提出了一种增加输出多样性的缓解框架：元人格锚定结合滤波温度缩放（FTS）。该方法采用两阶段生成过程，先在模型中选择独特人格作为起点，再通过Top-p过滤保持语法有效性和极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）探索扩展的概率分布。在约20B参数的开源模型上，平均余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著减少语义收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型即使在高温度采样下也会产生高度同质化的输出（平均相似度约0.80-0.90），这种"人工蜂群思维"效应导致AI输出的语义多样性受限，无法达到人类水平的类型学多样性，需要有效的框架来缓解这一模式坍塌问题。

Method: 提出元人格锚定（Meta-Persona Anchoring）与滤波温度缩放（Filtered Temperature Scaling, FTS）相结合的框架。两阶段生成：第一阶段让模型自选独特的人格来锚定生成起点；第二阶段采用双阶段采样筛，先用Top-p滤波保证语法有效性，再对幸存候选施加极端温度（T≥4.0）以探索拓宽后的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上，针对约20B参数的开源模型进行评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，多数问题的相似度降至0.7阈值以下，有效缩小了人工模式坍塌与人类类型学多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了语义收敛程度，提高了LLM输出的多样性，并已开源实现框架以支持更多样化和创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [265] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体和超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，发现实例空间中的逻辑复杂度并非均匀分布，而是由超平面组织，并在高维空间中呈现"复杂度局部化"现象，其中全对角线是唯一例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的原子概念学习研究通常假设实例空间在结构上是均匀的，但作者观察到r维超立方体并非结构均匀：超平面上的初等等价类数量有界，而全对角线上的类别数却无界增长。这种不对称性暗示了概念本身的归约结构，需要用几何-逻辑的视角重新理解复杂度的分布规律。

Method: 基于作者先前提出的高维框架，本文通过规范简单概念（canonical simple concepts）、最小排序（minimal orderings）和代表性归约（representative reductions）重新解读超平面行为。论文包含完整的二元情形推导、三元超立方体的显式处理，以及对归约机制的详细展开说明。三维情形已展现出正交族、部分对角线和例外全对角线等本质现象。

Result: 建立了高维中超平面行为的分类体系，证明复杂度在实例空间中局部化而非均匀分布。三维情形已能完整呈现正交族、部分对角线和全对角线等关键现象，为理解高元原子概念学习的复杂度集中位置提供了几何-逻辑框架。

Conclusion: 该几何-逻辑视角揭示了原子概念学习中复杂度集中的位置，并为约束假设空间和结构化分类提供了现代解释，表明复杂度不对称性反映了概念自身的归约结构本质。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [266] [Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Knowledge Graph Construction for Multi-Lecture Educational Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03161)
*Sahil Al Farib,Momota Ahsana Meem,Sheikh Redwanul Islam,Md. Tanvir Raihan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的多模态知识图谱构建流水线，用于从讲座视频中提取概念和关系。该流水线结合了转录、语义锚点选择、OCR和视觉语言模型，确保提取的内容均有证据支撑。在三个神经网络讲座上测试，保留了1,022个概念提及和312个关系提及，最终得到172个规范化概念和282个关系，端点覆盖率达90.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲座视频中的知识分布在语音、幻灯片文本、图表、公式和演示顺序等多个模态中，仅依赖转录文本进行检索无法完整保留这些信息，因此需要一种多模态方法来构建更丰富的知识表示。

Method: 提出一个多模态流水线：(1)转录讲座内容；(2)选择语义锚点；(3)应用光学字符识别(OCR)处理幻灯片；(4)使用视觉语言模型提取有证据支持的概念和类型化关系；(5)对提及进行验证和规范化，构建带有来源信息的知识图谱。

Result: 在三个神经网络讲座上，处理了3,118帧、756个转录片段和559个锚点。保留了1,022个概念提及和312个关系提及，得到172个规范化概念和282个关系，端点覆盖率达90.38%。初步三项问题检索测试实现了100%的top-1和top-3准确率及100%的top-5平均召回率。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于提供了一种可审计的知识图谱构建方法，而非追求最先进的性能指标，强调构建过程的证据可溯源性和透明性。

Abstract: Lecture videos distribute knowledge across speech, slide text, diagrams, equations, and presentation order, which transcript-only retrieval does not fully preserve. This paper presents an evidence-grounded multimodal pipeline that transcribes lectures, selects semantic anchors, applies optical character recognition (OCR), and uses a vision-language model to extract only concepts and typed relationships supported by transcript, OCR, or visual evidence. Mentions are validated and canonicalized into a provenance-rich knowledge graph. On three neural-network lectures, the pipeline processed 3,118 frames, 756 transcript segments, and 559 anchors. It retained 1,022 concept and 312 relationship mentions, yielding 172 canonical concepts and 282 relationships with 90.38% endpoint coverage. A preliminary three question retrieval test achieved 100% top-1 and top-3 accuracy and 100% mean top-5 recall. The contribution is an auditable construction method rather than a state-of-the-art performance claim.

</details>


### [267] [Reachability Is Not Realization: Tracing the Sources of LLM Benchmark Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03219)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yanbo Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题级别的审计框架，区分LLM的"已实现"能力（默认部署流程产生正确答案）与"可达"能力（在固定预算内通过指定探针找到答案）。研究发现推理时层路由在匹配预算下随机路由即可匹敌结构化搜索，答案盲过程无法保留增益；静默单个MLP块可修复68-92%的失败；训练倾向于提升已实现性能而非扩展可达上限，因此能力扩展声称应同时报告两种指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试分数的提升常被等同于LLM能力的增强，但相同的得分可以反映截然不同的行为变化：模型可能真正学到新答案，也可能只是更稳定地输出原本就可达的答案。聚合分数无法在问题级别区分这些差异，这导致对模型能力提升的解读存在根本性模糊。

Method: 建立问题级别的审计框架：在固定预算、温度和答案格式下，区分"已实现"（默认部署流程产生正确答案）与"可达"（指定探针在预算内找到答案）。通过三种实验验证：1)推理时层路由对可达性的影响；2)静默特定MLP块对失败集的修复效果；3)训练前后可达性天花板与已实现性能的变化对比。

Result: 在43个模型和任务设置中，随机路由在匹配预算下匹配或超越结构化搜索；且增益依赖正确答案访问。静默一个已识别的MLP块修复68-92%的失败集。在六个匹配评估中有五个，训练提升已实现性能但可达天花板持平或下降；DAPO的部署分数上升14.7分而可达上限下降13.3分。

Conclusion: 已实现性与可达性并不总是同步变化。能力扩展的声称应同时报告匹配评估条件下的已实现性能和可达性，以更准确地刻画模型能力的真实变化。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often treated as evidence of greater LLM capability. Yet the same gain can reflect different changes in model behavior. A model may reach new answers, or produce answers that were already within reach. Aggregate scores do not distinguish these changes question by question. We establish a question-level audit under fixed budgets, temperatures, and answer formats. A question is realized when the default deployment procedure produces the correct answer. A question is reachable when a specified probe finds that answer within a fixed budget. We first test whether inference-time layer routing can expand reachability. Under a matched budget, random routes match or exceed structured search in all 43 model and task settings. Answer-blind procedures retain almost none of this gain, which instead requires access to the correct answer. We then ask why reachable answers sometimes fail to appear. Across six cases spanning 0.5B to 31B, silencing one identified MLP block repairs 68 to 92 percent of a predefined failure set. We next test whether training closes the gap by expanding reachability. In five of six matched evaluations, deployed performance rises while the reachable ceiling remains flat or falls. For DAPO, the deployed score rises by 14.7 points while the reachable ceiling falls by 13.3 points. Across the settings we audit, realization and reachability therefore do not always change together. Claims of capability expansion should report both realized performance and reachability under matched evaluation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/reachability-not-realization

</details>


### [268] [Distractor-Aware Truncation: Disentangling Context-Length Effects from Signal Loss in Long-Context LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03297)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了"较短上下文更好"这一文献中的标准论断。作者在BABILong和GraphWalks两个长上下文基准上，对四种上下文保留比例（100%/75%/50%/25%）和两种截断协议（朴素中段删除 vs. 干扰物感知删除）进行了系统测试，使用Claude家族三个规格和GPT-5.5验证。结果显示：朴素截断下性能单调崩溃，而干扰物感知截断下性能保持或提升。作者指出朴素协议测的并非上下文窗口效果，而是"中段删除恰好保留答案内容的概率"，并呼吁未来研究必须明确区分信号与干扰物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战检索增强和记忆增强语言模型文献中"较短上下文更好"的标准论断，指出以往该结论可能源于有缺陷的截断协议（朴素地从中间删除内容），而非真实的上下文长度效应。作者希望通过系统性对照实验揭示这一方法论缺陷。

Method: 在BABILong和GraphWalks（BFS）两个长上下文基准的每个样本上，以四种上下文保留比例（100%、75%、50%、25%）和两种截断协议（朴素中段删除协议 vs. 干扰物感知协议——仅删除与任务无关的内容）进行测试。模型覆盖Claude家族三规格（Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7）及跨供应商的GPT-5.5，另在MRCR v2和Oolong两个额外基准上应用相同协议。使用配对Wilcoxon检验（Holm校正）进行统计显著性验证。

Result: 在朴素截断下，所有八组BABILong和GraphWalks测试中性能单调崩溃（p_adj < 0.05）。在干扰物感知协议下，性能保持或提升：两个较小的Claude模型在BABILong上表现显著提升，较大的Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5则处于全上下文的性能上限。GPT-5.5上复现了一致结果，排除了单供应商假象。机制上，朴素协议在25%保留率时仅不到1%的样本保留了答案相关内容，而干扰物感知协议则按构造保证保留。

Conclusion: 朴素截断协议测的并非上下文窗口效应，而是"中段删除偶然保留答案"的概率。未来关于上下文长度效应的研究必须明确说明如何区分信号与干扰物，否则结果在"较短上下文更好"和"较长上下文更好"两种相反假设之间都是模糊的。

Abstract: A standard claim in the literature on retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented language models is that shorter context is better when the relevant information is preserved. We test this claim by running every sample of two long-context benchmarks -- BABILong and GraphWalks (BFS) -- at four context-retention fractions (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%) under two truncation protocols. The first is the naive protocol implicitly used in much prior work: drop content from the middle of the prompt. The second is distractor-aware: identify the task-relevant content for each sample and drop only the rest. We evaluate three sizes of the Claude family (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.7) and, to test cross-provider generality, GPT-5.5 from a different provider; we apply the same protocol to two further benchmarks (MRCR v2, Oolong). Under naive truncation, score collapses monotonically (paired Wilcoxon, Holm-corrected p_adj < 0.05 in all eight BABILong and GraphWalks cells). Under the distractor-aware protocol -- which preserves the signal by construction -- performance is preserved or improves: the two smaller Claude models show statistically significant gains on BABILong, while the larger models (Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) sit at their full-context ceiling. The naive collapse and its distractor-aware recovery replicate on GPT-5.5, ruling out a single-provider artifact. The mechanism is direct: under the naive protocol the answer-bearing content survives in fewer than 1% of samples at 25% retention; under the distractor-aware protocol it is preserved by construction. The naive protocol is therefore not a measurement of context-window effects; it is a measurement of how often middle-removal happens to spare the answer. We conclude that future studies of context-length effects must specify how they distinguish signal from distractor, or they are at best ambiguous between two opposite hypotheses.

</details>


### [269] [ChartAnno: Evaluating MLLMs for Chart Annotation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03464)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Lidan Tan,Xin Lin,Xingchen Zeng,Yi Shan,Ziyue Lin,Xiaoliang Fu,Xinyuan Liu,Yuetong Guo,Fen Wang,Bongshin Lee,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChartAnno基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图表注释生成任务上的能力。该基准包含1,200个真实世界图表及其配对代码和三种不同指令具体程度的注释指令，并评估了10个代表性MLLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图表理解、生成和编辑方面已有显著进展，但对现有图表进行注释的能力尚未被充分探索。图表注释是一项常见但具有挑战性的沟通任务，要求模型推断意图、理解图表语义并放置适当的文本或图形元素，因此需要专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 构建包含1,200个真实世界图表及配对代码和注释指令的ChartAnno基准，指令分为三种具体程度。在两种主要输入设置下评估10个代表性MLLM：（1）仅图表代码；（2）图表代码和图表图像，并额外进行仅图表图像的消融研究。

Result: 专有模型整体表现更强，但大规模开源模型正在缩小差距。更具体的指令能提高注释质量，而推断抽象意图对当前MLLM来说仍然最困难。提供图表图像带来的整体提升有限，改进主要出现在设计相关指标上。

Conclusion: 图表注释生成是一项需要语义接地和有效注释设计的挑战性任务，当前MLLM在该任务上仍有较大提升空间，特别是在抽象意图推断方面。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in chart understanding, generation, and editing, but their ability to annotate existing charts remains underexplored. Annotating charts is a common yet challenging communicative task, requiring models to infer intended messages, interpret chart semantics, and place appropriate textual or graphical elements. To address this gap, we introduce ChartAnno, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on chart annotation generation. It contains 1,200 real-world charts with paired code and annotation instructions across three levels of instruction specificity. We evaluate 10 representative MLLMs under two primary input settings: (1) chart code alone and (2) both chart code and chart image, and further include a chart image-only ablation study. Results show that proprietary models remain stronger overall, although large-scale open-source models narrow the gap. More specific instructions improve annotation quality, while inferring abstract intent remains most difficult for current MLLMs. Providing chart images brings limited overall gains, with improvements mainly appearing in design-related metrics. These findings highlight chart annotation generation as a challenging task requiring semantic grounding and effective annotation design. Code and data will be released in a future version.

</details>


### [270] [When Outputs Disperse, Does Epistemic Revision Follow? A Black-Box Coupling Diagnostic for Machine Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03722)
*Molood Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"离散度-修正耦合"这一诊断框架，用于检测LLM群体在干预下产生的输出多样性是否真正伴随认知立场修正，而非仅仅是表面上的表述重构。通过双通道测量（输出通道的Coherence Index和认知通道的逐轮立场标注），并引入Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS)配合Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP)作为评估方法，在两种模型配置上验证发现：GPT-4o-mini在条件性异议下虚假前提恢复提升+17.7点，而Gemini-2.5-flash同样干预无显著收益，且94%的post-RDP响应为重构而非让步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集体智慧研究将分歧视为认知多样性信号，但LLM群体中这一假设可能失效——模型能产生看似多样的论证却保持相同结论。需要一个能够区分"真实的立场修正"与"前提保留的表述重构"的诊断工具。

Method: 提出了双通道诊断框架：输出通道通过Coherence Index (CI)验证干预是否改变输出离散度；认知通道通过逐轮立场标注衡量群体是否真正修正立场。该框架是黑盒的，仅基于生成文本操作。在此基础上提出Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS)，在输出过度收敛时插入Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP)来估计耦合状态。在两种模型配置（gpt-4o-mini和gemini-2.5-flash）的五智能体群体上进行评估，每条件310对情节。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上，条件性异议使虚假前提恢复提升+17.7点（p<1e-6），而静态人格多样性反而损害恢复（-8.1, p=.007）。在gemini-2.5-flash上，同等预算下的相同干预无显著收益（26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84），尽管验证了离散度下降。两种处理效应存在显著差异（z=3.79, p<.001）。机制标注显示Gemini通过框架内异议保留虚假前提：94%的post-RDP响应是重构而非让步（GPT仅为24%）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM群体中"多样性幻觉"问题的存在及其模型间差异：某些模型（如Gemini）能产生看似多样化的输出但缺乏实质性的立场修正。建议在报告准确性时，同时报告逐干预立场偏移和前提保留率，以更全面地评估群体智慧干预措施的真实效果。

Abstract: Collective intelligence research treats disagreement as evidence of epistemic diversity: if agents express different views, the group should retain capacity to revise. In LLM collectives this proxy can break: agents can produce diverse-looking arguments while preserving the same conclusion. We operationalize dispersion-revision coupling: the degree to which an intervention that verifiably increases the dispersion of a collective's outputs in embedding space is accompanied by genuine revision of its epistemic stance rather than premise-preserving reformulation. The diagnostic is black-box: it operates on generated text alone and makes no claims about the internal representations of the generating models. Two channels are measured independently: an output channel, the Coherence Index (CI), verifies that the intervention changed output dispersion; an epistemic channel, per-turn stance annotation, measures whether the collective revised. We propose CI with the Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS), which inserts a Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP) when outputs over-converge, as a reusable method for estimating this coupling regime. We evaluate five-agent collectives from two configurations (gpt-4o-mini and gemini-2.5-flash; 310 paired episodes per condition). On gpt-4o-mini, conditional dissent improves false-premise recovery by +17.7 points (p<1e-6) while static persona diversity harms recovery (-8.1, p=.007). On gemini-2.5-flash, the same intervention at a comparable budget yields no gain (26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84) despite a verified dispersion drop; the two treatment effects differ from each other (z=3.79, p<.001). Mechanism tagging shows Gemini preserves the false premise via intra-framework dissent: 94% of tagged post-RDP responses reformulate rather than concede (vs 24% on GPT). We recommend reporting per-intervention stance shift and premise-preservation rate alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [271] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）中的思维链（CoT）忠实性与安全拒绝能力之间的对齐张力，提出了靶向推理替换（TRR）技术构建HazMart数据集，并通过表征引导方法在提升安全性的同时保持模型基础能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理为模型监控提供了窗口，但监控依赖于推理轨迹的忠实性（模型输出严格由推理产生）。然而模型既要忠实到能被监控，又要足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全的推理，这两者之间存在内在矛盾。当前LRMs中这种张力尚未被系统研究，且已有测试忠实性的方法依赖在提示中提供暗示，缺乏直接干预推理链的手段。

Method: 1）提出Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR)技术，直接替换推理链中的不安全或不合逻辑思维，而非依赖提示暗示；2）构建HazMart人类撰写的自主AI店长场景数据集；3）在DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B和QwQ-32B上进行测试；4）对QwQ-32B进行机制分析，揭示内部表征方向与冲突的关联；5）应用表征引导（representation steering）独立放大安全方向。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B表现出高忠实性（97.5%）但拒绝不安全推理能力低（12.3%）；QwQ-32B更鲁棒（73.9%安全性）但忠实性较低（74.7%）。机制分析发现这两个属性由在动作承诺token处达到峰值的反相关内部方向表征。表征引导可将安全行为提升9个百分点，同时保持基础能力。

Conclusion: LRMs中存在忠实性与安全性之间的对齐张力，这证实了"可监控但必须鲁棒"的矛盾在现有模型中确实存在。但通过机制层面的理解和表征引导干预，可以在一定程度上独立增强安全性而不牺牲模型基础能力，为解决这一张力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [272] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContinualSkillBench，一个用于上下文持续技能学习的动态评估框架，涵盖5个代表性领域、每个领域100个相互关联的子任务，实验发现顺序执行普遍提升性能但收益差异大，上下文学习与显式技能维护效果相当，而较弱模型倾向于积累更大且更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架通过外部技能库增强大语言模型解决复杂任务的能力，但目前尚不清楚这些系统能否有效进化其技能，以及进化后的技能是否真正提升任务解决能力。研究者希望弥合这一认知空白，系统性地评估上下文中持续技能学习的有效性。

Method: 引入ContinualSkillBench动态评估框架，覆盖五个代表性领域，每个领域包含100个按难度递增排列且具有跨任务技能复用机会的相互关联子任务。通过对比顺序执行与基线方法、上下文学习与显式技能维护在不同模型和领域的表现来评估技能进化机制。

Result: 顺序执行普遍提升性能但增益因模型和领域而异；上下文学习在平均水平上与显式技能维护表现相当，表明大部分改进源于对先前上下文和反馈的适应而非可复用的技能抽象；显式技能在需要可复用流程或精确输出的任务上提供选择性优势；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。

Conclusion: 当前的上下文技能进化机制能够支持持续适应，但在将经验一致地整合为稳健且可迁移的技能方面仍存在困难，这为未来智能体自主技能进化研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills.
