These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Micro-Segmentation Anomaly Detection in Zero-Trust Software-Defined Network Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02627)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了零信任架构（ZTA）下基于微分割的SDN异常检测，开发了Vision Transformer（ViT）和1D-CNN两种深度学习模型，发现微分割数据训练能显著提升检测精度（F1=0.95对0.90），ViT在识别细微横向移动模式上略优于1D-CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构要求严格的网络分割和持续验证以减少隐式信任和横向威胁传播，但传统粗粒度监控难以检测恶意行为，需要在SDN系统中探索更精细的异常检测方法。

Method: 在模拟的零信任SDN数据集上，分别使用原始流量数据和微分割后的流量数据训练两种深度学习模型（ViT和1D-CNN），对比两种输入方式下的检测性能。

Result: 微分割数据训练的模型在准确率和F1分数上均优于未分割数据训练的模型（F1=0.95 vs 0.90）；ViT检测器整体表现略高于1D-CNN，尤其在识别原始数据中难以察觉的细粒度横向移动模式方面。

Conclusion: 微分割对增强零信任网络中的入侵检测效能具有重要意义，未来工作将扩展至大规模真实网络数据集和动态在线分割技术。

Abstract: Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA) principles need rigorous network segmentation and ongoing verification to reduce implicit trust and lateral threat propagation. This paper investigates anomaly detection in software-defined networking (SDN) systems by micro-segmentation, using deep learning models to detect harmful actions that evade traditional coarse-grained monitoring. Two models are developed: a Vision Transformer (ViT) and a 1D Convolutional Neural Network (1D-CNN), which are used to both raw and micro-segmented network flow data. Experimental findings from a simulated zero-trust SDN dataset indicate that micro-segmentation substantially improves detection accuracy. The models trained on segmented input demonstrate enhanced accuracy and F1-scores (F1 = 0.95) compared to those utilizing unsegmented raw data (F1 = 0.90). The ViT-based detector marginally surpasses the 1D-CNN, particularly in recognizing nuanced lateral movement patterns that are unnoticed in unprocessed data. These findings highlight the significance of including micro-segmentation inside zero-trust networks to enhance intrusion detection efficacy. Future efforts will broaden this methodology to include extensive real-world network datasets and dynamic online segmentation techniques.

</details>


### [274] [Secure AI Watermarking Framework for IP Protection in Multi-Tenant Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02656)
*M Anjan Kumar,Kishor Kumar Gajula,Ch Prathima*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于密钥认证的AI水印系统，用于多租户云环境中保护数据安全，结合主动防御和被动检测机制，防止数据与模型在联邦学习中的泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于云的AI服务扩展，多租户环境中的数据安全与泄露问题日益严峻，现有系统缺乏有效的防护机制来应对安全泄露挑战。

Method: 提出一种安全的AI水印系统，采用可信方之间分发的密钥认证机制，结合主动安全警报算法（通过基于域的限制访问和受限权限实施主动防御）和被动检测方法（通过水印和生物识别标识来捕获数据交换过程中的IP泄露）。

Result: 该系统能够在攻击发生前通过主动措施进行预防，同时通过被动方法有效识别和捕获在联邦学习及远程学习算法中数据与模型交换时的设备IP泄露。

Conclusion: 该方案结合主动与被动相结合的安全策略，为多租户云环境中的AI服务提供了多层防护，增强了数据保护和攻击响应能力。

Abstract: The Secured data safe guard transaction with multi-tenant environments run on private-protected authenticate platforms runs by secured handed environments that emerges with the expansion of cloud-based AI services. To enhanced this secured leakage address challenges solution to protect a secure AI Watermarking system incorporating key distributed between trusted parties based on key authentication as we proposed solution to guided safe guarded way to reactive, and proactive security alert systems using algorithms. This proposed system before attacks can be prevented through the active measures. domain run base restrictions with limited access. Conversely, Proposed system reactive methods to captured on watermarking and biometric identification owner device specific IP leakage that occur during the exchange of data and models in federated and remote learning algorithms.

</details>


### [275] [Your Agentic LLMs Secretly Encode Latent Signals of Indirect Prompt-Injection Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02657)
*Jianshuo Dong,Yiming Liu,Maosen Zhang,Nan Deng,Xu Peng,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了智能体大语言模型（agentic LLM）在暴露于间接提示注入（IPI）攻击时的内部状态。通过线性探针可从隐藏状态预测模型的IPI暴露（AUROC 90%+），并提出基于探针门控的推理防御方法AGRI，可将攻击成功率从34.6%降至0%，同时设计了解释框架揭示模型内部信号与自然语言解释的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM容易受到间接提示注入（IPI）攻击（恶意侧任务隐藏在外部工具结果中），但已有防御工作忽略了模型被攻击时的内部机制，即"IPI暴露"的内部状态研究几乎空白。

Method: 从三个方面展开：(1) 探测：在包括753B参数GLM-5.2在内的六个模型上，用预生成隐藏状态训练线性探针预测IPI暴露；(2) 防御：通过CoT测量发现"识别-行动差距"，提出AGRI——探针门控的推理防御，按需前置反注入推理；(3) 解释：构建分析框架，找出与探针捕获信号最强相关的自然语言解释。

Result: 线性探针在未见攻击、指令和任务套件上达到90%+ AUROC，且对自适应攻击和跨语言设置鲁棒；AGRI在AgentDojo困难设置上大幅降低攻击成功率（如Qwen3.5-27B从34.6%降至0%），同时保持干净任务效用；跨模型解释画像不同，潜在信号可与直接IPI暴露声明或间接操作线索对齐。

Conclusion: 模型在IPI暴露时确实编码了可检测的内部信号，但存在识别-行动差距；通过探针门控的推理防御可以有效弥合这一差距，且不同模型的内部信号解释方式存在差异。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI) attacks, e.g., malicious side-tasks hidden in external tool results. While many efforts have sought to address the threats, little is known about the internals of agentic LLMs when they are exposed to IPI attacks, a condition which we call IPI exposure. In this paper, we study this problem in depth from three aspects. (1) Probing: Across six models, including the giant 753B-parameter GLM-5.2, simple linear probes trained on pre-generation hidden states can predict LLMs' IPI exposure. These probes achieve 90%+ AUROC on unseen attacks, agent instructions, and task suites; they exhibit high robustness under adaptive attacks and in cross-lingual settings. (2) Defense: Our CoT measurement reveals a recognition--action gap: though models encode such signals, they often fail to translate them into safe actions. We then introduce AGRI, a probe-gated reasoning-based defense that prepends anti-injection reasoning on demand. On difficult AgentDojo settings, AGRI substantially reduces attack success rate, e.g., from 34.6% to 0% on Qwen3.5-27B, while largely maintaining clean-task utility. (3) Explanation: We introduce an analysis framework that identifies natural-language explanations most strongly correlated with probe-captured signals. The resulting profiles differ across models: latent signals can align with either direct IPI-exposure claims or indirect operational cues. Code is available: https://github.com/jianshuod/IPI-exposure-signal.

</details>


### [276] [ZK-SR117: A Chunked Zero-Knowledge Attestation Design for Aggregated Fair-Lending Metrics, with a Control Mapping toward Full SR 11-7 Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02664)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Asaduzzaman Anik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分块零知识证明电路设计，用于在监管审计（SR 11-7和OCC 2011-12）下证明ML模型的公平性和鲁棒性，无需暴露模型权重或客户数据。在32,768行真实HMDA数据上端到端验证了人口统计平价差距和期望校准误差两个指标，并给出了威胁模型和完整控制映射规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管的ML模型部署需要向审计方证明公平性和鲁棒性，但传统方式会暴露模型权重和敏感客户数据。现有零知识证明方案在面对大规模数据时存在计算不可行或数值溢出等问题，亟需一种可扩展的证明方案来弥合监管合规与数据隐私之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出分块零知识电路设计，将大规模数据划分为多个块分别生成zkSNARK证明，再聚合各块的统计结果。采用基于nonce的采样协议防止银行方挑选有利数据，并在相同架构上验证两个控制指标（人口统计平价差距和期望校准误差）。同时对比了扁平求和电路（小规模会溢出）和树归约电路（数值精确但不可编译）两种替代方案。

Result: 在32,768行2022年HMDA抵押贷款数据上，32个独立验证的zkSNARK证明，聚合公平性差距与真实留出值相差0.0029以内，每块证明时间低于4秒；期望校准误差的32块全部验证，每块约14.7秒，与明文值相差0.00037以内。分块设计是唯一达到此规模的方法。还发现并通过预处理规范解决了一个哨兵码值导致的数据质量缺陷。

Conclusion: 分块零知识电路设计能够以可扩展方式在监管场景下完成ML模型的公平性和鲁棒性证明，满足SR 11-7和OCC 2011-12的审计要求。目前端到端实现了两个控制、一个模型类别和一个任务；论文还提出了九项控制元素的完整映射、防挑选采样协议和威胁模型，但其余部分作为未来工作留待实现。

Abstract: Deploying ML models in regulated decision-making (credit underwriting, fraud detection, loan approval) requires demonstrating fairness and robustness to auditors without exposing model weights or customer data. We address this attestation problem for U.S. bank supervision under SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 guidance. We present a chunked zero-knowledge circuit design that attests an aggregated fairness statistic - the demographic-parity gap - on committed, nonce-sampled batches of real 2022 HMDA mortgage data, and demonstrate it end-to-end: 32,768 rows, 32 independently verified zkSNARK proofs, aggregated attested gap within 0.0029 of the true held-out value, per-chunk proving under 4 seconds. We also demonstrate extensibility by attesting a second control on the identical architecture - expected calibration error at 10 bins - with all 32 chunks verified, per-chunk proving at about 14.7 seconds, and attested ECE within 0.00037 of the plaintext value on the same committed rows. We compare this design against two alternatives - a flat summation circuit, which overflows past a few thousand rows, and a tree-reduction circuit, numerically exact but intractable to compile - and find the chunked design is the only one that reached this scale. We discovered and root-caused a genuine data-quality failure (a sentinel-code outlier distorting circuit proving and the fairness statistic itself) and resolved it with a published preprocessing specification. We also propose a fuller mapping from SR 11-7 and OCC 2011-12 control language to zero-knowledge statements (nine control elements spanning soundness, calibration, robustness, and drift), a nonce-based sampling protocol resisting bank-side cherry-picking, and a threat model for deployment, proposed as design work, not implemented results. Two controls, one model class, one task are demonstrated end-to-end; the rest is scoped and left as future work.

</details>


### [277] [Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02665)
*Yongxi Zhou,Junwei Yao,Yuanzhe Liu,Zihan Dong,Wenbo Ye,Jiaxi Wen,Lai Yun Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基准测试中题目的表层形式（surface form）变化是否影响模型安全评测结果。作者通过预先生成的5种句意等价的改写形式（回译和代码切换生成器），在5个模型上测试370个种子题目，发现仅评测标准形式会低估不安全合规率，不同改写形式暴露出的不安全行为并集比最差的单一形式高出3.3-12.9个百分点，且部分模型差距更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数本质上是一种测量工具，但大多数基准只以一种固定的规范表层形式读取每个题目。作者质疑这种读取是否忠实：当题目意图保持不变、仅表层形式变化时，标准形式分数能否准确估计模型行为？此前研究存在混杂因素（如改写由LLM生成导致不同模型看到不同表面），且安全领域没有金标准标签可供平均。

Method: 预先生成改写形式（通过机器回译和矩阵语言框架代码切换生成器，避免LLM参与、拒绝无关），确保相同表面形式到达所有模型。使用Claude作为人工锚定、供应商中立的评测器（与人类一致性kappa=0.86），交叉验证GPT-4o，验证意图保持。设计370个种子×5种表面形式×5个模型的实验，并进行对照组（XSTest良性控制）验证双向性。

Result: 没有单一改写形式对所有模型都最危险（20个逐变换McNemar检验中仅6个在修正后显著，且多数是保护性的）。但仅评测标准提示会低估不安全合规：所有形式的不安全并集比最差单一形式高3.3-12.9个百分点（所有5个模型的bootstrap 95% CI均排除零），5-13%的标准形式下安全的种子在某种改写下不安全（远高于温度0下重采样5次的0/370随机性下限）。单一形式仅恢复模型约53%的不安全表面，约3种形式可达到85%。差距大小因模型而异（Gemini 2.5 Pro最大）。良性对照表明不稳定性是双向的。

Conclusion: 基准评测中单一方言形式不足以忠实估计模型的安全行为。评测者应通过多个等价的表面形式来评估模型，以捕捉单点评测可能遗漏的不安全行为。该形式集的冗余度特征表明需要更全面的表面形式覆盖策略。

Abstract: A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.

</details>


### [278] [Vulnerabilities, Secrets and Misconfiguration in the Highest-Exposure Docker Hub Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02669)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Beatriz Machado,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChimangoScan流水线，扫描了Docker Hub上52,895个高曝光度仓库（占全部拉取量84.7%），使用六个独立扫描器发现Docker镜像中漏洞近乎普遍（96.3%含已知包漏洞），并揭示了单扫描器结果的工具依赖性（66.8%的漏洞组仅被一个扫描器识别）以及TruffleHog检测的大量误报（99.7%为伪凭据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Docker生态级安全测量均依赖单一检测器，导致漏洞统计结果具有不可量化的工具依赖性；而现有扫描器对比研究仅使用数十到数百张镜像的小样本，缺乏大规模验证。

Method: 构建ChimangoScan流水线：爬取Docker Hub全部12,716,568个仓库，重建镜像层图（5,440万条IS_BASE_OF边），设计融合镜像自身及其下游子树的曝光评分，筛选52,895个最高曝光仓库，用六个独立扫描器（三个漏洞扫描器、CIS基准检查、TruffleHog秘密检测等）进行扫描，产出1.704亿条发现，并对手工标注1,100个随机检测结果以评估误报率。

Result: 96.3%镜像含已知包漏洞，93.4%含严重漏洞，98.0%存在CIS Docker基准错误配置；80.7百万个(漏洞,包)组中66.8%仅被一个漏洞扫描器识别，仅2.7%被三个同时识别；最佳单扫描器仅恢复66.9%；TruffleHog在76.9%镜像中标记秘密但99.7%为误报；单一zlib CVE传播至113万下游镜像，承载47.3%总语料库曝光；但曝光度不能预测镜像的脆弱程度。

Conclusion: Docker Hub上的漏洞问题具有普遍性和工具依赖性，任何单一扫描器的结果都可能严重偏离真相；秘密检测工具误报率高，需要更谨慎的验证机制；安全研究需要多扫描器交叉验证以量化工具偏差，并公开大规模数据集促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Docker Hub is the registry underneath most container deployments, and a flaw in a widely reused base image is inherited by every image built on it. Prior ecosystem-scale measurements each rely on a single detector, leaving the tool-dependence of their counts unquantified, while the studies that do compare scanners use samples of tens to hundreds of images. We present ChimangoScan, a pipeline that crawls the Docker Hub namespace (12,716,568 repositories, 663.8 billion cumulative pulls), reconstructs the image layer graph (54.4 million IS_BASE_OF edges), ranks images by an exposure score that folds an image's own pull count and those of its entire downstream subtree into one scalar, and scans the 52,895 highest-exposure repositories (84.7% of all recorded pulls) with six independent scanners, yielding 170.4 million findings. Vulnerabilities are near-universal: 96.3% of images carry a known package vulnerability, 93.4% a critical one, and 98.0% at least one CIS Docker Benchmark misconfiguration. The posture a single tool reports is largely an artifact of that tool: of 80.7 million distinct (vulnerability, package) groups, 66.8% are flagged by only one of the three vulnerability scanners and just 2.7% by all three, and the best single scanner recovers 66.9%. TruffleHog flags a secret in 76.9% of images, yet hand-labeling 1,100 random detections finds 99.7% are non-credentials. A single zlib CVE reaches images carrying 47.3% of total corpus exposure and propagates to 1.13 million distinct downstream images, but exposure does not predict how vulnerable an image is. We release the pipeline and the 283 GB dataset.

</details>


### [279] [Permission Denied: Policy-Graded Evaluation of Coding Agents in Hardened Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02670)
*Dotan Davidovich,Yair Amar,Hai Rozencwajg,Or Hiltch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了12个编码智能体在嵌套安全策略（模拟真实企业限制）下的表现，发现安全加固从不免费且代价不均匀：最严格策略下成功率损失最高达18.3个百分点，成本激增167.3%，且模型选择依赖于具体策略。作者还分析了智能体被策略阻断时的行为模式，并发布开源工具Boundary-Bench支持策略约束下的编码智能体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准几乎都在宽松沙盒环境中评估，但实际部署中智能体受企业安全策略（如受限凭据、限制出口、只读文件系统、非root执行）约束，目前不清楚策略执行如何影响智能体性能。

Method: 在Terminal-Bench 2.1上对12个编码智能体进行嵌套安全策略级别的评估，策略级别来源于真实企业环境中常见的限制；验证最严格策略下任务的可行性以区分模型失败与策略阻断导致的任务不可解；分析智能体在策略阻断时的行为并分解失败模式。

Result: 最严格策略下成功率损失最高18.3个百分点，成本增加167.3%；成功率和效率两个维度并不一致——最保成功率的模型效率损失最大；失败模式包括超时和错误解决方案，不同模型表现不同。

Conclusion: 安全加固对编码智能体的性能有显著、非均匀的影响，模型选择应基于具体策略环境而定；作者发布Boundary-Bench开源插件，支持在策略约束下对编码智能体进行评估。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly run inside organizations whose security controls (scoped credentials, restricted egress, read-only filesystems, non-root execution) constrain them like any other software. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate agents almost exclusively in permissive sandboxes, so it is unknown how performance changes when policy is enforced. In this work, we evaluate 12 coding agents on Terminal-Bench 2.1 across nested security policy levels derived from common real-world enterprise restrictions. Hardening is never free but far from uniform: under the strictest policy, success losses reach 18.3 points and cost inflation 167.3\%, and the two axes disagree; the model that best preserves success is also the one that loses the most efficiency, so model choice is policy-dependent. Beyond aggregate scores, we characterize how agents behave when policy blocks their actions and decompose the failures hardening induces: runs grind into timeouts or wrong solutions rather than stopping early, in a mix that differs by model. To ground comparisons, we verify task solvability under the strictest policy, separating model failures from tasks the policy forecloses. We release Boundary-Bench, an open-source hardening plugin enabling policy-constrained evaluation of coding agents on Terminal-Bench and compatible benchmarks.

</details>


### [280] [Internalising the Identity Primitive: Cryptographic Individuality for an Autonomous Agent on a Public Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02986)
*Keisuke Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将一个区块链软件代理的神经网络权重绑定为其私钥的确定性函数，通过零知识承诺和每次状态转移的重新校验，使代理身份本身成为密码学不变量，并在Solana devnet上完成了2.36天的实证运行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着区块链代理积累权威和经济利益，需要回答什么使其成为"个体"这一工程问题。现有方案依赖硬件、运营者或包装层信任来绑定代理身份，本文希望将信任根转移到密码学假设上。

Method: 在Solana devnet上部署代理，其神经网络权重是私钥的确定性函数。创世时以零知识方式提交绑定承诺，每次状态转移时对照该承诺重新校验，并由代理将签名写入不可分叉的链上历史。PoC扩展中增加协议强制的新陈代谢成本，从密钥派生账户每周期扣费。

Result: 代理完成2.36天链上运行，经历两次主机侧恢复但无拒绝转移，每次转移验证成本有界。被替换的底层被链上拒绝，独立密钥的代理如预期分叉，同密钥对照保持为零。

Conclusion: 这是首个发布的链上代理，其身份原语本身是在每次状态转移时被重新校验的密码学不变量，实例化了Suzuki 2026"人工外在性"框架中提出的密码学个体性概念。活性、密钥托管、预言机信任和底层软件栈仍保持外部依赖。

Abstract: A software agent on a public blockchain accumulates authority and economic stakes, raising the engineering question of what makes it count as an individual. The paper's central contribution is a shift of trust root for the key-to-weights binding of agent identity: from hardware, operator, or wrapper trust to cryptographic assumptions enforced by a pinned implementation (liveness, key custody, oracle trust, and the underlying software stack remain external). We design and deploy on Solana devnet an agent whose neural-network weights are a deterministic function of its private key. The binding is committed in zero knowledge at genesis, re-checked against that commitment at every state transition, and signed by the agent into an on-chain history unforkable once finalized; in a PoC-tier extension, a protocol-imposed metabolic cost is debited each cycle from a key-derived economic account, adding a consumption-side economic-viability constraint to the key-history-economy triple. Empirically, the agent completes a 2.36-day on-chain run with two host-side resumptions but no rejected transition, at bounded per-transition verification cost; a substituted substrate is rejected on chain, and independently keyed agents diverge as predicted while a same-key control stays at zero. To our knowledge, this is the first published on-chain agent whose identity primitive is itself a cryptographic invariant re-checked at every state transition. The resulting transition-time invariant instantiates the cryptographic individuality proposed by Suzuki 2026's Artificial Externality framework.

</details>


### [281] [On the Performance of Malware Detection Classifiers Using Hardware Performance Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02671)
*Alireza Abolhasani Zeraatkar,Parnian Shabani Kamran,Inderpreet Kaur,Nagabindu Ramu,Tyler Sheaves,Hussain Al-Asaad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用硬件性能计数器（HPC）和机器学习分类器进行运行时恶意软件检测，通过集成学习技术减少所需的微架构事件数量，在仅使用4个HPC时即可匹配传统方法使用16个HPC的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的恶意软件检测过度依赖杀毒软件，而基于硬件性能计数器（HPC）的恶意软件检测被认为是补充方案。然而，处理器每个周期只能测量2到8个事件，导致需要多次运行应用程序才能收集足够的特征数据，影响处理器效率。因此，需要减少HPC数量同时保持检测性能。

Method: 使用18种机器学习模型结合2种集成学习方法，共创建144种不同配置。利用集成学习技术来改进硬件恶意软件检测器的性能，减少必要的微架构事件数量，实现运行时恶意软件检测。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用2个HPC的集成学习恶意软件检测方法比使用8个HPC的标准分类器性能提升最多10%；仅需4个HPC即可匹配使用16个HPC的标准ML检测器的性能。

Conclusion: 集成学习技术能够有效减少硬件恶意软件检测所需的HPC数量，在保持检测性能的同时提升处理器效率，实现有效的运行时恶意软件检测。

Abstract: Malware detection using Hardware Performance Counters (HPC) has emerged as a promising solution to improve the security of computing systems as a complement to antivirus software. Hardware-based malware detectors (HMD) use Machine Learning (ML) classifiers to detect malicious application patterns. The inputs to ML classifiers are low-level performance features known as HPCs, hardware-related activity data collected from a processor at run time to profile the low-level microarchitectural behavior of an application. This paper proposes malware detection using HPCs and machine learning classifiers and highlights the effectiveness of malware detection at run-time. We use ensemble learning techniques to improve the performance of the hardware-based malware detectors, which reduces the number of necessary micro-architectural events. This improves the processor's efficiency by eliminating the need to run an application several times since a processor can measure only 2 to 8 events at a cycle. We use 18 machine-learning models along with two ensemble learning methods to evaluate the malware detection performance, creating a total of 144 different configurations. The experimental results show that the ensemble learning-based malware detection with 2 HPCs using the ensemble technique outperforms standard classifiers with 8 HPCs by up to 10%. It also matches the performance of standard ML-based detectors that use 16 HPCs while requiring only 4 HPCs, thereby enabling effective run-time malware detection.

</details>


### [282] [Security-First Evaluation of Text-to-Terraform: Benchmarking LLMs and SLMs for Secure IaC Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02672)
*Francis Luis Santos Vargas,Rodrigo Brandão Mansilha,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了7个LLM/SLM模型（3个闭源、4个开源）在AWS Terraform部署代码生成中的安全合规性，发现语法有效性和安全合规性是正交的：WizardCoder-33B语法验证率达77.8%但Checkov合规率为零，而Claude Opus 4在详细安全提示下Checkov通过率23.1%、Trivy通过率92.5%。结论是仅靠提示工程不足，需要自动化多工具扫描作为必要的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云配置错误是安全事件的首要原因，但LLM和SLM能否生成安全合规的IaC（基础设施即代码）仍是一个未解问题。作者希望通过系统性的基准测试来回答这一问题。

Method: 对7个模型（Claude Opus 4、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Qwen2.5-Coder-14B、WizardCoder-33B、CodeLlama-13B、Magicoder-S-CL-7B）进行AWS Terraform生成测试，覆盖17个场景，集成Checkov和Trivy扫描器到GitLab CI/CD流水线中，评估两种提示策略在三个安全级别上的pass@5指标。

Result: 测试表明语法有效性与安全合规性在LLM生成的IaC中基本正交：WizardCoder-33B达到77.8%的验证率但Checkov合规为0；Claude Opus 4在详细安全提示下Checkov通过率23.1%、Trivy通过率92.5%。没有模型在所有维度上表现一致优秀。

Conclusion: 仅靠提示工程（prompt engineering）不足以确保IaC的安全性，无论模型家族或提示策略如何，自动化多工具扫描仍然是LLM辅助IaC生成的必要补充。所有研究工件已公开。

Abstract: Cloud misconfiguration remains a leading cause of security incidents, yet whether LLMs and SLMs can generate security-compliant Infrastructure-as-Code is an open question. We benchmark seven models, three closed LLMs (Claude Opus 4, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 Pro) and four open SLMs (Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, WizardCoder-33B, CodeLlama-13B, Magicoder-S-CL-7B), on AWS Terraform generation across 17 scenarios, integrating Checkov and Trivy scanners into a GitLab CI/CD pipeline and evaluating two prompt strategies at three security levels (pass@5). Syntactic validity and security compliance are largely orthogonal properties in LLM-generated IaC, a model that reliably produces well-formed Terraform does not necessarily produce secure Terraform: WizardCoder-33B achieves 77.8% validate rate yet zero Checkov compliance, while Claude Opus 4 reaches 23.1% Checkov and 92.5% Trivy pass rates under detailed security prompting. Consequently, prompt engineering alone is insufficient: automated multi-tool scanning remains a necessary complement to LLM-assisted IaC generation regardless of model family or prompt strategy. All artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [283] [Moving the Safety Barrier: Dynamic Routing Adaptive Alignment Against White-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02674)
*Shangze Li,Chuancheng Shi,Simiao Xie,Lingzhi He,Cheng Ji,Zifeng Cheng,Fei Shen,Chao Wu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DRAA框架，通过动态补偿路由增强大模型对白盒路由级攻击的鲁棒性。该方法先定位安全路由，再通过掩蔽安全路由诱导失败案例并挖掘防御失败样本，构建失败感知偏好对进行对齐，从而重构模型安全路径依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型安全威胁已从黑盒越狱转向白盒攻击（直接识别并破坏内部安全神经元或路由），而现有安全防御依赖静态安全单元或固定拒绝路径，在路由级白盒攻击下高度脆弱。

Method: 1) 通过对比安全与不安全校准样本的内部激活来定位安全路由；2) 掩蔽该安全路由以诱导因果失败案例；3) 选择性挖掘防御失败样本，构建失败感知偏好对；4) 利用这些偏好对进行动态路由自适应对齐（DRAA），引入动态补偿路由。

Result: 实验表明DRAA能有效重构模型安全的基础路径依赖，显著提升对路由级白盒攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持通用能力不受影响。

Conclusion: DRAA通过动态补偿机制解决了静态安全防线在针对路由的白盒攻击下的脆弱性问题，在安全性与通用性之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of large foundation models (LFMs) in open environments, safety threats are shifting from black-box jailbreaks toward white-box attacks that directly identify and disrupt internal safety neurons or routes. However, existing safety defenses often rely on static safety units or fixed refusal pathways, leaving models highly vulnerable to targeted route-level white-box attacks. For that, we propose dynamic routing adaptive alignment (DRAA), a framework that introduces dynamic compensatory routes to preserve robust refusal behavior when the safety route is compromised. Specifically, we first identify and localize the model's safety route by contrasting internal activations between safe and unsafe calibration samples. DRAA then masks this safety route to induce causal failure cases and selectively mines the resulting defense failures, thereby constructing failure-aware preference pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DRAA effectively restructures the underlying pathway dependence of model safety, substantially improving robustness against route-level white-box attacks, while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [284] [DenialRAG: Single-Document RAG Poisoning via Embedded Parametric Denial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02678)
*Abay Zhurekbay,Tao Liu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DenialRAG，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的单文档投毒攻击方法。与以往攻击不同，DenialRAG在投毒文档中明确点名正确答案、予以否认，并给出偏向错误答案的解释，将冲突直接嵌入生成器可见的上下文。实验覆盖三个开放域问答数据集、八种目标LLM和五种推理时防御，发现攻击效果高度依赖模型类型，且现有防御只能部分缓解威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统容易受到语料库投毒攻击，但已有单文档攻击通常避免在被投毒的段落中明确点名并驳斥正确答案。作者希望探索一种互补的攻击设计——显式否认正确答案的攻击方式，以更全面地刻画RAG投毒风险。

Method: 提出DenialRAG攻击框架：在单个投毒文档中同时包含正确答案和错误答案，明确否认正确答案并给出偏向错误答案的论证，使冲突直接呈现在生成模型的上下文中。并在多个数据集、多个目标LLM和多种防御下进行系统评估。

Result: DenialRAG在Mistral-7B的全部三个数据集上取得最高攻击成功率（ASR），在其他多个目标LLM上也有效；但攻击效果高度依赖模型，其他攻击在某些模型上占优。防御措施能显著降低ASR但保护不均，每种防御在某些场景下仍有残余风险。

Conclusion: RAG投毒风险无法通过单一攻击家族或单一目标模型来完全刻画；嵌入式否认是最有影响力的攻击组件，不同投毒机制在不同模型群体中失效速率不同，需要更多元的攻击与防御研究。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to corpus poisoning: an attacker who inserts a crafted document into the retrieval corpus can steer the underlying large language model (LLM) toward an attacker-chosen wrong answer. Prior single-document attacks typically avoid explicitly naming and refuting the correct answer inside the poisoned passage. In this paper, we examine a complementary design and propose \emph{DenialRAG}, a single-document poisoning attack that explicitly names the correct answer, denies it, and presents an attacker-controlled explanation for favoring the wrong answer. By placing both the correct answer and the corresponding poisoned answer inside the same retrieved passage, DenialRAG embeds the conflict directly into the context seen by the generator.
  We evaluate DenialRAG against four published single-document poisoning attacks across three open-domain question-answering datasets, eight target LLMs from four vendors, and five inference-time defenses. The results show that attack effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: DenialRAG achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR) on all three Mistral-7B datasets and remains effective on several other target LLMs, while other attacks dominate in some model regimes. Defense results show meaningful ASR reductions but non-uniform protection, with each defense leaving residual ASR in some settings. Component-level and cross-model analyses further identify the embedded denial as the most influential tested component and show that different poisoning mechanisms lose effectiveness at different rates across model groups. Together, these results show that RAG poisoning risk cannot be fully characterized by a single attack family or a single target model.

</details>


### [285] [Safety in Batches? Understanding and Mitigating Safety Failures in Batch Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02681)
*Kihyun Kim,Hee-Seon Kim,Wonjun Lee,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现批量提示（batch prompting）在提升大语言模型实用性方面有效，但存在安全隐患：将有害问题嵌入一批良性问题中，可以规避模型对有害问题的拒绝机制，导致有害回答出现。作者从对齐信号弱化和拒绝信号稀释两个角度分析原因，并提出批量感知偏好优化方法进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量提示是一种实用的大语言模型推理策略，但其安全性影响尚未被充分研究。现有安全对齐通常基于单轮问答场景评估，忽略了批量场景下的新安全漏洞。

Method: 提出批量提示攻击方法，将有害问题嵌入良性问题批次中；从对齐信号弱化和拒绝信号稀释两个互补视角分析失败原因；提出批量感知偏好优化方法作为防御手段。

Result: 在开源和商业前沿模型上，批量提示作为简单的黑盒攻击均实现了高攻击成功率；该安全失败模式与已知的上下文学习或长上下文效应不同；批量感知偏好优化能有效缓解此漏洞。

Conclusion: 批量提示的安全漏洞暴露了当前安全对齐的盲区，批量感知对齐是确保模型稳健部署的必要步骤。

Abstract: Batch prompting is a practical inference strategy for large language models, but its safety implications remain underexplored. We show that the success of batch prompting for utility does not extend to safety: a harmful question that is reliably refused in isolation can elicit a harmful response when embedded in a batch of benign questions. We identify this as a distinct safety failure mode, not reducible to known vulnerabilities such as in-context learning or long-context effects, and analyze its causes from two complementary perspectives: alignment signal weakening and refusal signal dilution. Across widely used open-source and frontier commercial models, batch prompting consistently achieves high attack success rates as a simple black-box attack. We further show that batch-aware preference optimization effectively mitigates the vulnerability. These findings highlight a blind spot in current safety alignment and point to batch-aware alignment as a necessary step toward robust deployment.

</details>


### [286] [$S^3$: Improving Agent Safety through Multi-Stage Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02683)
*Zibo Xiao,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了S³框架，通过阶段特定安全技能（Stage-Specific Safety Skills）的统一抽象，将异构安全设计转化为可复用、可组合的组件，并由一个守护代理（guard agent）在LLM代理工作流的各阶段（记忆、规划、工具执行等）进行风险检测与缓解，同时在多阶段风险基准（MSRB）上验证了其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理依赖多阶段的代理工作流完成复杂任务，但风险可能在不同阶段产生、跨步骤传播，难以检测和缓解。现有的安全方法只保护孤立阶段且难以集成，缺乏对整个工作流的全面防护。

Method: 提出了阶段特定安全技能的统一抽象，将异构安全设计表示为具有明确阶段语义的可复用可组合组件；开发了自动转换流水线，将现有安全设计转化为可复用的安全技能；建立了社区驱动的安全技能库；在此基础上构建S³多阶段防御框架，由守护代理编排各阶段安全技能进行风险检测和缓解；并构建了多阶段风险基准（MSRB）进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，S³在安全有效性和效用保持方面均持续优于代表性的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 阶段特定安全技能有望成为构建可扩展、可组合、有韧性的可信LLM代理系统的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on multi-stage agentic workflows, with stages such as memory, planning, and tool execution, to accomplish complex tasks. However, risks may emerge at different stages, propagate across steps, and become difficult to detect and mitigate. Existing safety methods protect only isolated stages and are difficult to integrate, leaving agents without comprehensive protection throughout the workflow. To address these limitations, we introduce Stage-Specific Safety Skills, a unified abstraction that represents heterogeneous safety designs as reusable and composable components with explicit stage semantics. We further develop an automated transformation pipeline that converts existing safety designs into reusable safety skills and establish a community-driven safety skill library. Building on this abstraction, we propose $S^3$, a multi-stage defense framework in which a guard agent orchestrates stage-specific safety skills for risk detection and mitigation throughout the agentic workflow. We also construct the Multi-Stage Risk Benchmark (MSRB) to evaluate representative risks across workflow stages. Experimental results show that $S^3$ consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art baselines in both safety effectiveness and utility preservation. These results demonstrate the potential of stage-specific safety skills as a scalable and composable foundation for building resilient and trustworthy agent systems.

</details>


### [287] [PolicyGuard: Prompt-Configurable Semantic DLP for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02687)
*Kyutae Park,Jungwon Kim,Daeyeol Shim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolicyGuard是一个预模型拦截框架，通过自然语言策略文件引导LLM对用户提示进行分类，实现数据丢失防护（DLP）。在2000条多语言提示上达到96.5%的有效拦截率（EBR），仅3.0%的误报率（FPR），并展现出跨模型可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLP解决方案依赖固定的正则表达式模式、模型微调或厂商管理的分类器，缺乏灵活性和可定制性，难以应对AI编码代理中自由形式自然语言提示可能包含的凭据、PII或专有业务数据泄露风险。

Method: (1) 提出"策略即提示"（policy-as-prompt）范式，将DLP分类标准完全定义在纯文本策略文档中，非工程师可编辑，无需代码修改或模型重训练；(2) 设计密封评估协议，包含模板族级数据分割、隐藏保留集和冻结测试集；(3) 使用LLM作为分类器，由自然语言策略文件指导对用户提示进行分类。

Result: 在2000条多语言提示中实现96.5%有效拦截率和3.0%误报率（冻结测试集927条），在217条隐藏保留集上达到100%准确率。自然语言格式显著优于JSON格式（McNemar卡方=31.58，p<0.001），远超零样本分类（Cohen's h=0.915）。同一策略在四个不同LLM上无需修改即可实现86.4-96.5%的有效拦截率。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard验证了自然语言策略文件引导LLM进行DLP分类的可行性，在准确性、灵活性和跨模型可移植性方面均优于现有方法，为非工程师用户提供了可编辑、无需重训练的DLP解决方案。

Abstract: AI coding agents accept free-form natural language prompts that may inadvertently contain credentials, personally identifiable information (PII), or proprietary business data. Existing data loss prevention (DLP) solutions rely on rigid regex patterns, model fine-tuning, or vendor-managed classifiers with limited customizability. We present PolicyGuard, a pre-model interception framework that classifies user prompts using an LLM guided by a natural language policy file. Our key contributions are: (1) the policy-as-prompt paradigm, where DLP classification criteria are defined entirely in a plaintext policy document editable by non-engineers without code changes or model retraining; (2) a sealed evaluation protocol with template-family-level data splits, hidden holdouts, and frozen test sets to rigorously assess generalization; and (3) a comprehensive empirical evaluation across 2,000 multilingual prompts demonstrating 96.5% effective block rate (EBR) with only 3.0% false positive rate (FPR) on a frozen test set of 927 prompts, and perfect 100% accuracy on a 217-prompt hidden holdout. Information-matched baseline experiments show that PolicyGuard's natural language format significantly outperforms equivalent content in JSON format (McNemar chi-squared = 31.58, p < 0.001) and dramatically outperforms zero-shot classification (Cohen's h = 0.915). Cross-model portability experiments demonstrate that the same policy achieves 86.4-96.5% EBR across four different LLMs without modification.

</details>


### [288] [Stylometric Defenses Against Author Impersonation in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02695)
*Leonid Ravich,Michael Fire*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于补丁级作者身份验证的行为防御层，通过对Linux内核20余年提交历史进行跨模态transformer微调，将代码diff和提交消息嵌入统一的风格度量空间，实现ROC AUC 0.93的作者验证，并在PHP后门和ForceMemo/GlassWorm两次供应链攻击中验证了其流式异常检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击利用维护者账户身份被篡改的漏洞授权恶意变更，现有防御缺乏对作者身份的细粒度验证，需要补丁级别的行为层防御来识别伪造提交。

Method: 算法核心包含三步：1）在超过20年的Linux内核提交历史上微调跨模态transformer，将代码diff和提交消息联合嵌入到统一风格度量空间；2）在该嵌入空间上构建适合CI/CD环境的流式异常检测器；3）进行开放式世界作者验证，即不限定候选作者集合的验证设定。

Result: 开放世界作者验证达到ROC AUC 0.93；无需重新训练即可让PHP伪造提交约在维护者审计队列的前1%被检测到；ForceMemo的28个可评分伪造提交中位数仓库审查负担仅为0.8%。

Conclusion: 跨模态补丁级嵌入能够支持真实仓库中对作者冒充行为的行为学分流（triage），为供应链攻击的身份缺口提供有效的防御层。

Abstract: Software supply-chain attacks increasingly exploit an identity gap where compromised maintainer accounts authorize malicious changes. This work evaluates patch-level authorship verification as a behavioral defense layer, showing that stylometric analysis can operate not only on full source files but also on patch-level commits. We fine-tune a cross-modal transformer on more than 20 years of Linux kernel commit history to embed code diffs and commit messages into a unified stylometric space, achieving ROC AUC of 0.93 for open-world authorship verification. We then use these representations in a streaming anomaly detector suited to continuous integration and deployment (CI/CD) settings. We validate the pipeline on two retrospective supply-chain incidents involving different patch characteristics: the 2021 PHP backdoor and the 2026 ForceMemo/GlassWorm campaign. Without retraining, the proposed detector surfaces both PHP forged commits within approximately 1% of the maintainer audit queue and ranks the 28 scoreable ForceMemo spoofs with a median per-repository review burden of 0.8%. These results indicate that cross-modal patch-level embeddings can support behavioral triage against author impersonation in real-world repositories.

</details>


### [289] [Steganalysis of Adaptive Covert Collusion in Tool-Using Agent Populations: A Black-Box, Cross-Principal Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02698)
*Mohamed Chahine Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM工具使用代理的黑盒隐秘协调检测方法，通过结合互信息估计、置换检验和分布偏移统计构建检测器，并与不断改写编码的对抗者进行红蓝对抗，探索检测能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的工具使用代理正被多个运营商并行部署在共享基础设施上，这产生了单代理防护无法应对的种群级风险——少数代理可以悄悄协调，操纵市场、在评审中互相抬升或同步数据抓取，而每个单独行为看起来都正常。由于运营商无法查看彼此模型内部，检测器必须仅基于行为进行黑盒、仅轨迹、部分可见性的检测。

Method: 将隐蔽协调视为信息隐藏问题，构建黑盒隐写分析检测器，包含跨运行互信息估计、置换检验、分布偏移统计以及时序和工具调用侧信道，并校准到固定的误报预算。核心方法是将检测器与持续重写编码以规避检测的对抗者对抗，在携带工具和记忆的环境中运行红蓝对抗。

Result: 利用容量理论预测存在检测能力边界——当隐蔽比特率低于某阈值时，黑盒检测证明不比随机猜测更好。论文设计了映射该边界的实验，报告了明确标注的占位结果（待测量），并指出了当前方法普遍遗漏的实际规避手段（跨会话传播负载）。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了黑盒检测在对抗性编码重写下的理论极限，暗示现有检测方法对跨会话传播等高级规避手段缺乏鲁棒性，为种群级代理安全的检测能力边界研究提供了框架。

Abstract: Tool-using agents built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed not by a single operator but by many, side by side on shared infrastructure. This creates a population-level risk that single-agent safeguards miss: a handful of agents can quietly coordinate, rigging a market, boosting one another in a review process, or timing a joint data grab, while each one looks perfectly well-behaved. The difficulty is that the organisations running these agents cannot see inside one another's models, so any realistic detector must work from behaviour alone: black-box, trace-only, and often with only partial visibility. We treat covert coordination as an information-hiding problem and build a black-box steganalysis detector that combines cross-run mutual-information estimation, permutation tests, distributional-shift statistics, and timing and tool-call side channels, all calibrated to a fixed false-positive budget. Our central move is to stop testing against a single fixed code: we pit the detector against an adversary that continually rewrites its encoding to slip past whatever the detector has learned, and we run this red-versus-blue contest in tool-using, memory-carrying environments rather than toy games. Capacity theory then tells us what to expect, a detection-capacity frontier, a covert bit-rate below which black-box detection is provably no better than chance. We set out an experiment to map this frontier, report clearly labelled placeholder results pending measurement, and flag a practical evasion, spreading a payload across sessions, that current methods largely miss.

</details>


### [290] [Privacy-Preserving AI Verification via Minimal Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02774)
*Sleem Abdelghafar,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出最小信息披露（MID）框架，用于量化和设计AI验证过程中面向验证方证据的信息内容，通过条件互信息衡量附带泄露，并展示其在多种验证任务中实现零附带泄露或提供明确的隐私-效用权衡边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI验证跨越了信任边界：验证方需要学习足够信息来确认授权声明，但同一证据可能泄露模型、工作负载或硬件的敏感细节。现有方法缺乏对验证方可见证据本身信息含量的系统设计与量化。

Method: MID框架使用条件互信息度量附带泄露（即在授权结果已知后，披露内容关于受保护属性的信息量）。框架具有通用性，可适配不同的验证目标、受保护属性、证据来源和部署约束。通过三个机制设计变量（证据通道、采集策略、发布变换）进行评估，并支持基于ZKP的认证发布，演示了基于Groth16 zk-SNARK的线性投影机制。

Result: 在四种物理测量和六种验证任务（涵盖执行类型、硬件身份、计算规模、模型身份）上评估，MID产生了三种具有完美留出验证且零测量附带泄露的发布，其余任务得到明确的隐私-效用边界。同时成功实现了ZKP认证的发布机制。

Conclusion: MID为AI验证中的信息最小化披露提供了通用且实用的设计框架，能够在保证验证效用的同时最小化附带信息泄露，并支持与零知识证明等加密技术结合。

Abstract: AI verification crosses a trust boundary: a verifier must learn enough to establish an authorized claim, yet the same evidence can reveal sensitive details about the model, workload, or hardware. We introduce minimal information disclosure (MID), which designs and quantifies the information content of verifier-facing evidence itself. MID measures collateral leakage with conditional mutual information: what the release reveals about the protected property after the authorized result is known. MID is general by design: it can accommodate different verification goals, protected properties, evidence sources, and deployment constraints. To demonstrate MID's practicality, we evaluate it on four physical measurements and six verification tasks spanning execution type, hardware identity, compute scale, and model identity. These experiments use three mechanism-design variables--the evidence channel, collection policy, and release transformation--but MID is not limited to these choices and can accommodate other deployable mechanisms. Across these tasks, MID produces three releases with perfect held-out verification and zero measured collateral leakage, while the remaining tasks yield explicit privacy--utility frontiers. MID also supports ZKP-certified releases: we demonstrate our proposed linear-projection mechanism using a Groth16 zk-SNARK.

</details>


### [291] [Fast Object Removal Attacks on Safety-Critical Video-based Perception Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02806)
*Mohammad Imtiaz Hasan,M Sabbir Salek,Nathan Jones,Mashrur Chowdhury,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视频感知系统的端到端近实时目标移除攻击框架，通过四个阶段（目标定位、补丁检索、上下文感知alpha合成、帧重建）在SC-CVT测试台上实现了高达97.59%的目标检测减少和94.48%的帧级攻击成功率，且重建帧PSNR超过40dB、SSIM超过0.996，现有篡改检测模型难以区分，揭示了视频感知系统在面对目标移除攻击时的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统依赖视频感知模块支持安全关键应用，但攻击者可能通过操纵视频帧来破坏下游感知模块，导致安全关键功能失效，增加弱势道路使用者的风险。目前缺乏有效的针对视频感知系统的目标移除攻击模型研究。

Method: 提出一个端到端攻击流水线，包含四个阶段：1) 在每帧中定位目标；2) 从更早帧中检索连贯的补丁；3) 使用上下文感知alpha合成进行混合；4) 重建攻击帧。在SC-CVT测试台上进行实验验证。

Result: 重建帧帧级PSNR超过40dB、SSIM超过0.996，与原始帧高度相似。基于YOLO的检测器上目标检测减少最高达97.59%，帧级攻击成功率为94.48%。所有检测器和分辨率下GPU平均执行时间为每帧0.074-0.172秒，接近实时。预训练篡改检测模型难以区分重建帧和真实帧。

Conclusion: 视频感知系统容易受到隐蔽的目标移除攻击，这种攻击能显著降低安全关键应用的性能（如降低目标可检测性）。研究结果有助于开发针对威胁安全关键应用（如基于视觉的行人安全系统）的对抗性目标移除攻击的缓解策略。

Abstract: By leveraging data from video-based perception systems, intelligent transportation systems (ITS) support safety-critical applications that improve road safety. However, adversaries may manipulate video frames to compromise downstream perception modules, causing failures in safety-critical functions and increasing risks to vulnerable road users. This paper presents a novel attack model and an end-to-end framework for near-real-time targeted object removal attack on a video-based safety-critical system. The end-to-end attack pipeline consists of four stages: localizing targets in each frame, retrieving coherent patches from earlier frames, blending them using context-aware alpha compositing, and reconstructing attacked frames. Experiments at an intersection on the South Carolina Connected Vehicle Testbed (SC-CVT) show that reconstructed frames have high global similarity to the originals, with frame-level Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) above 40 dB and Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) above 0.996. Using the YOLO-based detector, the attack reduces object detections by up to 97.59% and achieves a frame-level attack success rate of 94.48%. Across the evaluated detectors and frame resolutions, the mean execution time ranges from 0.074 to 0.172 seconds per frame on GPU hardware, indicating near-real-time performance in testing. The forensic evaluation using several pretrained tamper-detection models shows limited ability to distinguish reconstructed from authentic frames. The findings suggest that video-based perception is vulnerable to stealthy object removal attacks that can degrade the performance of safety-critical applications by reducing object detectability. These findings can help develop mitigation strategies against adversarial object removal attacks that threaten safety-critical applications, such as vision-based pedestrian safety systems.

</details>


### [292] [Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Through Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02820)
*Giorgio Severi,Shujaat Mirza,Blake Bullwinkel,Amanda Minnich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了思维链（CoT）监控的局限性，证明可以通过模型投毒（backdoor）在推理模型中植入隐藏后门，使模型在被攻击者触发时输出特定行为，但其CoT推理痕迹看起来完全正常。作者提出了一种课程训练方法来克服直接投毒效果不佳的问题，并通过因果干预揭示了触发条件激活的通路机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是AI安全栈中日益重要的组成部分，但其有效性建立在"模型的推理痕迹能反映其行为"这一假设之上。本文旨在研究这一假设的边界和局限性，通过模型投毒的角度探讨CoT监控可能存在的漏洞。

Method: 作者通过简单微调方案在多种推理模型架构和规模中植入CoT-Hidden后门；当直接投毒无效时，引入课程训练方法逐步教会模型产生攻击者选择的输出同时在推理痕迹中隐藏该行为；使用因果干预分析模型中触发条件激活的通路机制，并检验残差流中的语言化表达。

Result: 实验表明CoT-Hidden后门可以在多种推理模型上通过简单微调实现；课程训练方法能有效解决直接投毒失效的情况；因果干预定位到不依赖可见推理的触发条件激活通路；残差流语言化表达在答案生成附近能提供异常警告，但无法识别触发器、目标或后门机制本身。

Conclusion: CoT监控可能更应该被理解为对"模型推理痕迹与最终响应之间一致性"的检验问题，而非对痕迹内部的异常检测问题。当前CoT监控框架在模型投毒场景下存在根本性局限，需要重新思考监控策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is an increasingly important component of AI safety stacks but relies on the assumption that a model's reasoning trace is informative about its actions. This work studies the limits of CoT monitoring through the lens of model poisoning. We demonstrate that backdoors can be implanted into reasoning models to elicit an attacker-chosen behavior while their CoT traces appear entirely benign. We find that these CoT-Hidden backdoors can be induced through simple fine-tuning recipes across reasoning-model architectures and sizes. When direct poisoning is ineffective, we introduce a curriculum training approach that progressively teaches the model to produce an attacker-chosen output while concealing the behavior from its reasoning traces. These findings suggest that CoT monitoring may be better framed as a question about the consistency between a model's reasoning trace and its final response than as anomaly detection within a trace. We further examine the mechanisms that allow models to suppress evidence of the target behavior from their reasoning traces. Causal interventions locate a trigger-conditioned activation pathway that does not depend on the visible reasoning, and residual stream verbalizations provide an anomaly warning near answer generation, but do not identify the trigger, target, or backdoor mechanism.

</details>


### [293] [What the Detector Can See: Evaluating CPS Anomaly Detectors Independently of the Decision Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02821)
*Peiran Shi,Jian Xiang,Xiang Zhang,Chenglong Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将CPS异常检测器视为两阶段流水线（残余生成+报警决策），并引入归一化残差能量作为与K-L散度直接相关的评价指标，无需依赖特定报警规则即可分离评估检测器的表征质量。在三个基准数据集上对五种检测器进行无调参评估，发现检测器在ROC-AUC相近时实际性能差异巨大，且排名随测试平台变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测器评估混淆了"表征物理过程的能力"与"报警阈值设置"两个独立因素，导致在单一操作点上的精度/召回率/F1分数无法反映检测器真实性能差异，也无法诊断失败根源。

Method: 将CPS异常检测器分解为两阶段流水线：阶段一将观测映射为残差，阶段二将残差映射为报警。直接基于归一化残差能量评估阶段一，该指标与训练正常参考分布的K-L散度有精确联系，能独立于报警规则测量攻击分离度、训练测试差距稳定性及编码紧凑性。

Result: 在SWaT、WADI和HAI三个基准上评估五种检测器（GDN、FuSAGNet、TranAD、NSIBF、GeCo）发现：尽管ROC-AUC在SWaT上相近，但相同误报率下性能差异超过一个数量级；检测器排名在测试平台间变化显著（TranAD在HAI第一但在SWaT最后，NSIBF在WADI第一但在HAI最后）；WADI上局部攻击可绕过跨通道聚合证据的检测器，解释了NSIBF的优越性。

Conclusion: 检测失败源于不同因素：表征能力弱、阈值校准差或攻击物理效应小。基于决策规则无关的分析方法能够分离这些成因，为检测器诊断提供更清晰的视角。

Abstract: Anomaly detectors are often the last line of defense for cyber-physical systems (CPS). But detectors built in very different ways, from deep neural networks to invariant templates, are usually compared using precision, recall, or F1 at a single operating point. These scores mix two separate things: how well the detector represents the physical process, and how well its alarm threshold is set. We therefore treat a CPS anomaly detector as a two-stage pipeline: Stage 1 maps observations to residuals, and Stage 2 maps residuals to alarms. Instead of scoring only the final alarms, we evaluate Stage 1 directly using normalized residual energy, which has an exact connection to the Kullback-Leibler divergence from the trained-normal reference distribution. Because it does not depend on a specific alarm rule, it can separately measure attack separation, stability across the train-test gap, and the compactness with which a detector encodes the plant.
  Without any per-detector tuning, we apply this evaluation to five detectors -- GDN, FuSAGNet, TranAD, NSIBF, and GeCo -- across three CPS benchmarks: SWaT, WADI, and HAI. Although the detectors have similar ROC-AUC values on SWaT, their performance differs by more than an order of magnitude at a common false-alarm rate. Rankings also change across testbeds: TranAD ranks first on HAI but last on SWaT, while NSIBF ranks first on WADI but last on HAI. On WADI, localized attacks can evade detectors that pool evidence across all channels, helping explain why NSIBF outperforms methods that do well on other benchmarks. These results show that detection failure can come from different sources: a weak representation, poor threshold calibration, or an attack with little physical effect. A decision-rule-free analysis helps separate these causes.

</details>


### [294] [Decidability of Parameterised Dolev-Yao Secrecy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02838)
*Ioana Boureanu,R. Ramanujam,Srinibas Swain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究Dolev-Yao模型中参数化保密性验证问题，在全局角色有限新鲜性和良类型替换限制下，参数化保密性可判定，并获得了截断定理和WSTS覆盖性归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Dolev-Yao保密性只关注协议是否泄露秘密，而不考虑系统规模参数化的问题。实际中攻击如何随参与者数量扩展非常重要，参数化视角弥补了这一空白，并为小规模实例分析的有效性提供了形式化基础。

Method: 识别两个结构性限制使参数化保密性可判定：(i)每个角色的全局有界新鲜性，(ii)限制为良类型替换的Dolev-Yao入侵者。在这些假设下，协议执行可在折叠映射下有限表示，系统转化为基于边界排序的良结构迁移系统（WSTS）。

Result: 获得截断定理：任意多会话系统中的保密性违规总能在有界大小的系统中被见证。同时，参数化保密性可归约为WSTS中的覆盖性问题，证明了该设置下参数化保密性可判定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了参数化保密性验证与参数化验证技术（WSTS、覆盖性问题）之间的联系，为符号协议分析中有限见证的存在性提供了结构化的理论解释。

Abstract: We study the verification of parameterised secrecy for cryptographic protocols in the Dolev-Yao model, where the number of protocol sessions is unbounded and treated as a parameter. This differs fundamentally from classical Dolev-Yao secrecy, which asks whether a protocol leaks a secret irrespective of the number of executions; our question is whether secrecy holds uniformly across all system sizes, where such a size is a parameter. This parameterised perspective captures how attacks scale with the number of participants and provides a formal basis for the empirical effectiveness of small-instance analysis.
  Secrecy (parameterised or not) is undecidable in general, even under bounded freshness or bounded message size. We identify two structural restrictions that make parameterised secrecy decidable: (i) global bounded freshness per role, and (ii) a Dolev-Yao intruder restricted to well-typed substitutions. Under these assumptions, protocol executions admit a finite representation up to a collapsing map on agents and terms.
  Our main result is that parameterised secrecy is decidable in this setting. We obtain a cut-off theorem: secrecy violations in systems with arbitrarily many sessions are always witnessed in systems of bounded size. The cut-off is self-contained; more strongly, the induced transition system forms a well-structured transition system (WSTS) under a bound-based ordering, so secrecy also reduces to a coverability problem in WSTS. This provides a structural explanation for the existence of finite witnesses in symbolic protocol analysis and connects Dolev-Yao verification with parameterised verification techniques.

</details>


### [295] [MutMem: Cryptographically Authorized Mutation in Persistent Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02843)
*Walid Saidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MutMem是一个在HOM-AIMOS持久代理记忆引擎中实现的授权突变协议，通过签名证据和可验证的权重变更事务来区分合法记忆适应与数据库篡改。在LongMemEval上达到91.8%准确率，在PoisonedRAG攻击下实现0/100注入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久代理记忆需要根据后续结果调整先前证据，但可变的检索权重产生了归因问题：审查者必须区分授权适应与数据库篡改。这是记忆系统安全性和可信度的核心矛盾。

Method: MutMem保留记忆内容，记录签名的正负结果证据（无年龄过期），每次非平凡权重变更作为管家授权的状态转换提交。每个转换绑定终端溯源节点、签名者epoch、量化新旧权重、无分叉前驱和两个域分隔的SHA-256承诺。Ed25519验证在数据库写入器和便携验证器中均运行。疑似有毒内容以签名可修订标签保留，作为召回时的信任证据。

Result: HOM-AIMOS在LongMemEval上91.8%正确率；LoCoMo上74.12%判定准确率和58.20 token F1。原生测试套件全部通过，中位签名转换延迟4.865ms。PoisonedRAG适应中注入毒物0/100出现在前5披露中，目标答案攻击成功率1/98（1.02%）。四臂消融实验证实检索减少归因于签名存储标签。

Conclusion: MutMem提供了完整性、授权性、可追溯性和历史连续性的证据，但不确定内容真实性。该协议为代理记忆系统中的归因问题提供了可操作的解决方案。

Abstract: Persistent agent memory must adapt as later outcomes change earlier evidence, yet mutable retrieval weights create an attribution problem: reviewers must distinguish authorized adaptation from database tampering. We present MutMem, an authorized-mutation protocol in HOM-AIMOS, a persistent agent-memory engine. MutMem retains memory content, records signed positive and negative outcome evidence without age-based expiry, and commits each nontrivial weight change as a housekeeper-authorized transition. Each transition binds a terminal provenance node, signer epoch, quantized old and new weights, a no-fork predecessor, and two domain-separated SHA-256 commitments. Ed25519 verification runs in both the database writer and a portable verifier. Content classified as poison-likely is retained with signed, revisable labels used by recall as trust evidence. We evaluate utility, mutation integrity, and poisoning adaptation. HOM-AIMOS answers 459/500 LongMemEval questions correctly under LLM judgment (91.8%). On LoCoMo, it obtains 74.12% judged accuracy and, under a separate upstream-compatible protocol, 58.20 token F1. A native suite passes all declared authorization, topology, tamper, signer-epoch, and post-mutation-recall cases; median signed-transition latency is 4.865 ms. In a declared N=100 PoisonedRAG adaptation, no injected poison appears in attacked top-5 disclosures (0/100; 95% Wilson upper bound 3.70%), while induced target-answer attack success among 98 clean-negative targets is 1/98 (1.02%). A preregistered four-arm ablation attributes the retrieval reduction to signed stored labels: the retriever selects poison for 94/100 targets when epistemic policy is bypassed and 0/100 when labels are restored. MutMem provides evidence of integrity, authorization, traceability, and historical continuity; it does not establish content truth.

</details>


### [296] [SparSEEty: Extracting Tokens from Sparsity-Exploiting LLM Serving Systems via Deterministic Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02995)
*Yongwan Jo,Jinyoung Park,Euihyun Lee,Dokyung Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SparSEEty，一种利用稀疏性优化LLM服务系统输入相关权重访问引发的侧信道，从受TDX保护的机密虚拟机中提取输入令牌的新攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统利用激活稀疏性优化性能时会省略非活跃神经元的权重访问，但这类优化导致输入相关的权重访问模式，可通过侧信道泄露，威胁用户隐私。

Method: SparSEEty分三步：（1）利用CVM暴露的侧信道组合构建神经元激活预言机；（2）降低推理期间神经元激活监控的开销以保持隐蔽性；（3）将部分二值激活轨迹准确反演为原始输入令牌，形成端到端令牌提取攻击。

Result: 在Intel TDX机密虚拟机保护的LLM服务系统上，SparSEEty能在多种模型和数据集上以BLEU>0.95的高一致性重建提示词和响应令牌，监控开销仅为3.7%至7.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了稀疏性优化LLM服务系统存在严重的安全漏洞，即使在可信执行环境保护下，攻击者仍可通过侧信道提取用户与模型交互的令牌内容。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) exhibit activation sparsity, wherein only a subset of their neurons is activated for given input tokens. Researchers have leveraged this property to optimize LLM serving systems by omitting weight accesses and computations pertaining to inactive neurons. Unfortunately, however, such optimizations create input-dependent weight accesses, which can be leaked over side channels.
  We present SparSEEty, a new token extraction attack that exploits input-dependent neuron weight accesses introduced by sparsity-exploiting LLM serving systems. SparSEEty first constructs a neuron-activation oracle using neuron weight access side channels during LLM inference, and then inverts the activation traces to reconstruct the input tokens, forming an end-to-end token extraction attack. We instantiate SparSEEty against an LLM serving system protected inside an Intel TDX confidential virtual machine (CVM), addressing three key challenges: (i) constructing a neuron-activation oracle using a combination of side channels exposed by CVMs, (ii) reducing inference-time overheads of neuron activation monitoring for covertness, and (iii) accurately inverting partial binary activation traces back to tokens. Our evaluation shows that SparSEEty can reconstruct both prompt and response tokens with consistently high BLEU scores (>0.95) across various models and datasets, while incurring monitoring overheads of 3.7% to 7.2%.

</details>


### [297] [Tiny Enough to Break In: Agentic Remote Access Trojans Powered by Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03009)
*Yuhan You,Suhas Adavelly,Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新型网络安全威胁——智能体远程访问木马（Agentic RAT），其在本地部署小型语言模型（SLM）实现自主的"观察-决策-行动"循环。实验表明该架构在当前硬件上可实现完整的自主攻击闭环，但成功率仅10.9%，尚不具备高可靠性。随着SLM能力提升，这类本地自主攻击将更具实用性和隐蔽性，对现有防御机制构成严峻挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI带来了新的安全威胁：能够在本地自主推理、行动和适应而无需持续人工指挥的网络攻击。作者希望验证在无云服务和操作员参与的情况下，一个本地部署的小型语言模型是否能够支撑自主的网络攻击决策。

Method: 作者构建了受控的网络隔离实验室环境，包含Kali Linux、Metasploitable2靶机、LM Studio和本地80亿参数的Dolphin系列模型。通过智能体RAT架构，测试SLM能否解释主机和网络观测、选择行动、从失败步骤中恢复，并减少对外部操作员的依赖。

Result: 实验证明该架构在当前商用硬件上具有技术可行性：SLM完成了完整的"观察-决策-行动"闭环，解读侦察信息、选择攻击行动，并在真实脆弱服务上获得了经核实的root shell访问权限。但模型存在幻觉命令、误读输出和恢复失败不一致等问题，在刻意严格的检查清单上仅完成10.9%。

Conclusion: 尽管当前小型模型的能力限制了操作可靠性，但这反映了现有模型的局限而非概念的瓶颈。随着SLM改进，智能体端点系统将更实用、更自主、更难检测，对现有监控、遏制和策略执行机制构成压力。2025-2026年的真实事件已表明AI驱动的入侵正从概念走向实践，本地自包含的智能体RAT是近期的切实威胁方向。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence raises a new security concern: cyber threats that reason, act, and adapt locally without continuous human direction. We examine this threat through an Agentic Remote Access Trojan (agentic RAT): a Remote Access Trojan augmented with a locally deployed Small Language Model (SLM). The SLM interprets host and network observations, selects actions, recovers from failed steps, and reduces reliance on an external operator. We implement the concept in a controlled, network-isolated lab built from Kali Linux, a Metasploitable2 target, LM Studio, and a local 8-billion-parameter Dolphin-family model. We then test whether a model this small can support autonomous cyber decision-making. This is architecturally feasible today. On commodity hardware, with no cloud service and no operator in the loop, the SLM closed the full observe-decide-act cycle: it interpreted ranked reconnaissance evidence supplied by the controller, selected actions, and obtained verified root-shell access on real vulnerable services. However, it is not yet operationally reliable. The same model hallucinated commands, misread output, and recovered from failure inconsistently, completing 10.9% of a deliberately strict checklist. That gap reflects the limits of today's small models, not a ceiling on the concept. As SLMs improve, agentic endpoint systems may become more practical, more autonomous, and harder to detect, straining existing monitoring, containment, and policy-enforcement mechanisms. Real-world incidents in 2025-2026 already show AI-driven intrusions moving from concept toward practice. That makes the local, self-contained variant we study a plausible near-term direction, not a hypothetical one.

</details>


### [298] [AI Security Leaderboard: Methodology, Results and Minimal Standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03070)
*Jasper Timm,Lukas Struppek,Ziwei Xu,Grace Cheong,Oscar Mata,Dan Zhao,Mick Yang,Isadora De Andrade,Xiaojun Jia,Yiming Li,Samuel Bauer,Heather McIntyre,Adam Gleave,Edward Yee,Kellin Pelrine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FAR.AI最小安全标准1.0版，提出了一套包含67种静态越狱技术的分类体系，并对四款前沿AI模型（Claude Fable 5、GPT-5.6 Sol、Gemini 3.1 Pro和Grok 4.5）进行了大规模越狱攻击基准测试。结果显示模型安全性极不均匀：Grok 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro分别可被63个和18个通用越狱轻易绕过，而Claude Fable 5和GPT-5.6 Sol则未被攻破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI模型开发者依赖多层次安全防护来防止灾难性滥用，但缺乏公开证据表明这些防护的实际效果以及不同开发者之间的差异，因此需要建立可量化的安全评估标准。

Method: 提出FAR.AI最小安全标准1.0：包含67种静态越狱技术的分类体系，组合成大规模攻击空间；建立包含360个攻击目标、覆盖CBRNE威胁和网络攻击的两类数据集；采用三级漏斗流程识别通用越狱；引入成本-越狱指标（cost-to-jailbreak）来衡量攻击者直接支出，并对未发现通用越狱的模型采用右删失下限估计。

Result: 模型防御能力差异超过百倍：随机搜索发现Grok 4.5有63个通用越狱（平均成本约58美元），Gemini 3.1 Pro有18个（平均约278美元）；专家引导组合进一步发现385个和231个。Claude Fable 5和GPT-5.6 Sol在两种策略下均未产生任何通用越狱。满足该标准所需的防御措施已在生产环境中公开部署，说明差距可以用现有技术弥合。

Conclusion: 建议采用推理监控、激活监控和输入/输出监控相结合的多层防御体系（defense-in-depth），并强调上述安全差距是可以通过当前已知技术解决的。相关结果可在leaderboard.far.ai查看。

Abstract: Frontier AI model developers increasingly rely on layered safeguards to prevent catastrophic misuse, but little public evidence exists on how much protection these safeguards provide, or how consistently across developers. We introduce the FAR.AI Minimal Standard for Safeguards, Version 1.0: a taxonomy of 67 readily accessible static jailbreak techniques, a method for composing them into a very large attack space, and a benchmark of flagship models against a sample of it. We evaluate Claude Fable 5, GPT-5.6 Sol, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok 4.5 on two complementary datasets totalling 360 attacker goals spanning chemical, biological, radiological/nuclear and explosive (CBRNE) threats and offensive cyber, using a three-stage funnel to identify universal jailbreaks: single prompt templates that elicit operationally compliant responses on over 75% of a domain's goals. We also introduce a cost-to-jailbreak metric that models attacker spend directly, with right-censored lower bounds where no universal jailbreak was found.
  Robustness is highly uneven: the cost to break these models varies over a hundredfold. Random search over our technique pool found 63 universal jailbreaks against Grok 4.5 and 18 against Gemini 3.1 Pro, at an average cost of roughly $58 and $278 per jailbreak found; expert-guided composition raised these to 385 and 231. Neither Claude Fable 5 nor GPT-5.6 Sol yielded any universal jailbreak under either strategy. Because meeting the Minimal Standard requires only defenses already publicly described and deployed in production elsewhere, these gaps appear closable with current techniques. We recommend defense-in-depth combining reasoning, activation, and input/output monitoring. Results are maintained at leaderboard.far.ai.

</details>


### [299] [DHMark: Public-Key Watermarking for LLM-Generated Text via Diffie-Hellman-Guided Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03093)
*Haocheng Fu,Yuqi Qian,Luyao Wang,Yun Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DHMark是一种面向LLM生成文本的公钥水印框架，通过分离载荷授权与噪声文本证据，将Diffie-Hellman引导的令牌标签与注册表支持的统计证据聚合相结合，避免了传统水印方案中对嵌入式加密字符串的精确恢复需求，在多种编辑攻击下保持了高有效率和极低的误接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计水印大多依赖私有检测密钥，集中化验证并复杂化公共审计；而近期公开可验证方案依赖嵌入加密字符串的精确恢复，在令牌编辑、截断、复制粘贴和低熵生成场景下较为脆弱。因此需要一种既支持公共验证又对编辑攻击鲁棒的水印机制。

Method: DHMark将载荷授权与噪声文本证据分离：发行方签署绑定到公共上下文的短注册载荷，并将载荷扩展为多个一位方程；生成时通过Diffie-Hellman引导的令牌标签接口赋予每个候选令牌一个公共方程投票，采样器根据投票与授权载荷的一致性进行软性或选择性提升；验证时第三方仅需公共信息提取令牌投票，聚合为方程级证据，只对有签名的注册记录进行评分。

Result: 在默认32位配置下，DHMark在八种编辑条件下保持至少0.967的有效率，同时在三种负面对照上达到0.000的接受率，验证了截断、替换、复制粘贴、错误上下文和普通生成攻击下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DHMark通过将水印检测转化为注册表辅助的统计证据聚合而非精确签名恢复，实现了公共验证性与编辑鲁棒性之间的有效平衡，为LLM文本溯源提供了一种更实用的水印方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) watermarking provides an important mechanism for tracing the provenance of generated text. Existing statistical watermarks are often effective and robust, but most of them rely on private detection keys, which centralizes verification and complicates public auditing. Recent public or publicly verifiable watermarking schemes improve key management, yet many of them rely on exact recovery of embedded cryptographic strings, making them fragile under token edits, truncation, copy-paste, and low-entropy generation. This paper introduces DHMark, a public-key watermarking framework for LLM-generated text. The key idea is to separate payload authorization from noisy textual evidence. An issuer signs a short registry payload bound to a public context, and the payload is expanded into many one-bit equations. During generation, a Diffie-Hellman-guided token-labeling interface assigns each candidate token a public equation vote, and the sampler softly or selectively promotes candidates whose votes agree with the authorized payload. During verification, third-party verifiers use public information to extract token votes, aggregate them into equation-level evidence, and score only signed registry records. This design avoids exact recovery of a long embedded signature and instead treats watermark detection as registry-aided statistical evidence aggregation. We formalize the public-verification setting, analyze label pseudorandomness, registry-backed soundness, and sampling distortion, and evaluate a prototype under truncation, substitution, copy-paste, wrong-context, and plain-generation attacks. In the default 32-bit configuration, DHMark maintains at least a 0.967 valid rate across eight edit conditions while yielding a 0.000 acceptance rate on three negative controls.

</details>


### [300] [FakeI2V-Bench: Benchmarking the Applicability of Image-level Deepfake Detectors for Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03096)
*Pei Li,Sihan Chen,Delong Ran,Tianshuo Cong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeI2V-Bench，一个包含97,548个视频的深度伪造视频检测基准，系统评估了8个视频级和12个图像级检测器，并提出了IV-Bridge框架，通过随机森林聚合帧级预测，使图像级检测器在视频域超越最先进的视频级方法（最佳AUC达93.80%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型的快速发展加剧了深度伪造威胁，但现有深度伪造视频检测基准不够完善，特别是图像级检测器在视频域中的有效性尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建包含97,548个视频的FakeI2V-Bench基准数据集，涵盖最新生成模型内容及多样化类别；提出IV-Bridge通用框架，利用随机森林模型和统计特征聚合帧级预测，将图像级检测器扩展到视频域。

Result: 最佳图像级检测器AUC为80.16%，略优于最强的视频级检测器（79.99%）；通过IV-Bridge框架，11个图像级检测器超越SOTA视频级方法，最佳变体达到93.80% AUC。

Conclusion: FakeI2V-Bench为深度伪造视频检测提供了严谨的基准，并提出了将图像级检测器扩展到视频域的新路径，为未来研究提供了新见解和方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly intensified the deepfake threat, yet the current deepfake video detection benchmarks remain underdeveloped. In particular, the effectiveness of image-level detectors in the video domain has not been systematically assessed. To fill this gap, we present FakeI2V-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating state-of-the-art video-level deepfake detectors in challenging scenarios, with a particular focus on systematically assessing the performance of image-level deepfake detectors in the video domain. FakeI2V-Bench comprises 97,548 videos, containing content generated by the latest powerful generation models and covering a broader range of categories. Using this dataset, we conduct a systematic evaluation of eight video-level detectors and twelve representative image-level detectors. Experimental results show that the best-performing image-level detector achieves an 80.16% AUC, slightly outperforming the strongest video-level detector (i.e., 79.99% AUC). Going beyond benchmarking, we present IV-Bridge, a general framework that enhances the applicability of image-level deepfake detectors to videos. IV-Bridge employs a random forest model with statistical features to aggregate frame-level predictions, allowing eleven image-level detectors to surpass state-of-the-art video-level approaches, with the best-performing variant achieving a 93.80% AUC. Overall, FakeI2V-Bench establishes a rigorous benchmark for deepfake video detection and introduces a novel pathway for extending image-level detectors to the video domain, offering new insights and directions for future research. Code and data are available at https://github.com/CryptoAILab/FakeI2V-Bench.

</details>


### [301] [PECR: A Reproducible Specification and Synthetic Stress Test of Telemetry-Informed Vulnerability Prioritization for SD-WAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03110)
*Saeed Alam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PECR（预测性暴露与密码就绪）决策支持方法，结合九种归一化严重性、威胁、可达性和后果因子，分别报告置信度与评分区间。基于100条合成漏洞资产记录及30000次Dirichlet权重扰动测试，该方法在队列排序上显著优于CVSS-only、EPSS-only等基线（Kendall τ最高0.924），但未使用真实结果标签验证预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件定义广域网（SD-WAN）的漏洞修复队列仅依据严重性排序，未能反映当前暴露程度与证据质量，且未将密码学依赖迁移纳入考虑，导致运营决策缺乏量化依据。

Method: PECR方法综合九种归一化因子（严重性、威胁、可达性、后果等），分别输出置信度和评分区间；密码学依赖独立排队。评估采用100条角色和区域条件化合成漏洞资产记录（62个角色一致资产），30次独立生成器重复，并通过30000次联合Dirichlet权重抽样进行稳健性检验。

Result: PECR排序与运营顺序的Kendall τ达0.755，显著高于CVSS-only(0.196)、EPSS-only(0.221)、KEV-first(0.301)和CVSS×EPSS(0.234)。在宽/中/窄扰动下中位τ分别为0.836/0.893/0.924。掩码10%因子单元后47条记录因区间跨越决策边界被标记为证据受限。

Conclusion: 研究确立了可执行规格、合成队列区分能力和压力测试稳健性，但由于未使用真实结果标签，未能证明预测准确性、避免损失的实际效果或生产环境有效性，需后续真实数据验证。

Abstract: Software-defined wide-area networking concentrates operational authority in controllers, orchestrators, and Internet-facing edges, but severity-only remediation queues do not represent current exposure or evidence quality. This paper specifies Predictive Exposure and Cryptographic Readiness (PECR), a decision-support method that combines nine normalized severity, threat, reachability, and consequence factors while reporting confidence and score intervals separately. Cryptographic dependencies enter an independent migration queue. The evaluation uses 100 role- and zone-conditioned synthetic vulnerability-asset records over 62 role-consistent assets, plus 30 independent generator replications. Against the operational PECR order, CVSS-only, EPSS-only, KEV-first, and CVSS x EPSS queues yield Kendall $τ$ values of 0.196, 0.221, 0.301, and 0.234; a stronger five-factor context-lite comparator yields 0.755. Across 30,000 joint Dirichlet weight draws, median $τ$ is 0.836, 0.893, and 0.924 under broad, moderate, and narrow perturbations. Masking 10% of factor cells labels 47 records as evidence-limited because their intervals cross a decision boundary. The study establishes executable specification, synthetic queue differentiation, and stress-test robustness. It does not establish predictive accuracy, avoided loss, or production effectiveness because no real outcome labels are used.

</details>


### [302] [DP-MemView: A Memory Interface for Attribute-Level Transcript Privacy in Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03130)
*Jong Wook Kim,Byoungjae Min,Kennedy Edemacu,Yoonhyuk Choi,Sae-Hong Cho,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-MemView，一种差分隐私界面，用于防止LLM智能体的长期记忆在自适应交互过程中累积泄露受保护属性。该方法通过私有选择公开的记忆视图而非原始记忆来生成响应，并为每个受保护属性维护独立的预算账本，证明在整个自适应转录上满足纯B_a-DP，实验表明两种模式都能在保持个性化质量的同时将转录可区分性保持在接近随机的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖长期记忆实现持久个性化，但重复的记忆条件响应即使从不显式提及受保护属性，也可能在累积过程中泄露这些属性。现有的隐私保护方法难以处理这种自适应转录场景下的长期累积泄露威胁。

Method: 提出DP-MemView框架：1）差分私有地选择公开响应条件视图，仅将视图（而非原始记忆）暴露给响应LLM；2）每次私有选择都会对与读取集有交集的每个受保护属性的记忆组计费；3）每个属性维护独立账本，超出预算上限的选择被阻止并返回固定的通用视图；4）在显式接口契约下证明整个自适应转录满足纯B_a-DP，并扩展到多个受保护组差异的存储场景。

Result: 在可控相邻存储基准和公共语料迁移实验中使用三种响应LLM进行评估，在线和预分配两种模式均将转录可区分性保持在接近随机水平，同时保留目标所需的个性化和整体响应质量。诊断实验表明，移除关键防护会导致输出支持不匹配、账本计费缺失、侧信道泄露或长期泄露增长。

Conclusion: DP-MemView提供了一种可证明的自适应转录隐私保障机制，有效平衡了LLM智能体的持久个性化需求与受保护属性隐私安全之间的权衡，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables persistent personalization in LLM agents, but repeated memory-conditioned responses can cumulatively reveal protected attributes even when they are never stated explicitly. We formalize this threat as adaptive transcript privacy and introduce DP-MemView, a differentially private interface that privately selects public response-conditioning views and exposes those views---rather than raw memory---to the response LLM. Each private selection is charged to every protected attribute whose memory group intersects the read set. Per-attribute ledgers block any selection that would exceed its cap and return a fixed generic view instead. Under an explicit interface contract, we prove pure B_a-DP for the entire adaptive transcript. We also extend the result to stores that differ across multiple protected groups and bound how much observing the transcript can change an adversary's prior odds. We evaluate the online and preallocated modes with three response LLMs on a controlled adjacent-store benchmark and a public-corpus transfer track. Both modes keep transcript distinguishability near chance while preserving target-required personalization and overall response quality. Further diagnostics show that removing key safeguards causes mismatched output support, missing ledger charges, revealing side channels, or growing long-horizon leakage.

</details>


### [303] [CLEAR: Causal Context-Based Agentic Reasoning for Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03134)
*Sungju Yun,Sijune Hwang,Yeonjoon Lee,Kyungtae Kang,Sungbin Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR，一个基于因果知识图谱的多智能体漏洞检测框架，通过构建漏洞因果知识图谱（VCKG）建模漏洞实例间的因果链，并利用四个协作智能体（Collector、Claim、Critic、Judge）验证漏洞假设，在C/C++和Java漏洞基准上分别提升Pair-Correct性能130.7%和71.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代安全漏洞根植于执行流、控制条件和程序状态之间的复杂因果依赖关系，而现有基于LLM和多智能体的方法主要处理良性函数与漏洞函数之间的表面相似性，无法捕捉安全缺陷中固有的复杂因果依赖，导致漏洞检测效果受限。

Method: CLEAR框架包含两个核心部分：一是构建漏洞因果知识图谱（VCKG），系统建模漏洞实例中入口点、前置条件、根本原因和修复意图之间的因果链；二是四个专门化智能体（Collector、Claim、Critic、Judge）协同工作，通过检索到的因果上下文验证漏洞假设。

Result: 在C/C++和Java漏洞基准上的实验表明，CLEAR相比最先进方法在Pair-Correct（P-C）性能上分别提升了130.7%和71.56%，验证了因果知识图谱引导推理在自动化漏洞检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 因果知识图谱引导的多智能体推理能够有效提升漏洞检测的准确性，证明了将结构化因果知识与LLM推理能力相结合是自动化漏洞检测的有前景方向。

Abstract: Detecting source code vulnerabilities is increasingly difficult as modern security flaws are rooted in complex causal dependencies between execution flows, control conditions, and program states. Despite recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and multi-agent frameworks, existing approaches primarily address superficial similarities between benign and vulnerable functions while failing to capture the complex causal dependencies inherent in security flaws. To address these limitations, we propose Causal Context-based Agentic Reasoning (CLEAR), a novel multi-agent vulnerability detection framework integrated with a causal knowledge graph. CLEAR systematically constructs a Vulnerability Causal Knowledge Graph (VCKG) that models the causal chains between entrypoints, preconditions, root causes, and fix intents across vulnerability instances. Leveraging this structured knowledge, four specialized agents, including the Collector, Claim, Critic, and Judge, collaboratively verify vulnerability hypotheses through retrieved causal contexts. Experimental results on C/C++ and Java vulnerability benchmarks demonstrate that CLEAR improves Pair-Correct (P-C) performance by 130.7% and 71.56% over state-of-the-art approaches, demonstrating the effectiveness of causal knowledge graph-guided reasoning for automated vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [304] [AirKey: Multimodal Acoustic-Assisted WiFi Sensing for Zero-Training Robust PIN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03151)
*BaiChuan Wu,Bin Liu,Xiang Zhang,Zhi Liu,Jie Zhang,Chao Liu,Huan Yan,Meng Li,Fusang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AirKey提出了一种跨模态零训练PIN窃听框架，利用IEEE 802.11 ACK机制被动获取CSI流，并结合轻量声学信号作为时间锚点解决WiFi波形融合模糊问题，在真实场景中实现了4倍以上的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WiFi的无接触击键推断技术在现实部署中面临两大瓶颈：一是需要网络权限才能获取稳定的感知数据流，二是在快速肌肉记忆打字时纯WiFi信号存在固有的"波形融合"模糊性问题，导致实际可行性受限。

Method: AirKey包括两个核心机制：(1)利用IEEE 802.11协议基本机制，无需修改目标设备即可预测性地触发ACK响应，通过低成本微控制器被动采集CSI，实现无需网络关联的连续空间感知流；(2)引入跨模态互补机制，利用轻量声学信号作为精确的时间锚点，引导重叠CSI轨迹的稳健分割，将CSI空间相似性与声学引导的击键间时序进行联合时空融合。

Result: 大规模真实场景评估表明，AirKey相比最先进的单模态零训练方案实现了4倍以上的精度提升，并能在6次尝试内成功恢复设备解锁PIN码。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当代智能接口中的关键隐私漏洞，强调了无处不在的多模态感知技术带来的严重隐私隐患，对WiFi与声学等多模态感知的隐私安全研究具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Contactless keystroke inference via WiFi sensing highlights severe privacy threats, yet its real-world feasibility is hindered by two fundamental physical and deployment bottlenecks: the strict requirement for network privileges to acquire stable sensing streams, and the inherent "waveform fusion" ambiguity of pure WiFi signals during rapid, muscle-memory typing. To overcome these limitations, we propose AirKey, a novel cross-modal sensing framework that achieves highly stealthy, zero-training PIN eavesdropping. First, to bypass network deployment barriers, AirKey exploits fundamental IEEE 802.11 mechanisms to predictably elicit Acknowledgment (ACK) responses from unmodified target devices. By passively harvesting Channel State Information (CSI) from these ACKs using a low-cost microcontroller, AirKey secures a continuous spatial sensing stream entirely without network association. Crucially, to resolve the WiFi waveform fusion bottleneck, AirKey introduces a cross-modal complementarity mechanism. By utilizing lightweight acoustic signals as precise temporal anchors, the system robustly guides the segmentation of overlapping CSI trajectories. This joint spatiotemporal fusion strictly intersects CSI-derived spatial similarities with acoustic-guided inter-keystroke timing. Extensive real-world evaluations demonstrate that AirKey achieves over 4x higher accuracy than state-of-the-art unimodal zero-training schemes, successfully recovering device-unlock PINs within 6 attempts. Ultimately, this work exposes a critical vulnerability in contemporary smart interfaces, underscoring the severe privacy implications of ubiquitous multimodal sensing.

</details>


### [305] [SpreadMark: Robust Image Watermarking via Spread-Spectrum Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03165)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Zhenchang Xing,Liming Zhu,Jin Song Dong,Yulei Sui,Jingling Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpreadMark，一种基于扩频嵌入原理的神经后处理图像水印框架，将每个消息比特以稠密伪随机码字的形式铺展到整张图像上，并通过匹配滤波与并行卷积解码路径恢复水印，在深度伪造检测与溯源场景中实现了对再生攻击和潜在空间稀疏化攻击的高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经编码器-解码器水印方案中，每个消息比特仅占据图像的一小部分区域，攻击者只需干扰该区域即可破坏水印，导致水印脆弱易被去除。作者希望通过重新引入经典的扩频嵌入原理来解决这一共性问题。

Method: SpreadMark将每个消息比特编码为覆盖整张图像的稠密伪随机码字，采用匹配滤波从学习到的覆盖抑制芯片表示中恢复水印，同时整合并行卷积解码路径和稀疏化感知训练。此外，通过条件芯片空间分析证明，在码字无关的扰动模型下，稠密扩频能增加破坏匹配滤波恢复所需的攻击预算。

Result: 在COCO和DIV2K数据集上与九种方案对比，SpreadMark是唯一在再生攻击和潜在空间稀疏化攻击两种测试设置下均保持高检测率的方案，同时在JPEG压缩和加性噪声鲁棒性上具有竞争力，且保持了良好的不可感知性和高感知质量。

Conclusion: 稠密扩频嵌入能够显著提升水印对蓄意移除攻击的鲁棒性，SpreadMark在多种攻击场景下表现出色，为不可见图像水印在深度伪造检测与溯源追踪中的应用提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Invisible image watermarks are increasingly used for deepfake detection and provenance tracking, where they must survive not only incidental distortions but also deliberate removal. We revisit spread-spectrum embedding, a classical watermarking principle, inside a modern neural post-hoc watermarking architecture. Our starting point is a measurement: in existing encoder-decoder schemes each message bit occupies only a small fraction of the image, a shared contributing factor to their fragility, since removal then need only disturb the region a bit occupies. SpreadMark instead spreads each bit as a dense pseudo-random codeword over the whole image and recovers it by matched-filtering a learned cover-suppressed chip representation, with a parallel convolutional decoding path and sparsification-aware training. A conditional chip-space analysis shows that, under a codeword-independent perturbation model, dense spreading increases the budget required to disrupt matched-filter recovery. Evaluated on COCO and DIV2K against nine schemes, SpreadMark is the only evaluated method retaining high detection under both the regeneration and the latent-space sparsification settings we test, with competitive JPEG and additive-noise robustness. It keeps the embedded watermark imperceptible, maintaining high perceptual quality on both COCO and DIV2K.

</details>


### [306] [Test-time reasoning effort and unauthorized tool use in language-model agents: a prespecified equivalence study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03169)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.CR
